In the teacher's student view the bottom bar also shows the teacher's 🔔 (el-cloud.js `bar()`). **Catalan first** (Roger, 25 Nov 2026): vocabulary cards carry `en` / `ca` (vCard; `ca` empty on Pro); el-flashdeck.js `dir` (`auto` default · `en` · `ca`, localStorage `el_fd_dir`, ⇄ button `data-fd="dir"` in the study top bar): auto = two or more words → Catalan front, English + example on the back. **Bottom bar for students and the student view** (el-cloud.js `bar()`): icon buttons `.elcb-i` with `data-tip` (CSS tooltip on hover/focus, `tipCss()`), the name under the side-menu brand via `window.elWhoPlace()` (`.el-who`; el-app.js calls it after drawing its rail), in the bar only when no brand is visible. **Teacher (25 Nov 2026):** teacher.html has an `<a class="nv">🧠 Flashcards</a>` after 🏠 Home (and a More › Materials row on a phone) to `lab/practice.html#deck`; practice.html `fdCloudOn()` now also saves the teacher's own progress to `students/{teacherUid}/flashcards/all` (owner write, already allowed by firestore.rules), never in a student view. The 🔀 every-LS card is `mixCard()` (`.elfd-mixc`) in el-flashdeck.js.
- **🧠 Flashcards place on the dashboard (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "I want to see who uses them"):** `teacher.html` place `fd` (tile on each group page, `fdtBox`): reads `students/{uid}/flashcards/all` once per student per session when the place opens (`FDT`, `fdtLoad`, 🔄 Refresh = `fdtre`), `fdtStats` counts only the course's LS decks from the day logs (`[reviews, right, seconds, new, review-card reviews, right]`, days start at 4:00) and card states (learnt = review state, long-term = interval ≥ 21 days). Demo data: `D.flash` in `tools/site-video/teacher-demo.mjs`. Each name links to `lab/practice.html?student=<uid>#deck`: `el-cloud.js` now starts the 👁 student view from `?student=` on Practise too (as on `today.html`), and `practice.html` then reads that student's cloud copy once (`fdAs`, `fdView`) and keeps anything studied in memory only.
- **🧑‍🎨 Streak mascot (Roger, 24–25 Nov 2026; rule in docs/REQUIREMENTS.md › The student's pages):** `shared/js/el-mascot.js` (`ELMascot`: `img(mood,px)`, `badge(n,"sm"|"xl")`, `data(uid,P)` → `{P, st, left, today}`, `mood(x,{sad})`, `milestone(st)`, `say(anchor,html,{host,href,ms})`, `seen` = guarded localStorage). Pictures `shared/img/streak/{happy,energised,worried,sad,sleeping,party}.webp` (320 px, from Roger's PNGs, cropped to the figure). Streak = `ELFlashDeck.streak(P,now)` in `el-flashdeck.js` → `{n, today, lost, gap, used}`: days (4:00 day start) with `log[day][0]>0` in any deck; Sat/Sun and `EL_TIMETABLE.off` (el-plan.js) are skipped when empty; used by 📊 stats, the board, Practise and `teacher.html` `fdtStats`. Practise writes `el_flash_sum_<uid>` = `{d, due, nw, un}` (`fdSum`, on render and every save) so the board knows the cards waiting; on another day `data()` adds the saved reviews due by tonight + `un`. Board: `today.html` `mzHome(lss)` (called from the home render next to `#lvl`, `mzOff()` elsewhere; an `<a id="elmz">` inserted in `.phead` before `#lvl`, href `lab/practice.html#deck`), `mzLine` picks the day's line (milestone / lost / cards / first `shadow*` or `book` tab of `todoItems` / late `tabsMiss(lateList)` / done), shown once a day (`el_mz_say_<uid>`; `el_mz_party_<uid>` = `n@day`, `el_mz_sad_<uid>` = the gap day), never when `vyClass()` or in the 👁 student view (which reads `students/{uid}/flashcards/all` once), and only once `#el-rem` / the race card is gone. `el-todo.js` remind header and `vyRace0` show `ELMascot.img` when the page has it. Phone placement in `el-phone.css` (`.phead #elmz` absolute top right, the tile's `.fdmz`).
- **No content of its own:** the page reads the LS pages at run time: LS2-style `data/<page>.js` (`vocab` groups and `core` lists, `mc`/`quiz` panels with `media` audio / videos / youtube and their `o` options, `shadow` clips, `speaking`/`talk`/`writing` models and vocabulary examples split into sentences for the builder; Pro panels from `data/native/` for an LS with `pro:true`) and LS1 pages (`vocabMap`, `ca`, `examples`, `levelInfo[].builders`, `listening` and the page's first `<audio>`). Route rules as in the engine (cumulative `r:`, `only`, Starter → Base and Pro → Plus fallbacks). A new LS built the same way appears on its own; keep those names and shapes. Only LSs the group can see and Learning Paths the teacher opened (`elLpOpen`; a closed LS counts as open). Everything corrects itself, nothing is handed in or synced (progress in `el_practice`, this browser only).

## Levels, listenings, videos and readings
- Each route has the level agreed in the levels document of the teaching programme:

  | Course | Year horizon | Beginners | Insecure | Safe | Daring |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | 1ESO | A1 consolidated; starting A2 tasks | Pre-A1 / A1 with a lot of support | A1 with a lot of support | A1 consolidated | A1+ / early A2 |
  | 2ESO | Functional A2 | A1 with a lot of support | A1+ / early A2 with scaffolding | Functional A2 | A2+ / emerging B1 |
  | 3ESO | More autonomous A2; towards B1 | A1 with a lot of support | A2 with support | More autonomous A2 | Emerging B1 |
  | 4ESO | B1 as the final horizon | A1 with a lot of support | A2 / A2+ with support | Indicative B1 | B1 / B1+ |

  Content marked as an extension of the next level (for example B1+ in 4ESO) goes only to Daring. This school year the site covers **2ESO and 4ESO** only.
- **Grammar and lexicon maps (Roger, 31 Oct 2026):** the vertical maps in Drive `01_General/05_Grammar and lexicon by level` (`Gramatica_A1-B1_1r-4t_ESO.docx`, `Lexic_A1-B1_1r-4t_ESO.docx`) are the reference for what each course and LS teaches; the programme tables copy their 2n and 4t columns. Before building an LP, check its grammar and productive vocabulary against the programme section and the map: nothing from another course except as a labelled chunk or preview, and no B1 item produced freely by a route below B1 (Insecure gets it in frames). When an LS adds or moves something, update the programme table (with a dated note) and make a new version of the map in Drive in the same task (old one to the folder's `Archive`).
- **Listenings and videos are pitched at the Safe level**, and every route hears the same input. Do not write separate scripts per route.
- **The activities around that input adapt to each route's level:** questions, tasks, support, hints and the language of instructions. Insecure gets easier questions, support in Catalan and, where possible, a slower version of the audio; Beginners gets the slow copy with subtitles from the first viewing and multiple choice or matching only. Daring gets the more demanding questions (inference, comparison, opinion).
- **Reading texts are different: they vary by route.** Each route reads a version written at its own level (for 4ESO: A2 / A2+ for Insecure, B1 for Safe, B1 / B1+ for Daring), on the same topic and with the same key information, so the whole class can share the content. Adapt length, sentence complexity, vocabulary and grammar to the level; the questions follow the route's text.
- **Per-route readings in code:** in pages built with `ls2-engine.js`, `media.text` is a string or `{beginners, insecure, safe, daring}` (`beginners` optional: without it Beginners reads the Insecure text); standalone pages keep one text per route in a `readingData` / `readingChat` object keyed by level. Questions stay cumulative (`r:`), so every question a route sees must be answerable from that route's text.
- **Scripted videos:** one MP4 at Safe level plus a slower copy for Insecure, stored next to the page in `video/`, with WebVTT subtitles (`.vtt`) that students switch on after a first viewing. The slow copy is made locally with ffmpeg (`atempo=0.85`, longer pauses), never with a second paid generation. Scripts are recorded in the LS docs before production.
- **Shadowing video per LS (Roger, 30–31 Oct 2026; 4 ESO and 2 ESO LS2):** one ~6-minute video per LS at Core level, the same for every route, and **always in this format** (Roger: "always like this"). **Every sentence is marked** (Roger, 31 Oct 2026; `marks` in the spec): stressed syllables underlined `**x**`, some linked words `a‿b` (not every link, one or two per sentence), and ↗ ↘ for the melody (Roger, 31 Oct 2026): ↗ before every comma, ↘ at the end of a statement, ↗ at the end of a yes/no question, ↘ at the end of a wh- question; check against the recording's pitch (praat-parselmouth) and prefer sentences whose recording follows this (avoid ones that clearly fall at a comma or rise at a full stop). A spoken intro (what shadowing is, the 4 steps, tips, with a legend of the marks on the tips slide) and 4 worked examples (stress, linking `==a‿b==`, a question with no question word going up ↗, a who/what/where question going down ↘; Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "explain this in the introduction"), played twice, then "Now you try" (the third and fourth have no narrator line, so the intro audio stays as generated); then about 15 sentences, each: listen and read (subtitled), a chime and "Your turn" pause with a timer bar and the sentence still shown in the same format, listen again with no text, a second pause (pause = 1.25 × speech + 1.5 s, at least 3 s). Sentences are cut from the LS's own recordings, unchanged (no generated audio): `python3 tools/shadowing-video/align-words.py <ls folder> words.json <data files>` gives word times (pocketsphinx), and the cuts are snapped to the quietest 30 ms nearby. Spec per LS in `tools/shadowing-video/<ls>.json`, built with `node tools/shadowing-video/build.js <spec>` into `<ls>/video/shadowing.mp4` plus a poster. The intro voice is Amelia (Emma's voice, ElevenLabs eleven_v3, script in project files `shadowing-video/intro-voice-script.md`): clips `l1`–`l8` in `tools/shadowing-video/voice/intro/`, shared by every LS and course (generated 31 Oct 2026 on Roger's PC with his key in the Windows user variable `ELEVENLABS_API_KEY`, ~330 credits; the cloud container cannot reach ElevenLabs). **Where it goes (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: keep both):** the Learning Path Shadowing steps stay as they are (short, each path's own listening), and the video is the unit's revision: the first tab of the sample exam (an `info` panel, not a step: nothing saved or counted), the student board next to the summary PDF, the dashboard's Materials tab (`EL_LS[].shadowing`) and a **Shadowing Video** button on the LS home page (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: "the shadowing video of the unit should be here too"; next to Sample Exam, Vocabulary Games and Reflect, it opens the video in a window on the same page: `#shadowOpen` + `<dialog id="shadowDlg">` in `<ls>/index.html`). A new LS adds its video and those four links (never LS1). The mp4s go to the LS's Drive `Videos` folder like the other videos. **⏭ Skip the intro (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "I want to have a mark where the introduction finishes so that I can skip it"):** `shadowingIntro` on the `EL_LS` entry = the second the "Ready? Let's start!" slide begins (`build.js` prints it); every player of the video on the site (LS home window, sample exam, dashboard and class tab via `elPlayVideo`, student board) then gets a bar under the video lined up with its time bar (Introduction | ⏭ Sentences from m:ss, yellow mark, tap to jump) and ⏭ Skip the intro over the video during the intro (el-catalog.js `elSkipIntro`, finds the videos by src). Its ⛶ full screen fills the screen with the video's box so both stay. The mp4 opened on its own (or from Drive) has no mark.
- **"This LS in 4 minutes" video per LS (Roger, 7–8 Nov 2026; LS2 both courses):** a short English video with the LS goals, the final product (steps and how it is marked), the grammar and the vocabulary, one slide per voice line (Amelia, ElevenLabs, only with Roger's OK). Script = `tools/ls-video/<ls>.json`, voice clips in `tools/ls-video/voice/<ls>/`, build `node tools/ls-video/build.js tools/ls-video/<ls>.json` (fast takes slowed to ~160 wpm) → `<ls>/video/about-this-ls.mp4` + poster, shown in the `.tile.vid` card under "About this LS" on the LS home page (bump `?v=` on rebuild); copy to the LS's Drive `Videos` folder. Make one for every new LS (never LS1).
- **"How English Lab works" videos (Roger, 31 Oct 2026):** three narrated tours made from real site screenshots, no subtitles: students in English (Amelia) `video/how-english-lab-works.mp4`, students in Catalan (Julian) `video/how-english-lab-works-ca.mp4`, teachers in American English (Michael C. Vincent) `video/how-the-dashboard-works.mp4`. The student ones are on the student board (a box under the points and a 🎬 link in the menu, `HOWTO` in `today.html`); all three are in the dashboard's Materials tab (`HOW_VIDEOS` in `teacher.html`, and the 🎬 How it works button). Source in `tools/site-video/` (`build.py`, `script*.json`, `shots*.json`, `voice*/`, `captures/`); the teacher screens come from `teacher-demo.mjs` (demo data with invented students, never real names). **Since 2 Nov 2026 the student video holds the whole board, part by part** (Roger: "this videos could be merged … the original video with the changes"; the separate "Your student board" video is gone): `full.json` lists the lines, the first video's (`script.json`, real screenshots) around the board video's (`board/script.json`, the demo board of `board/demo.mjs`: Alex Sample, 4 ESO Core in English; Pau Exemple, 2 ESO Base in Catalan). Build: `node tools/site-video/board/capture.mjs en|ca`, `python3 tools/site-video/board/compose.py` (lays the bottom bar, My week panel and notice cards of the old screenshots on the new board), `python3 tools/site-video/build.py video/how-english-lab-works.mp4 --set full [--lang ca]`; bump `HOWTO_V` and the `?v=` in `HOW_VIDEOS`. A shot's `on` is a phrase of the spoken line (when the picture changes), not a caption. When the site changes a lot, recapture and rebuild; new voice lines need Roger's permission. The mp4s also go to Drive Teacher Studio > 03_Site assets > Videos. **Since 7 Nov 2026 students no longer see these videos** (Roger: "obsolete"): Help has a picture guide instead (below).
- **Help picture guide (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "instead of the videos (which are obsolete) I need a guide with images so that students can check it if they want it"):** `helpGuide()` in `today.html` (`#help`): one folded part per place (My dashboard, the menu, an LS page, a learning path, study cards, Games and theory, My marks, Practise, Games), each with real screenshots and 2–5 short steps in the student's language (Catalan for Starter/Base). Pictures `shared/img/help/<id>-<ca|en>.webp` at Chromebook size from `node tools/help-guide/capture.mjs en|ca [ids]` (offline, invented students of `tools/site-video/board/demo.mjs`, never locked items or the class full screen); `help,help-open,help-phone` write previews of the guide itself. Retake them when student pages change a lot and bump `HELP_V`. The phone install steps stay on `#phone`.
- **⛵ Voyage to the challenge (Roger, 19-20 Nov 2026; mockups in the project's road-preview folder):** in the courses listed in `VOYAGE` in `today.html` (now `["4eso"]`), from LS2 on, the board home is a sea map instead of the garden (`voyageHome`, drawing in `shared/js/el-voyage.js`). A row of chips on top (⏰ late, 📣 news, ✅ To do, 📝 Homework, 📓 My notebook), then the map with 🧭 Your steps on the right (hidden under 900 px) and ▶ Continue under it, then the current LS as before. Every open tab (`lsBeds`, the same list as the garden; never locked or "later" tabs) is a stop labelled skill + title (titles from `shared/js/el-step-titles.js`, written by `tools/gen-sections.js`); each learning path reaches the next port of the English voyages in real order (England Plymouth 1620, USA Plymouth MA 1620, India Madras 1639, Jamaica Port Royal 1655, Canada Halifax 1749, Sierra Leone Freetown 1787) with a flag and a 📜 history fact; checkpoints and other paths are stops of the port before them. The final product is always the destination, Australia, Sydney Cove 1788. Each LS has its own world in `SKINS` (keyed by LS id, merged over `SEA`, the 4 ESO sea voyage): places and destination with their lines, pictures folder, background tile, stop pictures, vehicle (+ the band tinted per student), fog, decorations, route colours, sounds and wording (EN/CA), so a new world (2 ESO LS2: a reporters' bus touring sports events, ending at a vlog studio) needs only data and pictures; the field list is at `SEA` in el-voyage.js. **2 ESO LS2** (`VOYAGE` = `["4eso","2eso"]`, 20 Nov 2026) is the reporters' bus tour: pictures in `shared/img/road-2eso/` (Gemini, cut out of white), places England Wimbledon, Scotland Highland Games, USA baseball, Canada ice hockey, Jamaica Champs, Australia Bondi, ending at the vlog studio; the teacher's Class steps (`VOYF` in el-cm-agenda.js) and Class voyage mini map (`VY_ART` in teacher.html) follow the course. `fest` adds the 🎉 celebration lines; art from Gemini cut out of its background, 320 px WebP. Boats: every student of the group by steps done (s done = beside the s-th stop, the last one done, never at the next one still to do: Roger, 23 Nov 2026, "boats should always be by the last activity they did"; `spot` in `el-voyage.js`; none done = by the first stop, all done = at the destination), classmates with first names only, never marks (Roger: "All, with first names"), from `road/{ls}/groups/{group}` = {uid: {n, s}}: one read per board load, and the student writes only their own line when the count changes (rules: own uid key, n ≤ 40 chars, s int; testers and the teacher's student view never write). Sounds in `shared/audio/voyage/`: background music "Gathering by the Hearth" on the map only, never a loop under it (the sea loop was taken out on 22 Nov 2026 and `snd.amb` with it: on an iPhone it played as loud as the song, since iOS ignores `audio.volume`; Roger: "Leave only the music") (🎵 on/off in `localStorage` `el_voyage_snd`, starts at the first tap; `el-voyage.js` must be loaded once per page: it returns at once if `window.elVoyage` exists, because a second copy had its own tap listener and started a second song over the first on teacher.html, 21 Nov 2026). **No vehicle on a picture** (21 Nov 2026): `wire` keeps each port picture (`.vy-pimg`) as a box (`boxes`), and `fit` limits a stop's row room to the picture's edge; where not even `NEED` px fit, that picture's size (`it.psz`) shrinks (min `PMIN`). `layAll` lays out again until rows and sizes stop changing (at most 6 passes). After `map.innerHTML`, every `.vy-deco` (with the sweep of its swim/fly animation, its height from the loaded image or 1.75× its width) is checked against stops, labels, vehicle rows, You, pictures, port cards and the destination; it moves in 40 px steps up or down to the nearest free place, else it is removed. ⛵ Class voyage page (`vyBox` in teacher.html): `.vyhd` (title, Order by, counts, ⛶ Full screen) is sticky; `vfs()` = full screen like `gfs()` (`body.vfs`, the browser's full screen when allowed, Esc), kept through renders by `S.vfs`. `vfs(true,true)` = ▦ Table full screen (`S.tfs`, `body.tfs` hides `.vymap`/`.vysea` and the intro line). `hfs()` = the class map alone full screen (⛶ Full screen on the group page `vyHome`, ⛶ Map full screen in the `.vyhd` bar): `body.hfs` fixes the `.vyrank` box to the window, hides its `>p` and table `.wrap`, flexes `.vymap .vy-row` to fill the height and re-wires the map at 60 and 400 ms; `S.hfs`, Esc and fullscreenchange as `vfs()`. Then reward sounds compared with `localStorage` `el_voyage_<uid>_<ls>` (step chime, port fanfare + "Port reached" card, fireworks overlay when the final product is done, fog sound when new stops appear; the first visit only remembers). No boat sail sound (Roger disliked it). Every sound is off while the teacher's class mode is on (`elCmOn(LIVE.go)`) and in the teacher's view of a student. Offline pictures: `node tools/voyage/preview.mjs en <dir>`. **On a 4 ESO learning path (PR 2, 20 Nov 2026):** for a student the left column is 🧭 Your steps of the whole voyage with ⛵ Back to the map (`voyDraw` in `el-app.js`, from the board's copy `localStorage el_voyage_steps` written by `voyageKeep`; this page's ticks from its own tabs; without a copy, or in class mode, the path's own tabs as before). Students open in the notebook look and have the 📓 Notebook / 🖥 Normal page switch again (Roger, 25 Nov 2026, replacing the notebook-only look of 19 Nov: `nbOn` in `el-app.js` reads `localStorage el_lpnb`, default notebook, per device). Their choice is kept (`window.elNbOnly` = a student on these pages): ↺ Practise again in the notebook look starts a fresh notebook page (`state.nbAgain[id]` in both courses' `ls2-engine.js`: the latest try shows from then on) and coming back from a game keeps the look; only the teacher's page goes back to the laptop look then. 📓 bottom left (el-cloud.js nbFab, now always labelled) and 🔍 bottom right as before; no music on activity pages. **Teacher (PR 3):** on the group page of a `VOY_COURSES` course, the button ⛵ Class voyage: who needs a push opens the place `voyage` (`vyRank` in `teacher.html`): students ordered by steps done of the open steps (`vySteps`: open learning paths and sections, not the final product or optional work), then the LS mark so far (results `progress.total`, else `grade`), chips Steps done / LS mark / Name, the first step still to do (+n), 🟢 On course (≤1 missing and mark ≥5) / 🟠 Drifting (2-4 missing or mark <5) / 🔴 Needs a push (5+ missing), a red dashed line where 🔴 starts, and a mini sea of boats by steps done. No new reads. In class mode the docked panel has two tabs, 📋 Today (default, unchanged) | 🧭 Class steps (`drawSteps` in `el-cm-agenda.js`, 4 ESO): every tab of the LS's open learning paths under the ports, ● now on the class tab's step; a click takes the class there like a Today step (`go`, opens it for the group first if locked). **Class map on the group page (21 Nov 2026):** `vyHome`/`vyMap` draw the students' map with every vehicle; the right column is 🧭 Class steps (`classCol` in `el-voyage.js`, when `V.pct` is set): the same places and stops as the students' 🧭 Your steps, each stop with the % of the group's students who did all its steps (`vyPct` in `teacher.html`, from the stop's tab-group keys `plant.ks` and `doneSet`, no reads), each place with the % who did all its stops, colours from `elPctCol`; a stop not done in class yet (`elDueBefore`, the To do test) shows its % grey with "📅 not done in class yet" and stays out of the average in the header. A tap on a step or a place opens ✅ Done / ⏳ Not yet with short names (`V.who(key)` from `vyPct`, one `onclick` on the column so a re-wire doesn't add a second handler). With no `V.me`, `wire` starts the view at the median vehicle (it used to be a scroll after wiring that a re-layout undid), and the kept scroll is per map (`V.view`, e.g. `grp:2ESOA:<ls>`). **⛵ Show the class map in class mode:** `vyMap` keeps a copy of the map's `V` (no `ks`, no `who`) in `localStorage el_vymap_<group>`; the class tab's button above the 📋 Today | 🧭 Class steps tabs, or the end of the warm-up games (`o.onRace` in `el-warmup.js`, `mapOpen({fin:true})`) (`mapOpen` in `el-cm-agenda.js`) draws it full screen (`#el-cmmap`) and writes `live/{group}.go.look = {s, at}`, ✕ Close / Esc / pagehide write `look: null`. Students (`cmLook` in `el-cloud.js`, called from `goTo`): a 👀 Look at the screen cover (`#el-look`) while `go.look.s` is this class mode, at most 15 minutes; Stop clears it with the rest of `go`.
  - Class voyage full screens (teacher.html `hfs`, `vfs`): `vfs(true)` first leaves `hfs` (body.hfs hid the table) and takes over its browser full screen. Phone rules in `el-phone.css` (end of file): z-index above `.elptabs`, tab bar and `#fsctl` hidden, compact `.vyhd`. The map title `.vy-top` (el-voyage.js) is an opaque band, so port cards scroll under it, not through it (21 Nov 2026).
- **LS mark in points until every part is marked (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** `elLsPts(ls, {daily, exam, fp})` in `shared/js/el-points.js` returns each part (daily work = `elFormativeShare`, exam, final product; LS1 `only`: exam and final product) with its points of the LS's 10 (`mark × weight / 100`), `all` (every part marked), `got`, `of` (what the marked parts are worth: the big "2.9 / 4") and `left`; `elDailyOf(ls, progress)` = the daily work /10 from a stored `progress` (`gv`, `other`). `today.html`: `lsPts`, `ptOne`, `ptLine` (0 / 3.5 "to do", or 🔒 soon while the exam is held) in `marksHome` (hero: points so far + points to do, each part's points instead of its weight and mark), `progressBox` (heading, the three cards' "Points:", the total row), `progressPlace` (LS mark cell) and `zeroLine` (everyday work points now and how far they go up). `teacher.html`: `lsPtsOf(s, ls)` and `vyPts` in ⛵ Class voyage (column "LS mark so far (points)") and the 🌱 Progress card (group average points per marked part). Once `all` is true everything shows the LS mark as before. The stored `progress.total` and every mark calculation are unchanged: this is only how the mark is shown.
- **LS mark card on My dashboard + part names under the bar (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** `today.html` `mkSplit(ls, M)` draws the parts bar (each part `flex` = its weight, filled by its mark /10) with `PT_NM` names under each piece; used by `marksHome` (hero) and `markMini(ls)`, the small `a.hmk` card (title, `pt1(PP.got) / pt1(PP.of)` points, or `dgMark(total)` once `all`; links to `#marks`). Voyage homes: `voyageHome(ls, info, nx, top, side)` puts it right of the links row in `.hvtop` (350 px; at ≥1180 px it moves up 22 px into the space under 🎯 My level; below that it wraps to its own line). Other homes: under the level box in `#lvl`. No new reads.
- **⛵ Class voyage: only the steps due (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** `vySteps` in `teacher.html` counts `done`/`total`/`miss` only for the tab groups that `elDueBefore` (el-catalog.js) gives for the group: a past class day of the Time plan, ticked as done or set as homework (whole paths in `db.acts`). Tabs on the Time plan for coming classes are `later` ("+N coming" under Steps done); `all`/`allDone` count every stop of the map and place the boats on the class map. No agenda: every open tab counts, as before. The status (🟢 at most 1 missing, 🟠 2-4 or a mark under 5, 🔴 5 or more) uses only the steps due.
- **🌱 Progress on the teacher dashboard (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "skill mastery over time is super (formative assessment)"):** a tile on each group page opens `prPage` in `teacher.html`: four cards (LS mark so far, steps done, work marked, activities average), 📈 skill mastery over time (the whole school year week by week, the group or one student, every skill or one; the centrepiece), 🕸 the skills radar of the LS (activities = all tries as the LS page, exam = its parts, shown to the teacher even while held) and the average per assessment criterion. Charts are plain SVG in `shared/js/el-charts.js`; the skill of a step is `elVoyage.skill` (as `el-ls-home.js`). No new Firestore reads, no leaderboard. Preview with invented students: `EL_DEMO_PROGRESS=1 node tools/site-video/teacher-demo.mjs --serve`. Students see their own line in 🎓 My progress › 📈 How my skills grow (`skGrowBox` in `today.html`, same `ELCharts.work`): only their own work, no classmates, no average, plus each LS's exam (★, joined LS to LS; one skill: that exam part) and final product (■) as big points (`skMilestones`, exam only once released); plus 🕸 My skills (`skRadarBox`): activities and exam per skill for one LS, the exam only after its results are out. 📊 My marks has the same per-skill bars for the chosen LS (`mkSkills`: activities and exam, exam only once released). **The whole term on the line (Roger, 25 Nov 2026):** `ELCharts.work(pg, rs, games)` takes the class games as a third argument (the board: `Object.values(D.mg)`, myGames; the dashboard: `prGames(u)` from `GAMES`, gameResults, already read): each play of kind `vocab` / `grammar` is a dated Vocabulary / Grammar event (`r / of`, on its `day`), only in `ev` (the line), never in `act` (the LS page's % per skill and the radar stay the learning path steps). Mixed games (kind `mix`) have no right count per part and are left out. A teacher level (`results.levels[pid]`) is dated by the hand-in (`steps[pid].date`, else `doneAt[pid]`), and only without one by `marked[pid]`, so back-filled levels go to the week the work was done. An LS with no Time plan (LS1) puts its exam ★ and final product ■ on `ELCharts.lsEnd(lsId)`: the Friday before the course's next LS starts on its Time plan (LS1: Fri 23 Oct 2026). The student's ■ final product also gives its 🗣 Speaking part (LS1 the whole final product, from LS2 the rubric rows 3.x from `progress.parts.fp`), as the teacher's.
- **🗣 Speaking analysis (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "a collection of words well and badly pronounced and how they structure the oral text at lexical and grammatical level"; "use the final product too"):** teacher only. School script v34 action `speak_an` (teacher sign-in; `fp: true` + `fileIds` for a final product video via the Files API, LOW media resolution) makes Roig Flash write `{summary, fluency, good, sounds, voc, gram, str, pron, task}` with levels 1-4. `teacher.html` `spkPage` (🗣 Speaking tile), `spkBox` under the 🔊 player in `wrOpen`, and `prStu` adds the average level as Speaking evidence in 🌱 Progress. Stored in `settings/spk_{ls}_{group}` = `{uid: {Drive file id: {a, k, at, an, model, eur, done}}}` (settings is teacher only). 🔍 Analyse shows the count and cost. **Daily (Roger, 24 Nov 2026):** the weekday marking run calls `node tools/speaking/analyse.mjs` (dry run lists Speaking/Talk/Surprise recordings, never Shadowing `x.sh`, with no entry in `settings/spk_{ls}_{group}`, and the cost) then `--apply` (POSTs `speak_an` with `SCHOOL_MAIL_KEY`, which school script v38 accepts besides the teacher sign-in; saves with `by: "run"`; `SPK_MAX` caps a run, default 120). v38 adds `an.n = {words, clear, sentences, correct, conn, secs}`; `spkMeasure`/`spkTrend` draw **📈 Over time** (`ELCharts.line`, weekly averages, levels as % of 4) on the 🗣 Speaking tile; older analyses without `n` show only the level lines. Students never see any of it. Demo: `EL_DEMO_SPEAK=1 node tools/site-video/teacher-demo.mjs --serve` (it includes two later recordings per student for the trend).
- **🌱 My garden (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "Gardens to have all the tasks done (not always homework)"):** on the board home, under Continue and the tiles and before the current LS (`gardenHtml` in `today.html`, drawing in `shared/js/el-garden.js`, original SVG art). Every tab of the current LS (from LS2 on) is a plant: one bed per open learning path (sign LP1, LP2… from the LS page), a tree per recall checkpoint, pumpkins for the final product, golden flowers for the optional sample exam. Flower by kind of tab (vocabulary daisy, grammar tulip, reading sunflower, listening bluebell, speaking rose, writing lavender, recall mushroom). States: bloom = tab done, bud = started, sprout = not started, thirsty 💧 = on ✅ To do (same tabs as the To do count; never for optional work). A finished bed gets a butterfly that flies around the garden and lands on flowers now and then (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "can you make the butterflies fly?"; three flight paths `gdfa/gdfb/gdfc`, out of step), everything done a rainbow, and birds fly across as soon as everything the student can do now is done (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "if they do everything they see birds"; "make sure birds show up when everything is done"): the optional sample exam and tabs kept for a later class (🏫 read-only, `later` from `secOff`) never hold them back, and the headline then says "You've done everything for now!". The birds whistle when they fly in (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "when birds appear I want them to whistle"): `ELSound.soon("birds")` in `el-sound.js` (Web Audio, no file, muted with the student's sound setting), once per page, at the first tap or key if the browser has not allowed sound yet; clicking a bird plays `chirp`. **Rotten garden (Roger, 18 Nov 2026: "if there's nothing done, I want that everything is like rotted and full of flies and a help sign"; "everything to look rotten even worms"):** when the student has no flower and no bud yet while something is already due (thirsty), the whole garden rots: grey cloud instead of the sun, dry brown ground and dark soil, every plant rotten (stink lines, mould), dead trees, flies buzzing, worms crawling, no butterflies or birds, and a swinging HELP! sign that opens the first activity to do. Wilting (Roger, 18 Nov 2026): under 20% of what was done in class finished (flowers / (flowers + thirsty), the optional sample exam not counted) and not rotten = yellowish lawn, dry-looking plants, 4 flies instead of the free butterflies, headline "Your garden is wilting 🍂". One finished activity ends it. Three butterflies always fly over every garden ("there should be always butterflies") and a bed gets one more for every 5 flowers (up to 2). Each plant links to its tab. Only open paths and visible tabs (`visSteps`), never locked ones. No Firestore reads or writes: flowers already seen are kept per computer in `localStorage` `el_garden_<uid>_<ls>` so new ones grow in with "+N new flowers" and a soft chime (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "When I open the garden again and there's a new flower I should hear a nice sound"): `ELSound.soon("bloom")`, checked at every drawing (a garden drawn again or brought back by Back notices new flowers too); the seen list only grows and a computer's first visit only learns the garden silently, so old flowers never ring; if the browser hasn't allowed sound yet (iPhone after a page load) it rings at the first tap, and the new flowers (`gd-nw`) wiggle and shine (`gd-ring`) as it rings. A new tab id: add it to `KINDS` in `el-garden.js` (else it grows a marigold).
- Mix real, level-appropriate listenings with generated ones. Never call a paid generation service (for example ElevenLabs) or spend its credits without the user's explicit permission for that specific generation. An approved script is not permission to generate.
- **Generated audio files:** MP3s live in the LS folder's `audio/` (for example `4eso/seasons-and-celebrations/audio/culture-bites-origins.mp3`) and are referenced from the LS data as `media.audio`. The slow version for Insecure sits next to it with a `-slow` suffix (`culture-bites-origins-slow.mp3`). Both files are also copied to that LS's `Listenings` folder in Roger's Drive in the same task (see the Drive rules at the top).
- **ElevenLabs settings:** for new audio use the expressive model `eleven_v3` with audio tags in the script (`[excited]`, `[surprised]`, `[laughs]`…), shown to the user before generating; recordings made before 24 Oct 2026 used `eleven_multilingual_v2` without tags. One call per script line (one voice per character), lines joined with 0.45 s pauses and loudness-normalised (`loudnorm=I=-16:TP=-1.5`, mono, 96 kbps). Keep the same voice for a character across an LS; each LS's `AGENTS.md` lists its voices.
- **Recorded lines instead of the browser voice (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "all the audios with ElevenLabs"):** `ls2-engine.js` plays `audio/tts/<key>.mp3` (🐢 = `-slow.mp3`, atempo 0.75) for Listen and complete, Talk questions and micro-dictation when the LS's `data/tts.js` lists the key (FNV-1a of the text as the page passes it, `tools/tts/tts-key.js`); without a recording it still uses the browser voice. `tools/tts`: `build-jobs.js` lists the lines (eleven_v3, LS2 voices rotated per sentence, one Talk partner per path, `[excited]`/`[curious]` tags), `generate.ps1` runs on Roger's PC (key in his user variable), `process.py` covers the answer word with the beep (pocketsphinx alignment; the sentence is generated with the answer) and slows takes faster than 175 words a minute (atempo ≥ 0.72), `cut-chunks.py` cuts dictation bits from the LS's own recordings (no credits). **When a line changes or a new LP is added, rerun build-jobs and generate only the new keys (with permission); started paths are not regenerated.**
- **Flashcard voice = Kokoro, only for flashcards (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "Heart, American English, all the flashcards of everywhere", "when we turn a flashcard we hear the audio", "we leave Kokoro only for flashcards"; listenings, Talk and dictation stay ElevenLabs):** Kokoro (free, open source, Apache 2.0, no account or credits), voice `af_heart`, `en-us`, speed 0.9. `shared/js/el-voice.js` (loaded by `learning-path-support.js`, and by tag in `lab/practice.html` and `flashcards/`) adds 🔊 Listen / 🔊 Example under every `#flashcard` and plays word + example when the card is turned; it plays `audio/voice/<key>.mp3` (FNV-1a of the shown text, `ELVoice.norm`) when `audio/voice/index.js` lists it, otherwise the browser's en-US voice. The 🧠 Flashcards are covered too (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "use kokoro for the flashcards, heart"): `harvest.js` opens `lab/practice.html#deck` as the demo teacher (`EL_DEMO_STUDENT.harvest` exposes `EL_PRACTICE_HARVEST`) and lists every word, example and grammar / 💬 Expressions sentence of every course and route; recordings already made (Kokoro or ElevenLabs) are never redone. **A new LS or changed vocabulary:** `node tools/voice/harvest.js` then `venv/bin/python tools/voice/generate.py kokoro-v1.0.onnx voices-v1.0.bin` (setup in the script; model files from the kokoro-onnx GitHub release, HuggingFace is blocked in the cloud), commit `audio/voice/`.
- **Readings read aloud = Kokoro Heart (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "is it possible to make kokoro read the readings? Heart voice?"; "And can you add it to students' readings too? Not in the exams"):** this widens the flashcards-only line above to the reading texts. Every `.reading` box of a learning path (`media.text` in the LS2 engines' data files and `read.text` of the study guides, string or one text per route, `data/`, `data/native/` and `data/study/`: Towards Nativeness, 2 ESO Free Time Live, 4 ESO Seasons & Celebrations) gets 🔊 Listen to the text (▶ Go on / ⏸ Pause), 🐢 Slower (playback rate 0.8) and the time, and the paragraph being read is lit up (Catalan labels for Starter). `shared/js/el-read-aloud.js`, loaded by `learning-path-support.js`; it plays `audio/read/<key>.mp3` when `audio/read/index.js` (`EL_READ`: key → second where each block starts) has the key; key = FNV-1a of the text's letters and digits only. No recording = no button. Shadowing scripts and gap-fill texts are not read (their own audio / not a reading). **Never on exams, sample exams or recall checkpoints**: the script and its loader stop on any path with `exam` or `checkpoint`, and `readings.js` skips those data files. The "Paraules:" / "Words:" help line is not read. LS1 pages are unchanged (finished). Towards Nativeness is recorded at speed 1.0, the LS readings at 0.9. **A new or changed reading:** `node tools/voice/readings.js` then `venv/bin/python tools/voice/readings.py kokoro-v1.0.onnx voices-v1.0.bin`, commit `audio/read/` (old recordings stay for pages still using them).
- **Aa Easy reading (Roger, 25 Nov 2026, dyslexia-friendly reading; rule in REQUIREMENTS.md §3):** `shared/js/el-easy-read.js`, loaded by `learning-path-support.js` (every LP page, sample exams and recall checkpoints included) and by `el-web-exam.js` (only when `EL_EXAM` exists). It finds every `.reading` and `.wx-text` box (class `el-er-box`) and puts **Aa Easy reading** in the box's `.el-ra-bar` beside 🔊 Listen to the text (or its own `.el-er-bar` when the text has no recording; the button moves into the audio bar once that appears). One setting for the whole site in `localStorage.el_easy_read` ({on, big, plain, cream, ruler, letters}; big + plain on by default, the rest off) shown as classes on `<html>` (`el-er-on`, `el-er-big`, `el-er-plain`, `el-er-cream`, `el-er-ruler`, `el-er-letters`), so a text drawn again keeps the look. big = `max(17px,min(21px,4.6vw))`, line-height 1.8, letter-spacing .09em, word-spacing .22em, less side padding; plain = Lexend (Google Fonts, loaded only when on) → Verdana/Arial; its selectors outrank the notebook look's Kalam (`html.el-lpnb … .reading`). Letters: each b d p q of the text is wrapped in `span.el-er-l.el-er-<letter>` (never inside buttons, inputs, textareas or the bars) and unwrapped when turned off; other colours under `html.tn-dark`; cream is skipped at night in Towards Nativeness. Ruler: two `.el-er-dim` shades above and below the line under the pointer or the tap. `el-read-aloud.js` ignores `.el-er-bar` and `.el-er-dim`, so the recording keys never change. LS1 (`my-real-routine`) pages: not loaded. Off = the page is exactly as before. **Its controls always work** (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "this dosen't work": on a step already done the boxes were greyed out): `el-cloud.js` disables inputs on read-only pages (`freeze()`: a step done, the 👁 student view, a closed LS) and in `el-sec-off` / `el-sec-cls` panels, so `guard()` re-enables the Aa button, the checkboxes and the panel buttons (a MutationObserver on `disabled`, `style`, `tabindex`, `data-elro`, `data-elcls`) and CSS keeps `pointer-events:auto` on them and on a reading box while the ruler is on. Nothing else on a frozen page is given back.
- **🖍 Underline (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "I want students to be able to underline readings when doing activities"; rule in REQUIREMENTS.md §3):** `shared/js/el-underline.js`, loaded by `el-easy-read.js` (so wherever Aa Easy reading is: every `.reading` / `.wx-text` box, not LS1). Its button `.el-ul-btn` sits right after `.el-er-btn` in the same bar (moved along when Aa moves into the `.el-ra-bar`); `.el-ul-panel` holds pens `u` (red underline) `y` `g` `p` (highlighters), `x` rubber, ✓ Done and 🗑 Clear all (second tap within 3 s). The pen is kept in `localStorage.el_ul_pen`. No native selection (el-nocopy.js keeps `user-select:none`): while `.el-ul-on`, pointer events on the box hit-test the words' client rects (`words()`/`hit()`, Unicode word regex), drag = range, tap = one word, tap on a word already in that pen = rub out; `touch-action:pan-y`, so a vertical swipe scrolls (pointercancel undoes the preview). Marks are `[start, end, pen]` offsets in the box's text (text nodes outside bars, panels, buttons and inputs), kept per text hash (fnv) in one key per page: `ulMarks-<path>` (synced by el-cloud.js into `students/{uid}/progress/{ls}`, so the 👁 student view shows them; frozen pages keep them for the visit only), `tnUlMarks-<path>` on Towards Nativeness (tnKey), `el_ul_exam-<uid>-<path>` on the web exam (this browser only). Drawn as `mark.el-ul.el-ul-<pen>` wrappers around text-node pieces (whitespace-only nodes skipped), redrawn when the box is drawn again or its text changes (another hash = no marks); `tidy()` trims spaces and joins one pen's marks with only spaces between. Works with Easy reading's coloured letters (marks wrap the pieces inside `.el-er-l`) and read-aloud (text unchanged). Phone (`max-width:640px`): the panel is fixed at the bottom of the screen. `tn-dark`: dark text on the highlighters.
- **Choosing voices for new audio:** shortlist 3–4 ElevenLabs Voice Library voices per character, only from the most popular ones (added by at least 50,000 users; the Library has no votes or ratings) with the right accent and age, and send the user their free preview clips; generate only after the user picks. Every voice must be expressive: prefer voices described as expressive or emotive, and always generate with `eleven_v3` and audio tags. Library previews are the creator's own samples (older model, no tags), so tell the user they show the voice, not its v3 expressiveness; offer a one-line v3 test before a full run if the user wants to hear it. Existing recordings keep their voices.
- **Slow versions are always made locally, never with a second paid generation:** apply ffmpeg `atempo=0.85` to the same line clips and join them with 0.9 s pauses.

## The four communicative competences, worked equally (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: “VERY IMPORTANT”)
- **Rule for every LS:** the learning paths work the four communicative competences **equally**: CE2 Listening (comprendre textos orals i multimodals), CE3 Speaking (produir textos orals i participar en interaccions orals), CE4 Reading (comprendre textos escrits i multimodals) and CE5 Writing (produir textos escrits i multimodals). **The exams stay as they are** (“it’s the way we worked”); the sample exam mirrors the exam, so it does not change either.
- **How it is counted** (the dashboard’s whole-year view, “how much it is worked”): the steps of the LS activities per competence from `EL_STEP_CRITERIA` (`el-catalog.js`; a step with two criteria counts half each). **Use of English (vocabulary and grammar) counts as reading and writing alike** (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: students read and then write, complete or choose), so recall, cards, practice, grammar and mixed steps without their own criteria give 4.1 + 5.1. Aim for about 25 % each among CE2–CE5, every one inside 20–30 % (the same band as the minutes budget of the balanced learning path skeleton, which is written into this section as it is built). Check the shares before Roger approves the learning path plan (playbook step 2) and again when the site is built. This is how much each competence is **worked**; which competences are **assessed** each term is in *Assessment and grading* (CE2–CE5 every term).
- **How LS2 balances it** (both courses, 30 Oct 2026; before: 4 ESO listening 14 % · speaking 6 % · reading 43 % · writing 37 %; after: 21 · 20 · 31 · 27 %; 2 ESO 13 · 7 · 43 · 38 → 23 · 24 · 28 · 25 %; paths students had already started stay as they were, so only 4 ESO LP3–LP6 and 2 ESO LP2–LP6 change):
  - **Use of English heard or said** (`ls2-engine.js`, same amount of practice): the vocab panel’s `practiceMode: 'listen'` and a typed panel’s `mode: 'listen'` (*Listen and complete*: the browser’s en-GB voice says the sentence with a beep for each gap, ▶ and 🐢 slower, the written sentence opens only on demand; tagged 2.2 + 5.1); `mode: 'say'` (*Say it*: the student says the whole sentence and speech recognition fills the box when it hears an accepted answer, contractions expanded; the box can be typed in without speech recognition, after a mic error or two misses; tagged 3.2). Practice is heard and mixed practice is said. **Grammar is never heard or said** (Roger, 15 Nov 2026: "grammar no listening anywhere"): every Grammar step of both courses is read and typed, with no `mode` and no `hear` items. Recall warm-ups and checkpoints stay read and typed, because their first try counts. Study cards already ask students to say each word aloud before turning it, so they count 4.1 + 3.2 (`RS` in `el-catalog.js`). **More than the gap counts (problem report, 2ESOA, 13 Nov 2026):** a typed answer is also right when it is a run of words of the sentence with the gap filled that holds the whole answer (*I am watching* or the whole sentence for *I ___ (watch) a basketball match right now.*; `fits()` in `ls2-engine.js`, contractions either way); a wrong form still fails.
  - **Talk** (`type: 'talk'`, step id `talk`, after Speaking in 4 ESO LP3–LP6 and 2 ESO LP2–LP6): a self-study interaction. `partner: [{q, ca}]` (cumulative `r:` tags like items) are asked one by one by the browser voice while the student is recorded, with time to answer (`wait` per route, default 12/12/16/20 s); no transcript (16 Nov 2026, see below); handed in like a step (`state.steps.talk`, catalogue `steps` → the dashboard) once the recording lasts `min` words × 0.6 s, at least 15 s (`min` default 15/25/50/70). Safe and Daring hear each question before they can read it; Beginners and Insecure read it with the Catalan. Pro: no recording, a written plan per question. Tagged 3.2.
  - **Listen again** (step id `again`, a normal `mc` listening with the listening cycle and 2–3 `chunks`): a new task on audio or video the LS **already has** (another path’s recording, with a new focus tied to this path), so no new generation is needed. Tagged 2.2.
  - Paper worksheets print Talk as the questions with lines to answer, then the conversation aloud with a classmate.
- **Never change a learning path students have already started** (Roger, 30 Oct 2026), and respect the calendar (`el-plan.js`): a rebalance goes into later paths, inside their planned classes and due dates.
- **New LS:** design each learning path with listening, speaking (including interaction), reading and writing steps from the start, and report the four shares with the plan.
- **From LS3 on (Roger, 30 Oct 2026, "ok to all"; plan: https://claude.ai/artifact/JCL94dP8GBpZnJTjp4v14e):**
  - **Skills budget in minutes.** Each LS design (docs/<course>/<ls>/) has a table of its steps with estimated minutes per skill (a mixed step splits its minutes); 25 % each, never outside 20–30 %, balanced **over the whole LS** (a research path may lean one way if another makes up for it). Class work counts too (shadowing, games, reading or listening done together). The year view still counts steps until steps carry their minutes.
  - **Learning path skeleton (about 58 min):** shadowing 5 (L+S) · recall 5 (R+W) · vocabulary and cards answered aloud 8 (R+S) · listening with the cycle and micro-dictation 10 (L, a little W) · reading 8 (R) · grammar (read and typed) and practice, one exercise heard 8 (W+L) · speaking or Talk, revised 8 (S) · writing, revised 6 (W) = about 25 % each. Input before output on the same topic.
  - **Shadowing step** (`type: 'shadow'`, step id `shadow`, both `ls2-engine.js`, tagged 2.2 + 3.2; LS2: 2 ESO LP2–LP6 and 4 ESO LP3–LP6, since paths students had already started stay as they were): `clip = {title, src, slow, t, ts, lines, video?, poster?}` is a 25–45 s part of a recording the LS **already has**, played as it is between `t` (normal) or `ts` (the `-slow` copy) seconds; `lines` = its script (`[speaker, text]`). Never change or re-record the listening for it (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: “don’t change the listening we prepared”). Four rounds: listen and read, mumble, shadow with the script, shadow without it (the last go can be recorded; speech recognition shows the % of script words it caught, feedback only). Done after 3 plays + a self-rating (`state.shadow[shadow_route] = {plays, rate, pct, date}`): **counted by completion, never by accuracy**. Insecure and Beginners start on the slower copy. Times: video clips from the `.vtt` cues; mp3 clips by forced alignment of `media.script` against the mp3 (pocketsphinx 5 `set_align_text`, normal and slow; it bundles its English model, so it runs in the cloud session). Paper worksheets print the script with the three-round instructions. **Handed in and corrected by Roig (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "in the new shadowing activities I want students to turn them in and roig will correct them"):** unless the panel has `hand: false` (only `2eso/…/what-are-they-doing.js` and `4eso/…/where-traditions-come-from.js`, opened before that day), the record card is "Record yourself and hand it in" (with 🎙️ I can't record) and the last button is 📨 Hand it in to my teacher: after the 3 plays and the rating, the recording made with ● Record and play (`recSecs(id+'Rec')` ≥ 60 % of the normal clip, at least 8 s) goes through `handRec` with `shadowTask(c, mode, slow)` as the task (the model sentences, with marks when they fit in 1,500 characters, and what to judge), and `state.steps[id] = {sh: 1, audio, f: [Model sentences, Roig's transcript, Roig's notes], how}` completes the step. No new school script version: the existing `audio` action reads the task. `ELMark.fbItems` gives it mode `shadow` (pronunciation-only sheet; answer `well done ## tip ## tip`, saved with `sqText` like 🎲 Surprise questions). On the dashboard it opens like a Speaking (player, 🤖 Roig's notes with its €, transcript), shows in ✏️ Corrections with its cost, and is left out of 🗣 Speaking analysis (`spkRecs` skips `x.sh`). Every new LS: shadow panels hand in by default; Towards Nativeness has its own engine and is unchanged.
  - **Recorders and the microphone** (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “Check all the recording of all the LS and fix it”): every page with a recorder loads `learning-path-support.js`, which loads `shared/js/el-mic.js`. It wraps `getUserMedia`, `SpeechRecognition` and `AudioContext` for all of them (LS1 pages, Getting started, LS2 Speaking, Talk, Shadowing): a Microphone box right under the Record button with a live level bar (“I can hear you ✓”, and after Stop whether the recording has sound), the mic settings for Chromebook/Mac/Windows, and a list to choose the mic (saved in the browser), a warning when the mic gives no sound or is muted, a clear message when the browser, the Mac or another app blocks the mic, and when the automatic transcript fails (permission, network, no mic) it says why, tells the student to type it and stops retrying. New recorders need nothing extra; buttons are disabled at once on click, and an empty transcript after Stop says “type what you said”. Set `window.elMicCa` to the page’s Catalan test if it has no global `level`.
  - **Shadowing marks + two modes** (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "indications like going up/down, stressed words, linking" + "two options: first listen and repeat … and shadowing all as you listen"): every shadow clip also has `marks` (per script line, its sentences in the video's format: `**stressed**`, `a‿b`, ↗ before a comma or pause and at the end of yes/no questions, ↘ at the end of statements and wh- questions) and `cues` (`[from, to, slowFrom, slowTo]` per sentence). Write them in `tools/shadowing-marks/marks.json` and run `python3 tools/shadowing-marks/apply.py` (checks the marks give the script exactly, aligns each sentence in both copies with pocketsphinx, writes both into `data/<lp>.js`). `shared/js/el-shadow.js` (load it before `ls2-engine.js` on a page with a Shadowing step) shows the marks with a key (Catalan for Starter/Base) and a 🎵 show/hide button, and the two modes: 🔁 **Listen and repeat** (default: one sentence, then a "Your turn" pause of 1.3 × the sentence + 1.2 s, 2.5–12 s, the sentence lit, ↺ Again / Next ⏭; a run counts as one play) and 🗣 **Shadowing** (the four rounds, the sentence being heard lit). Used by the LP step, Class shadowing and Practise; marks and mode are remembered on the device. A new LS: add its clips to `marks.json`. Screenshots: `node tools/shadowing-marks/preview.mjs <dir>`.
  - **Class shadowing** (`shared/shadow/index.html?ls=<course>/<ls>`, linked from the dashboard's 📚 Materials): the projector page with the shadow panels of every learning path of the LS (from `shared/print/lp-list.js` and `data/<lp>.js`), normal or slower, the four rounds, the script hidden in round 4. A new LS needs nothing extra.
  - **Class session (3 English classes a week, about 55 min; two classes per learning path):** shadowing 5 (class A: a clip of the previous path, already understood; class B: this path's) · game-theory 10–15 (every third question said aloud to a partner before tapping) · together 10 (class A the path's listening on the projector, class B its reading + a 1-minute retell in pairs) · learning path on their route 20 (headphones) · final game, Blooket, 5–10. Extended reading at home.
  - **Reading story books (built for LS2, Roger, 31 Oct 2026):** "Maya Live!" (2 ESO) and "Lantern Night" (4 ESO), text by ChatGPT from Claude's Core drafts, every level checked word by word (≥99 % known words, no Past Perfect/unless/although). `tools/reading-story/build.py` makes one book-style A5 PDF per route from `<course>-<ls>.md` + cover image (cover, how to read it, contents, a chapter per learning path with a words box, the two questions without the key; Starter/Base with Catalan) into `<ls>/downloads/<ls>-story-<route>.pdf`. The LS home page (Overview tab) has `<article data-el-story hidden>`, which `el-cloud.js` (`storyCard`) fills from `story:{title,pdf}` in `EL_LS`: students get only their route's book (Pro reads Plus), the teacher and testers all four. All student PDF buttons (book, summary on the LS page and the board) open the PDF in a new tab, not as a download; students download it from the browser if they want (Roger, 1 Nov 2026). Answer keys and teacher notes stay in the Drive docs (Reading story folders).
  - **📖 Book learning path (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "a Book LP with a section per chapter, and we allocate time on the agenda … there should appear the chapters of the book"; students asked how to answer the PDF's questions):** each LS with a reading story has `book.html` (`2eso/ls2-book`, `4eso/ls2-book` in `EL_CATALOG`, right after Getting started so no recall checkpoint closes it), one step per chapter (`ch1` … `ch6`), all in one tab (see One book, one step below). **The chapter is the book itself** (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "I want students to see the pages of the book as a PDF … the feeling that they are reading a book, with the images"; "the feeling of turning pages"): `python3 tools/reading-story/build.py --web` prints the books of every route with the web wording (no printed "Think about it"; the site's LP numbers) into one WebP per page, `<ls>/book/<route>-NN.webp` + `book/pages.json`, and `shared/js/el-book.js` shows a chapter's pages (chapter 1 with the cover and "How to read this book") as an open book whose pages turn in 3D (▶, a tap, the arrow keys, or dragging the page with a finger or the mouse: the paper follows the hand and turns past a third of the way or with a flick, else falls back (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "I want them to pass pages by dragging them"); ⤢ full screen, a tap on the page opens it on a computer; 🔍 one page as wide as the screen; one page at a time on a phone). **The questions are the book's last page** (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "make them do the activity in full screen … they press the options there … they can only do it once and when they finish, they can go to the other chapter or leave the LP"): `bookPanel` in `ls2-engine.js` passes its form to `ELBook.mount` as `opts.end`; the student taps the options on the page, Check works only with both answered and counts once (no ↺, the options lock; right option green, a wrong pick red; the answers come back locked on a reload), then "Chapter n ▶" opens the next chapter (still full screen) or "Leave the Book" goes to the LS page. Full screen is the browser's on `<html>` (left only if the book entered it) plus a cover at z-index 9600. Then `tools/reading-story/lp.py` writes `<ls>/data/book.js`: the page pictures of the student's route (4 ESO Pro has its own C2 book: a `## Pro` section per chapter in the .md, `story.py levels_of`, also `ls2-story-native.pdf` with `story.pro` in `EL_LS`; elsewhere Pro reads Plus), a pill with the site's LP it goes with, and the two questions: the gist question is checked (right option first, the page shuffles), the "What happens next?" one has `guess: true` in `ls2-engine.js` (saved, never marked or scored; the next chapter opens with the guess and, after its check, says if it was right). The keys come from the Drive teacher copies (✓) and are in `lp.py` (`keys`); run it again after any change to the story. A chapter is a reading step (4.1) in "Skills work done on time"; the Book has no dates in `el-plan.js`: `agendaDue` makes each chapter due on the day Roger puts it on the group's Time plan agenda (`ELWeek.applyEdits`), so a chapter not on the agenda never counts as missing. **The book is always open to read** (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "they did the book as fast finishers so I want them to be able to see the book and turn the pages"): `laterTabs` (el-catalog.js) returns nothing for a Book path, so its tab is never hidden or read-only because the Time plan has no Book day or a later one; the teacher's section switches still apply, and a chapter is still due only on a Book day. The PDFs stay in 📖 Books. **One book, one step** (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "students will have different paces when reading the book so I need you to make only one SLP for the book so that people can continue where they left off"; "continue reading as many chapters as wanted"): `data/book.js` has `LP.book = {tab, title, intro}`, so `ls2-engine.js` draws ONE tab, `book` (`EL_SECTIONS`: "📖 The book"), with one book (`R.onebook`): it opens where the student stopped (`state.bookAt = {c, i}`, saved with their work), a row of chapter buttons shows the chapters they can open (done ones + the next), and "Chapter n ▶" after the questions remounts the book with the next chapter (`ELBook` `start`, `pre`, `onTurn`, `destroy`). The chapters stay the steps (`ch1` … `ch6`, `EL_ACTIONS`, grading): `elSecSteps(a, "book")` = all of them, and `elTabOf` (el-catalog.js) reads an older plan's `path#chN` as `path#book` (Time plan, class mode, boards). On time (Roger chose "One chapter per Book day"): the n-th day "The book" is on a group's agenda, chapter n is due (el-week.js `stepDue`); chapters past the last Book day have no deadline (teacher.html `dueStep` never falls back to the path's day). Dragging "The book" from the list onto another day adds it there and keeps its other days. Turning or dragging past an answered chapter's questions goes on into the next chapter, and back from a chapter's first page into the one before (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "they should be able to drag on to the next chapter"; `ELBook` `after`, `before`, `canNext`, `more`). The teacher's progress table keeps a column per chapter. ⚡ I've finished opens the `book` tab.
  - **📖 Books opens the website book (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "didn't we say that the book would open as a real book where they can turn pages and not as a pdf?"):** the board's 📖 Books place (and the 📚 card on the LS place on phones), `bookCard` in `today.html`, links **📖 Open your book** to the LS's `book.html#book` (the `EL_CATALOG` entry of that LS whose path passes `elBookPath`), so pages turn and answers save; the PDF of the student's level stays as a small **🖨 PDF to print** link. The teacher's own view gets 📖 Open the book plus small 🖨 PDF links per level. An LS with a `story` but no book page keeps the old PDF button.
  - **Extended reading (planned, not built):** one serial story per LS, a chapter per learning path, same plot at four lengths (Starter 150–250 words, Base 250–400, Core 400–600, Plus 600–900; shorter in 2 ESO), about 98 % known words checked by a script against the course word list (the Lexora list) plus a basic list, recycling words from earlier LSs; one gist question, “what happens next?” and a reading log; counted as reading by completion. The LS project writes the chapters.

## Writing and speaking revisions
- *Replaced (1–2 Nov 2026): every writing is handed in once and Claude corrects it; speaking is recording only (14 Nov). Kept as history.* **Revision counts (Roger, 26 Oct 2026, research audit):** in LS2 engine pages (`ls2-engine.js`), the first version a student sends for AI feedback is kept as the draft (`state.drafts[step_route] = {text, at}`; speaking: the transcript of that recording). The step completes only when they save an improved final version with a line on what they changed (`state.finals[step_route] = {text, changed, at}`; it must differ from the draft, and the line needs 3+ words). The final version is the product piece. The student sees their draft above the final version; the teacher sees draft, final version and "What I changed" in the activity dialog (`revisions` in `teacher.html`). Catalan for Insecure and Beginners.
- **Core words and dictation chunks (Roger, 26 Oct 2026, research audit):** each LS2 engine vocabulary panel has `core: {insecure:[…], safe:[…], daring:[…]}`: 15–20 words per route (all of them when the route sees fewer), the English text exactly as an item the route sees (`forLevel` rules; Beginners falls back to Insecure, so add `beginners` only for a shorter A1 list). Core words are for the Catalan→English flashcards and come back in later readings, warm-ups and checkpoints; the rest stay receptive. Each listening panel (`listen`, and the `chart` step) has `chunks: [...]`: 2–3 chunks of 3–6 words copied verbatim from `media.script` or the video `.vtt`, for the micro-dictation. Sample exams have none. A new LS adds both (playbook).
- **Goal and review (Roger, 26 Oct 2026, research audit):** every LS2 engine Learning Path (pages with a `recall` panel) and 4ESO Seasons & Celebrations `getting-started.html` (its own renderer, so it keeps a copy of the cards and the tracking note in `renderPlan()`; change both together) opens with a one-line "My goal" card and ends with "How did it go?" (what helped most, what to review), saved as `state.plan[route]` and `state.review[route]`, never a step and never marked; the teacher reads them in the activity dialog.

## 🎲 Surprise questions: a Time plan activity (Roger, 21 Nov 2026)
- **What it is:** a speaking activity the teacher makes from the Time plan, like 🎬 Shadowing or a class game ("like a game or shadowing that are anywhere"). The student presses Start, the recording begins, and four questions pop up one at a time, easy → harder (tiers About you · Describe it · the LS grammar · Your opinion), at the student's level, never seen before. Per level (`CFG` in `shared/js/el-surprise.js`): Starter 8 s to think + a sentence start (💬) + the Catalan; Base 8 s + word chips; Core 5 s, hears the question first (text shows when the voice ends), 💡 Help on a tap (logged); Plus 5 s, no help, longer answers; Pro like Plus. Stop ends the round; a new round draws questions not used last time (`state.sq[id].last`). Under 24 s of recording is refused.
- **Making one (teacher.html, Time plan):** the list has a **🎲 Surprise questions** row (`sqRow`) under the learning paths with **＋ New** (`sqOpen`): tick the SLPs (tabs) it draws from (the tabs done in class are ticked already), then **🏫 In class on** a class day or **🏠 As homework**. `sqMake` saves `ls/{id}.sq.sqN = {n, tabs: ["activity#panel"…], at}` and either puts `activity#sqN` on that class day (`agMove` + `agOpenSync`, so it opens with the class like any tab) or opens Set as homework with that tab ticked (`hwOpen`). Each one is a draggable chip "🎲 N" on the row.
- **Catalog:** one activity per LS with `sq: true` (`2eso/ls2-surprise-questions`, `4eso/ls2-surprise-questions`, before checkpoint 1 so no checkpoint closes it), page `surprise-questions.html`. Its tabs are the activities made (`elSqSync(ls, sq)` in `el-catalog.js`, called by `lsRead` in `el-cloud.js`, the live LS doc in `today.html` and `sqMake`; a copy in `localStorage el_sq_<ls>`; event `el-sq`). `elSqApply` fills `EL_ACTIONS`, `EL_SECTIONS`, `EL_STEP_CRITERIA` (3.2) and `EL_STEP_TITLES` for it, also when those files load later (property setters). It is never a learning path (`isLpA`), has no LP number and no "How this learning path works".
- **Where it shows:** students: a stop on the voyage road right after the last path it draws from (`vySq` in `today.html`; kind `bonus`), the tab on the page, To do / Homework like any tab. Teacher: in **Learning paths and activities** after the paths and checkpoints, a column **🎲 N** per activity (done / not done); with 🏫 Only done in class, only the ones done in class or set as homework.
- **Questions (Roger chose "Both"):** written questions where the LS has them, automatic ones for the rest. Written bank `data/surprise.js`: `window.EL_SURPRISE[bank] = {tiers, lps: {path: n}, kind: {panel: 'v'|'g'}, <route>: [[{q, ca?, h?, lp, k?}…] × 4 tiers]}` (2 ESO LS2: 64 questions; 4 ESO LS2: none yet). Automatic: `data/surprise-auto.js` = `window.EL_SURPRISE_AUTO[bank]["activity#panel"][route] = [{tier, q, ca?, h?}]`, made by `node tools/surprise/auto.mjs` from each tab's own words, grammar models and speaking task (re-run it when an LS's data changes). `draw()`: per tier, a written question of a chosen tab, else an automatic one of a chosen tab, else a written one of an earlier path.
- **Hand-in and marking:** a normal speaking hand-in (`handRec` → `elMic.upload`, Roig's transcript and notes) plus `sq: 1`, the questions asked and help used, so the teacher's speaking analysis (`spkQs`, `spkTask`) sees what was asked; it counts as speaking (`el-garden.js` `sq\d+`, criterion 3.2). The Wednesday run sends it with `mode: "surprise"` (`fbItems` in `el-marking.js`): one thing done well + 2–3 things to improve, never a level or a mark (`ELMark.sqText`; `el-corrections.js` shows "➜ Coses per millorar / Things to improve"). Students see only Done / Not done and those tips.

## Catalan-to-English cards and micro-dictation
- **Study cards have two directions (Roger, 26 Oct 2026, research audit):** English → Català (as before) and Català → English, where the card shows the Catalan and the student says the English before turning it (`state.cardDir`). The Catalan → English deck uses the vocab panel's `core` list (English words exactly as in the items, or one list per route) and every word of the Learning Path until the LS project adds it. Both LS2 engines. **No typing on the cards since 20 Nov 2026** (Roger: "no writing the prediction"): the 26 Oct / 3 Nov typed boxes (*Check and turn*) were removed; the cards are the 🎯 Practise ones (see Flashcards). **Every card is on the Explora list (Roger, 8 Nov 2026):** the English → Català deck also takes the panel's `review` words (earlier Learning Paths), so Explora shows them in a dashed "🔁 Repàs / Review · Learning Path N" box after the path's own groups; a word on a card must always be on the list, for every route. `node tools/vocab-cards/check.mjs` checks it (also run by the *Study cards on the word list* Action on PRs that touch LS data): every `core` word must be an item the route sees.
- **Listening cycle (Roger, 27 Oct 2026):** every listening panel (`mc` or `step` with audio, YouTube or video; not the sample exams or checkpoints) shows "Before you listen" (2–3 predicted words) above the media and "After the first listen" (a one-sentence gist, did you hear your words: all / some / none, then listen again for the questions) below it. Generic prompts, no LS content needed; practice only, saved in `state.cycle[panel]` (`isListening`, `predictHtml`, `gistHtml`, `bindCycle`; copy in 4ESO `getting-started.html` `renderCycle`).
- **Micro-dictation:** a listening panel (`type: 'mc'` with media, or a listening chart `type: 'step'`) that has `chunks: ['…', '…']` (2–3 bits of 3–6 words, exactly as in the script, chosen in the LS project) shows a dictation card after its questions: the browser's English voice says each chunk (normal and slower) and the student writes it. Practice only, not a step, not marked; answers in `state.dict[panel]`. No `chunks`, no card.
- 4ESO Seasons & Celebrations `getting-started.html` has its own renderer: it keeps copies of both (`renderCaCards`, `renderDict`), reading `core` from its `words` panel and `chunks` from its `listen` panel in `data/getting-started.js`. Change both places together.

## Grammar
- Grammar content is specific to each Learning Situation. Do not assume the same grammar across future Learning Situations.
- Follow the grammar requirements defined for the current LS.
- Differentiate routes mainly through scaffolding, complexity, amount of support and learner independence.
- **Grammar that comes back (Roger, 30 Oct 2026, after 4 ESO LS2 LP2 repeated the LS1 Present Simple drills for every route):** when an LS recovers a grammar point an earlier LS already taught (an R in the programme's grammar table), the basic drills of the old form go only to Starter and Base (Beginners, Insecure: items with no `r:` or `r:'insecure'`). Core and Plus (Safe, Daring) start from the new use or a contrast with what they know (4 ESO LS2: customs with *It is celebrated / takes place*, Present Simple vs Present Continuous, the passive), with at most one or two quick checks of the old form. A Learning Path never gives Core and Plus more old-form items than new ones. Paths students have already started stay as they are.

## Vocabulary and matching
- **Vocabulary games per LS (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "a set of vocabulary games for the new LS like this one, shown like in the other LS"):** every LS gets `<ls folder>/vocabulary-games.html`, the six self-practice games of LS1 (Alphabet Challenge, Jeopardy, Memory, Scramble, Word Rush, Mystery Picture), not graded and not in `EL_CATALOG` (so it is always open to the LS's groups; Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "games should be available"). `el-cloud.js` matches a page to the catalog by its path; a page not in the catalog never takes the id of another LS's page with the same file name (LS2's games were read as LS1's `4eso/vocabulary-games` and showed "not open yet"). From LS2 the pages are **generated**: `python3 tools/vocab-games/build.py` takes the LS1 page `4eso/my-real-routine/vocabulary-games.html` as the engine template, adds the Starter route (four routes, Starter · Base · Core · Plus; Starter and Base in Catalan; Starter always chooses from 3 options with a Catalan clue, Base types the rosco with letter hints and picks from 3–4 options, Core and Plus as Safe and Daring in LS1) and puts in `tools/vocab-games/data/<course>-<ls folder>.py` (the route's words from the LS vocabulary panels: bank, rosco letters, "Use it" Jeopardy questions, scramble sentences, Mystery scenes in `data/<course>-<ls folder>-scenes.js`). Edit the data and rebuild, never the generated JSON. Route buttons carry `.route[data-level]`, so `applyLevel` in `el-cloud.js` shows a signed-in student only their route (Pro plays Plus; the teacher and testers see all four). Linked like LS1: a `sample-exam-hero-link` "Vocabulary Games" on the LS landing page and a card in the English Lab Games menu (`lab/index.html`). A new LS only adds its two data files and runs the build.
- Vocabulary reference: `2eso/my-real-routine/a-day-in-my-life.html`. Reuse thematic groups (`.vocab-group`), compact expression pills (`.chip`) and hover **and keyboard-focus** support (`.chip-tooltip`) showing a Catalan meaning plus an English example. Adapt through the existing course palette.
- Matching tests only expressions explicitly studied in the route, cumulatively where appropriate. Do not accept untaught combinations merely because they sound natural in English.
- Judge the studied expression, not the original item ID or position. Identical left-side tiles must be interchangeable.
- Curate or validate generated sets so every round remains completable and pedagogically unambiguous; cumulative routes must meaningfully cover newly introduced vocabulary.
- Represent alternatives explicitly and naturally; avoid generic slash-splitting, awkward fragments and incomplete expression templates.
- Tell students to match studied Learning Path expressions even when other English combinations sound natural, and that identical tiles can be used interchangeably. Apply the route-language rules above.

## Flashcards
- **One card for the whole site (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "simplify the vocabulary cards, no writing the prediction, simple like this and full screen, like Practise"):** `shared/js/el-wordcards.js` (`ELWordCards.mount(host, {cards, ca, how, toolbar, note, onKnow, say})`) draws the cards of 🎯 Practise (`lab/practice.html`), of every LS2 Vocabulary step (all three `ls2-engine.js`) and of 4 ESO Seasons and celebrations Getting started. Look at the word, say it out loud, think what it means, **Gira la targeta / Turn over** (it plays the word and the example), then **✓ Ho sabia / I knew it** or **↺ Encara no / Not yet** (the card goes to the end of the deck). **No typing** on the cards. The whole deck, shuffled, counter `4 / 30 · group`. The first turn opens the cards full screen (an overlay on `document.body`, z-index 2147480000, below the class-mode lock bar); ✕ Tanca / Esc goes back and it stays closed until ⛶ Pantalla completa; Space / Enter turns the card; 🔀 Barreja mixes the cards left. The browser full screen is only left if the cards entered it. Never write a second card: change this one.
- **The LS2 cards step is done when the student has said ✓ Ho sabia to 10 cards** (all of them if the deck has fewer), as with the old set of 10; a line under the card says how many are left, then "✓ Pas fet". The cards never lock (they always work, as since 4 Nov 2026). LS1 pages keep `shared/js/flashcard-set.js` (Got it / Again, sets of 10) and are unchanged.

- **Grammar steps in parts (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "students don't have good activities to practise this theory"; "I also need to have examples to help me explain better and ask students to do examples"; first: 4 ESO LS2 LP3):** one "How it works" box per grammar point with the rule, several examples, the typical mistake (⚠) (these boxes are what 📘 Grammar together shows; no 🙌 All together / ✏️ Your turn boxes inside them, see Teaching flow), then `parts` ([{id, r, title, note}], numbered in the order each route sees them) with the practice in blocks (`item.p`), each block with different kinds of task: `hear: 1` 🎧 listen and complete · `opts` 👆 choose · `k: 'join' | 'change' | 'fix'` · `own: {must: [regex], min}` (+ `a: []`) ✏️ the student's own example, right when it has `min` words and every pattern (each question shows its kind as a small chip above it, except 👆 choose and plain fill-in questions, which get no chip: Roger, 19 Nov 2026, "I think it's evident"; join, change, fix, listen and own keep it because the chip is the instruction), never on Grammar together slides or the summary PDF · `show` = the answer as the student sees it. **Compulsory per block = the panel's `req` (now 4, see Teaching flow below; it was 7), the rest extra practice** (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "they are too many activities, make 7 of each section compulsory and the rest to practice … when they have done 7, a message appears saying you can turn in this activity"): per block, in the route's own list, the first 6 questions + the ✏️ own example are compulsory and shown first (so order the data: the first 6 of each block a mix of kinds), then "➕ Extra practice (optional)"; only the compulsory ones count (step done, first-try mark, Grammar together slides with the students' numbers); each block heading counts "3 / 7 compulsory", a green line says the block is done, and a bar "You can hand in this activity now · Check and hand it in" appears when all are answered. Each How it works box has `p` = its block and shows the block's number. Old-form points (connectors in 4 ESO LS2) go only to Starter and Base. **No listening exercises in grammar** (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "no fem exercicis d'escoltar a gramàtica. Canvia LP3 però no els altres"): 4 ESO LS2 LP3's Grammar has no 🎧 items and no `mode`, and since 15 Nov 2026 no grammar step anywhere has them (see below). More Past Simple practice with regular and irregular verbs, and *used to* + base form transformations (Roger: "they need more verbs to transform … sometimes they will have to use irregular verbs"). Panels without `parts` work as before (`ls2-engine.js` typedHtml).
- **A grammar step split into sections, one step each (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "This grammar section is too long, I need to break it down into 4 sections which I will explain in the next weeks, label them as what they are. They must be SLP with 7 activities (compulsory) and 7 optional"; first: 4 ESO LS2 LP3):** *Counts replaced on 16 Nov 2026: a new grammar block has 4 compulsory (3 questions + the own example) and the rest ➕ extra (REQUIREMENTS §5); 4 ESO LS2 LP3 keeps its 7 + 7 because students already started it.* each grammar point is its own panel (its own tab, Time plan slot, section switch and Grammar together), named "Grammar · <point>" (`grammar` keeps the first point, the others `grammar<Name>`: `grammarRel`, `grammarComp`, `grammarFeel`; `elIsGrammar(id)` in `el-catalog.js` is what 📘 Grammar together, class mode and the notebook check).
