Every level sees ONE block per step with exactly **7 compulsory (6 questions + the ✏️ own example) and 7 ➕ extra**, `req: 7`; an item's `r:` / `only:` decides who sees it, so check the counts per route after any change (each route's first 6 plain questions in data order are its compulsory ones). Starter and Base get their own easier list in each step (frames, choose, Catalan hints), Core and Plus the full one; no listening (no `mode`, no `hear`). The boxes come from one `GX(...)` function at the top of the data file (`window.LP = (function () { const GX = …; return {…}; })();`), no number on them (one block). The data's `LP.split = {from, ids, old}` moves each student's work once (`splitOld` in `ls2-engine.js`): answers checked in the old step go to the new step that holds each question (shown with a ✨ note until it is checked), answers typed but not checked move by their place in the old list (`old[route]`), the step that keeps the old id keeps its done and mark, and the new steps are done when checked (Roger chose "Carry answers, finish the rest"). New steps need: `EL_ACTIONS` + `EL_STEP_CRITERIA` (`el-catalog.js`), the "Practice done" list (`el-grading.js`), `node tools/gen-sections.js .` (keep the hand-made `ls2-book` lines), `el-coverage.js` links. A new step not on any class day shows 🏫 "We'll do it in class" until Roger puts it on the Time plan, so he opens them as he teaches them. **would** for past habits (Roger, 14 Nov 2026, 4 ESO only): a 💡 box in 4 ESO LP3's Past Simple step (Core/Plus and Base), two Plus extra questions, and the full explanation (states, time frame, weaker "not true now") in Pro's grammar (`data/native/`).
- **No listening or speaking in any grammar step (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "I dont want listening in the grammar here either", 2 ESO LP2; 15 Nov 2026: "yes grammar no listening anywhere"):** no Grammar panel has `mode: 'listen'` or `mode: 'say'` (2 ESO LP2, LP3, LP5, LP6 and 4 ESO LP3, LP5, LP6 lost theirs), so the gaps are written, with the same questions and the same compulsory ones. Mixed practice keeps `mode: 'say'`. The step criteria were left as they were (the 30 Oct mapping: a grammar step tagged LW or S keeps it), so no mark changed. A new grammar step never gets a `mode`.
- **Prepositions + vocab, out of the Vocabulary (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "here now it appears like a lot of vocab-grammar stuff and not real vocabulary … we could add a Prepositions + vocab and add it to the LP"; "and add activities to work on them"; first: 4 ESO LS2 LP3):** the Vocabulary step keeps real words of the topic (word groups, cards, Listen and complete); dates with in / on / at and words + preposition get their own step `grammarPrep`, tab "Prepositions + vocab", built like a grammar step (`type: 'typed'`, `req: 7`, `parts`, boxes from `GX`, 7 compulsory + 7 extra per level (in LP3, already started; from 16 Nov 2026 words with a structure are 💬 Expressions, 6 compulsory + 4 extra), no listening; its id starts with `grammar` so Grammar together, class mode and the notebook take it). Comparing words (similar to, both … and, unlike) belong to Grammar · Comparing. Students' answers move with `LP.split`, which can now be a list: `{from: 'vocab', ids: ['vocab', 'grammarPrep'], old}` takes a vocabulary panel's `practice` as its questions, and `old[route] = -1` marks a question that is in no step now.
- **How it works is always VISUAL, for grammar and for vocabulary structures (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "grammar should always be explained this way so that students understand better"; "Also vocab structures like prepositions"):** every `explain` box is built with the helpers inside the data file's `explain: (function () { … return [ … ]; })()` (copy them from any LS2 LP: `head`, `card`, `row`, `ex`, `small`, `scale`, `timeline`, `table`, `pill`, `tip`, `mistakes` + `bad`): what it is for, coloured cards with several examples each, a table or timeline when it helps and the red ⚠ ✗ → ✓ mistakes (no 🙌 All together or ✏️ Your turn box: see Teaching flow). Starter/Base boxes have `ca` (Catalan explanation, English examples, translations in the notes) and `en`. A Vocabulary step whose words have a structure (in/on/at, play/go/do, verb + preposition, It’s + adjective, adverbs of frequency…) gets the same `explain` boxes: `ls2-engine.js` shows them under Explore and the 📓 notebook shows them with the words. **They are 💬 Expressions, not grammar (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "why do we have grammar in the vocabulary section? … can we label them as Expressions"):** their card is titled "💬 Expressions · Words that go together: learn them as one block", 🎯 What's in the exam lists them in a 💬 Expressions section apart from 🧩 Grammar (`el-exam.js` gives the `vocabEx` panel `ex: true`, `el-notes.js`), and the notebook titles the slip "💬 Expressions · …" and lists it under 💬 Expressions in the summary (`el-notebook.js`). In the class games they belong to the 🧩 grammar game (see Class games, 💬 Expressions). **Their own step, 💬 Expressions (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to add these expressions on the agenda even if they are the same SLP of vocabulary"; "We need to add 6 activities for each Expression section … they won't see it as homework until I put it on the agenda"; "expressions must have the option like the grammar to correct together"; Vocabulary itself gets no together option):** the boxes are no longer on the Vocabulary step: each learning path that had them (2 ESO LS2 LP1, LP2, LP3; 4 ESO LS2 LP2) has a panel `vocabEx` right after Vocabulary, tab "💬 Expressions", built like a grammar step (`type: 'typed'`, `req: 6`, one part `ex`, the `explain` boxes, no listening): 6 compulsory questions per level (Starter and Base their own list with choices and Catalan hints, `only`; Core and Plus typed, `r: 'safe'`, with `only: ['safe']` / `r: 'daring'` where the boxes differ) and 4 ➕ extra questions for every level, which 📘 Expressions together takes its 🙌 4 questions from. `R.typed` titles its card "💬 Expressions" (both LS2 engines). `elIsGrammar` includes `vocabEx`, so 📘 Grammar together runs on it (shared/correct/, "💬 Expressions · together") and the whiteboard notes go to `gt-<page>-vocabEx` (notebook: 💬 Expressions together); the pages are in `EL_CORRECT_GRAMMAR`. It is a real step: `EL_ACTIONS` (after the Vocabulary steps), `EL_STEP_CRITERIA` (like practice), "Practice done" in `el-grading.js`, `el-coverage.js`. Until `<act>#vocabEx` is on a class day of the group's Time plan, students see the tab 🏫 for class, read-only, never To do or homework (`elLaterTabs`). When ticked it also ticks the 💬 points of the 🧩 grammar game (`lab/game/host.js`, which falls back to Vocabulary when the agenda doesn't have it). A new learning path's Expressions follow the same pattern. Changing only `explain` never touches students’ answers. Inline styles only (the boxes are shown raw on the LP, the projector, Practise, the worksheet and the notebook); 📝 notes keep their words with `<b>`/`<em>`/`<br>` (`flat` in `el-notes.js`, `gtFlat` in `el-cloud.js`). Core sees the defining / non-defining relatives difference too (4 ESO LS2 LP3, extra practice after the own example).
- **Teaching flow for grammar and vocabulary (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "when I teach grammar or vocab I want to have some activities to do all together … then they can write them on their virtual notebook their way and can work in pairs, in groups … then time to do the activities individually … not about volume but quality"):** 1) the visual How it works box, 2) 📓 notebook / pairs / groups, 3) a short individual set. **No practice boxes inside How it works** (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "take this out from everywhere … they have the activities already"): the 🙌 All together (gap-fill with ▶ Answer) and green ✏️ Your turn boxes were removed from every How it works box (2 ESO and 4 ESO LS2, Grammar and Vocabulary) with their `together` / `turn` helpers; the practice's own ✏️ questions (`own`) stay. Don't add them back to new learning paths. **🙌 4 questions together on the board (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "I only want to do 4 questions together and then give their time to do their questions on the SLP"):** 📘 Grammar together goes 📘 How it works → 🙌 4 questions → ⏱ Your turn, and ends there (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "students get instant feedback, question by question, I only want the questions before the time": no correction slides after ⏱ Your turn, Pro's neither; the class results stay on ⏱ Your turn). The 4 come from the ➕ extra practice, never from a question that is compulsory for any level (`togetherFor` in `correct-core.js`: the ones every level has first; otherwise half from the Starter/Base extras, half from Core/Plus); students keep the whiteboard during them. **One notes page per grammar point (Roger, 16 Nov 2026):** the whiteboard notes of a grammar step are saved in `notes/gt-<page>-<step id>` (`parts.gt.sec`), and the 📓 notebook puts each one (the boxes seen together, then their notes) right after its grammar step; an old one-per-page `gt-<page>` doc is moved to its step the next time Grammar together runs on that page (`gtLegacy` in `el-cloud.js`). **4 compulsory per block** (Roger chose "4 per point"): every LS2 Grammar step is in `parts` with `req: 4` (3 questions + the ✏️ own example, or 4 questions when the block has none); the rest is ➕ extra practice. `req` is read by `arrange()` in both `ls2-engine.js` and by `correct-core.js` (Grammar together's class results count only the compulsory ones); without `req`, 7. Reordering items into blocks keeps students' answers (they are matched by question text, `qid`) and a step already handed in stays done; a pure reorder shows no "new questions" note. A step in parts keeps its panel `mode` (🎧 / 🎤) for plain gaps; 4 ESO LS2 LP3 has none.

## Answer options
- **Randomize the answer options, never the questions.** Multiple-choice options and dropdown options are shuffled every time a question is shown, so the correct answer is not always in the same position. Question order stays as written.
- Use `learningSupport.shuffled()` / `learningSupport.shuffledIndexes()` from `shared/js/learning-path-support.js` (include that file in every page that renders questions). Keep each option's original value so scoring and feedback do not change.
- Do not change the wording of questions or options when shuffling.

## Typed answers: a spelling slip gets a spelling hint (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "the feedback is wrong then because I didn't make a grammar mistake"; "This should be the same for the students")
- **Where:** the block `SPELLING SLIPS … END SPELLING SLIPS` (`spellSlips(q, answers, typed, pageAnswers)` → `[[typed, right, 0 slip / 1 split / 2 joined]]`, `[]` = only apostrophes, `null` = not a spelling slip; `spellHint(slips, catalan)`), the same copy in every `ls2-engine.js` (2 ESO LS2, 4 ESO LS2, Towards Nativeness) and in `lab/practice.html` (🎯 Practise grammar). `tools/spelling-hint/check.mjs` (GitHub Action *Spelling hint*) fails when a copy differs or one of its cases changes: change the block in all four files and add a case.
- **Used by:** `checkTyped` (every `R.typed` panel: Catalan → English, grammar, practice, mixes; not `own`, `whole` (🔍 Spot the mistake has its own one-slip rule) or Starter's picks), the `quiz` panels' typed questions (sample exam too), 🎬 Movie dictation (Towards Nativeness) and Practise `gcheck`. It runs only on a wrong answer.
- **What is a slip:** the answer matches an accepted one (or a run of the gap sentence, as `fits`) word by word except: two letters swapped (laod; 2-letter words only when the typed word isn't a real word: od, not no/on), one letter wrong, missing or extra in a word of 4+ letters (two in 8+), a word split or joined (an other, alot). Never a slip: a typed word that is a real word (`SP_KNOWN` plus every word of the panel's answers), a grammar ending (`spGram`: s, es, d, ed, ing, n, en, r, er, est, ly), or more than a third of the words. Wrong answers with no slip keep the old message (the grammar rule `en`/`ca`).
- **Marking:** still wrong (the first try stays wrong, as a spelling slip in an exam); it doesn't add to `tries`, so it never brings the answer closer. Contractions count either way in `fits` (`bare(a) === bare(typed)`: It's = It is), which was already true for gaps.

## Typed answers: when an answer counts (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "I'm detecting a lot of mistakes, do students have the same problem?")
- **Where:** the block `ANSWER CHECK … END ANSWER CHECK` (`UK_US`, `bare`, `fits(q, answers, typed)`), the same copy in `2eso/free-time-live/ls2-engine.js`, `4eso/seasons-and-celebrations/ls2-engine.js` and `4eso/seasons-and-celebrations/getting-started.html` (its own renderer; it matched answers exactly until 22 Nov 2026). `tools/answer-check/check.mjs` (GitHub Action *Answer check*) fails when a copy differs or a case changes. Towards Nativeness keeps its own `fits` (with `wholeOk`); 🎯 Practise compares `expand(a) === expand(typed)`; checkpoints (`cpMark` in `teacher.html`) and web exams (`wxOk`) have their own keys and ✓ Accept.
- **What counts:** an accepted answer (contractions either way); a run of the gap sentence that holds the whole answer; American spellings (`UK_US`: gray, favorite, color, organize, practice…, mapped on both sides); a gap with first letters glued to it (`l_____`, `to c___`): the whole word or only the missing letters, and the cue letters are left out of the sentence run.
- **Keys:** list every natural answer in `a` (the first is the model shown): like / love / hate + to as well as + -ing, natural synonyms. An item whose grammar block tests one form keeps that form only (will vs going to, Present Simple for timetables, at the weekend). The audit of 22 Nov 2026 read every first try in `students/*/progress/{ls}` (`state.items`, read only): note that `q` indexes drift when items are added to a panel later, so the dashboard's Question analysis can show answers under the wrong question for older tries.

## Final Product
- Before working on an LS, read its `AGENTS.md`:
  - 4ESO: [LS1 My Real Routine](docs/4eso/ls1-my-real-routine/AGENTS.md) · [LS2 Seasons & Celebrations](docs/4eso/ls2-seasons-and-celebrations/AGENTS.md)
  - 2ESO: [LS1 My Real Routine](docs/2eso/ls1-my-real-routine/AGENTS.md) · [LS2 Free Time, Live!](docs/2eso/ls2-free-time-live/AGENTS.md)
- Follow the current LS’s route requirements and submission format.
- Required task elements must be explicit and must not be added to routes that do not require them. Language criteria support the task rather than obscure it.
- **On the site, Gemini is called "Roig" (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "Instead of Gemini I want you to call it roig, so that nobody understands it, but everywhere on the web").** Every word a person can read (teacher dashboard labels, buttons, tooltips, alerts, dialogs, emails and any student page) says **Roig**, never Gemini, Google AI or another AI name; model names show as "Roig 4 Argon" etc. (`fpgModel` in teacher.html). `esc()` in teacher.html also turns "Gemini" into "Roig" for text that comes from outside (school script errors, older saved drafts). Code names, comments, API calls, model ids and the Gemini prompts stay as they are. Students must never learn that AI is used at all. **Claude is called "Beltran" in the same way** (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "Claude must be called Beltran"): every visible word on the site and in the emails it sends Roger (tools/notify) says Beltran, and `esc()` also turns "Claude" into "Beltran"; the "Claude Desktop" app is "the app", and claude.ai link texts are not shown.
- **✨ Correct in my Roig: Roger marks videos in his own Gemini app, nothing paid (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "I dont want to use gemini anymore since it's going to be very expensive. What I want is that when I press correct, it opens my gemini with the video attached and a prompt with the rubric").** `FPG_APP = true` in `teacher.html`: the side panel and the final product box show ✨ Correct in my Roig instead of 🎬 Watch / 🔄 Watch again / ✏️ Ask to change / 📥 bulk / 📁 folder. It copies `fpgAppPrompt` (the school script's agreed `fpPrompt` wording, with the rubric, no names, 🧠 lessons, the answer as one JSON block, and "Please attach the video" when none is attached) and opens gemini.google.com with the school account. A web page cannot attach a file to another site's chat, so the panel lists ⬇ Download the video (drag it into the chat, or ＋ › Drive in Gemini), and a box to paste Gemini's answer: `fpgAppRead` reads it like `fpAsk` (rows by n, marks kept in FP_BAND) and it is saved as a draft (`model: "app"`, `app: true`, `gem0`), so ✅ Approve, levels, 🧠 learning and words said wrong work as before. 🤖 Automatic drafts are switched off per final product (`fp_auto_setup` with `on: false`) when its page opens. `FPG_APP = false` brings the paid buttons back.
- **Videos are uploaded on the site, and Gemini drafts the marks (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "students load the final product on the web … the files could go to my drive and once a year I delete the data"; "then I'd watch the videos and I'd approve it or not").** A final product video goes in through the Submit tab's 🎬 Upload your video card (`video: true` on the `checklist` panel, or `'group'` when one member uploads the group video; `shared/js/el-video.js`). It is sent in 8 MB pieces through the school Apps Script (`tools/notify/school-mail.gs`, `video_start`/`video_chunk`/`video_status`) into Drive Teacher Studio › 04_Students (private) › Final product videos › group, and saved as the hand-in `state.steps.video` (no fields, so the Wednesday correction skips it). The catalog's `video: true` makes the board say "upload the video on its page" instead of Classroom. In the dashboard's final product dialog, Gemini watches it (`fp_watch`: Drive → Gemini Files API, deleted from Gemini at once) and drafts reading or speaking, a level per rubric row (LS2: `fp.rows` + `ind`, the same for every route; LS1: `EL_FP_RUBRICS`) and feedback in Roger's voice. Roger watches the video next to it, then ✅ Approve (levels or mark + feedback, saved) or ✏️ Ask Gemini to change it (it watches again with his words). Drafts are kept in `settings/fp__{activity}` (teacher only). 💶 Gemini this month (`gemini_usage`) adds up every Gemini call. New LSs: put `video: true` on the final product's Submit panel and the catalog entry. Claude never deletes videos in Drive. **Classroom hand-ins** (LS1 and older ones; Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "some are links … you tell me the videos that you couldn't reach"): 📥 Look on Classroom / 📥 Draft the whole group from Classroom in the same dialog ask the school script (`fp_classroom`, Classroom API service, read-only) for the group's "Final product" hand-ins, matched by email; Drive videos and YouTube links are drafted one after another, and a report lists what Gemini couldn't reach (Canva links, Google Vids, files the school account cannot open) for Roger to watch himself. **The prompt** (`fpPrompt`, agreed with Roger 8 Nov 2026: "positive and friendly … I assume they will all read and we have to suggest that they try not to … I don't want to give grades they don't deserve"): kind but honest; reading counts only in the Speaking row and always gets a friendly tip; slips quoted with the correct form; LS1 rows get a mark /10 in steps of 0.5 inside the level's band (4 = 9–10, 3 = 6.5–8.5, 2 = 4–6, 1 = 1.5–3.5; "3+" = top of the band) and the final mark is the weighted average; a teacher comment that opens with the first name. Gemini also lists the **words said wrong** (`sounds`, teacher only, never in the feedback): 🗣️ List for the whole group gathers them for Roger's listen-and-repeat video in class (made without Gemini). **🧠 Gemini learns from Roger's changes** (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "I want gemini see my changes and learn from them … on the videos - final product"): when he ✅ approves a draft he changed (a level clicked, the comment rewritten, ✏️ Ask Gemini to change it), Gemini's first draft (`gem0` on the draft) and his final version are kept in ONE document, `settings/fp__lessons` `{l: [newest first, max 40]}`, read once per page. Every `fp_watch` sends `ask.lessons`: his 6 latest corrections as short texts (same final product first, never the student being watched, the student's own names blanked to "…", no ids), and the school script (v23+) adds them to the prompt. The final product page's 🧠 What Gemini learns from you lists them, with 🗑 Forget. Final product videos only, never speaking recordings. With Roger's changes on a draft (a level clicked, the comment rewritten, a ✏️ change) the button reads 💾 Save my marks instead of ✅ Approve (the same save for the student), the mark reads "Mark with your changes (Gemini: x)", and 🧠 under it says what Gemini learnt. A row's first click keeps Gemini's value (`g0`) and the first comment edit keeps Gemini's comment (`top0`), so drafts made before `gem0` still teach Gemini from then on. **Very good = 10/10** (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "4/4 is 10/10"): a rubric row at level 4 is always 10 (fpgRow, a click on Very good, school script v24 FP_BAND); Good/Fair/Developing keep Gemini's in-band marks, and a click gives 7.5/5/2.5.
- **LS1 2 ESO videos whose Classroom link doesn't work can be uploaded again on the site (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "all the links that didn't work … I want students to receive a mail and hand it through the app"):** `reup: true` on the catalog entry (`2eso/final-product`) puts the same `el-video.js` card at the top of its 7 Submit tab (saved as `steps.video` in `finalProduct2ESO`, top level beside the routes), and the email link is `2eso/my-real-routine/final-product.html#submit`. The dashboard drafts those uploads by itself as for `video: true` (school script v25, level A1 for LS1 2 ESO). A student with a saved 0 for nothing to mark (a draft of the teacher's own with a reason: not handed in / the link doesn't work) gets Gemini's draft in its place; the 0 stays saved and the row shows 🟡 to approve + 🆕 new video until the teacher approves (`re0` on the draft). A draft of the teacher's own with real marks is never replaced.

## Self-assessment at the end of every LS (Roger, 2 Nov 2026)
Every LS from LS2 on ends with a Reflect page whose **first tab is a self-assessment**: a panel `{type:'self'}` (`shared/js/el-self.js`; the engine has `R.self`, the page loads `el-self.js` before the engine; catalogue flag `self:"self"` on the Reflect entry, `self` in its `EL_ACTIONS`). When you design a new LS, write its statements in `data/reflect.js`: **two "I can…" statements for each communicative competence (CE2–CE5, the same number each, per the four-skills rule)**, then one or two for each other competence the LS assesses (integrated and transversal, with their criteria in `c`), then two on how I learn (CE9 9.3). Each statement names a concrete task of the LS and links (`lp:[page, tab]`) to the Learning Path tab that worked it. Catalan text for Starter/Base, English for Core/Plus/Pro, one set for all routes. Students tap 1–4 (no free text, nothing for Roger to read) and pick the one competence to improve in the next LS; a statement rated 1–2 gives them a review link at once. The dashboard's 🪞 Self-assessment box (LS view, `selfBox` in `teacher.html`) reads it from the synced page state (no extra reads) and shows each competence's self average next to the student's mark for it, with ⬆/⬇ for big gaps. It never counts in the mark (Roger chose this on 2 Nov 2026: ratings stay honest). Paper worksheets print it too (`lp-worksheet.js` `R.self`). Never add it to LS1.

## Assessment and grading
- **The weekday marking run also runs the speaking analysis (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: every recording, every day):** after the corrections (`fb-apply`) and before `audit`, the scheduled "English Lab: mark and correct" run calls `node tools/speaking/analyse.mjs --apply` (teacher-only Roig analysis of new Speaking, Talk and Surprise recordings; about €1 a month) and reports how many were analysed and the €. A recording it can't analyse is reported, never a reason to stop.
- **The weekday marking run gives every level moment its feedback (📐, Roger, 24 Nov 2026):** the scheduled "English Lab: mark and correct" run calls `node tools/marking/marks.mjs apply <group> <activity> <step> <file> --apply --feedback` for the Reading log, Listening chart and research card sheets, so students read the correction with its level (the degree box) from term 2. A line with `REDO?` (not a real try) is saved as NA and sent back to redo automatically; the run reports how many.
- **Every LS criterion ends with a degree** (NA, AS, AN, AE) that the teacher can justify with its indicators (`indicators.md` of the LS) and a piece of work. The level comes first; the mark is worked out from it. Each LS documents its evidence per criterion in a `grading.md`; worked example: [4ESO LS2](docs/4eso/ls2-seasons-and-celebrations/grading.md), also in Roger's reference doc *Worked example: from competence to LS criterion*.
- **Secure recall checkpoints (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “make sure they don't cheat”; `docs/general/secure-checkpoints.md`):** LS2 checkpoint pages have no answers or hints in their data (`secure` panel, item `id`s; the key is in `cpKeys/{page key}`, teacher only, uploaded with `tools/checkpoint-keys/upload-keys.mjs` from a file kept outside the repo). `shared/js/el-checkpoint.js`: one question at a time, 45 s each, no going back, no copy or paste, leaving the page for more than 2 s sends it (⚡), one try (`students/{uid}/checkpoints/{key}`, create only in `firestore.rules`), corrections only when the teacher presses “📣 Release feedback” in the group tab's “🛡️ Recall checkpoints” box, which also has “✓ Accept”, “↺ Let them do it again” (also for one student when the checkpoint is 🔒 closed or 📣 shown for the group: the “– ↺” cell of a student who never sent it writes `{redo: true}`, and `firestore.rules` `cpOk(letMe)` lets only that student send it; Roger, 11 Nov 2026, Ayman 4ESOB) and ⚠️ when a checkpoint is 35 points or more above the student's usual marks (exam marks so far and recall warm-ups, `cpGap`). The dashboard marks the tries with the key (`cpInject`, in memory) so grading, the progress table and the Question analysis are unchanged. Every new LS writes its checkpoints this way (playbook). **Full screen (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "you can't browse the website but can go to other tabs"):** Start puts the page in full screen (not on iPhones) and locks Esc (Chrome Keyboard Lock: a press only shows a warning; holding it down leaves); leaving full screen sends the checkpoint at once; a "Turn in" button (asks once) ends it early; the rules say so.
- **Web exams (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: 2 ESO LS2 exam, "like the checkpoints … if they exit full screen the exam is over, but I want to be able to allow students to go back"):** `<ls>/exam.html` + `data/exam.js` (questions, options sorted, NO answers; built with `tools/web-exam/build.mjs` from a source file kept in project files `web-exam-2eso-ls2/`, never in the repo) + `shared/js/el-web-exam.js`. The key is `cpKeys/{exam key}.web` (not `items`, so `cpHashSync` never hashes it for students), uploaded with `tools/web-exam/upload-keys.mjs` (dry run; `--apply` only after Roger says so). The teacher opens it per group (`ls/{id}.webExam[group].open`, the 🖥 Web exam box on the 📝 Exams place). Starter/Base get the `base` paper, Core/Plus `core`, version A, B or C by account (as many as the exam has, 19 Nov 2026); sections and options shuffled per student. Listening first, together; "Listening finished" locks it and opens the rest. Review before turning in. **✍️ Writing** (Roger, 17 Nov 2026 "add writing"): one `t: "write"` item per paper (textarea, word count; every level: the task in English with the Catalan underneath, Base also a frame; students can copy and paste their OWN text inside their boxes, as on the rest of the site, nothing from outside); no key: Claude marks it with C1–C4 (below, 17 Nov 2026); the 5.1 level click in the student's dialog on the dashboard (NA 8 · AS 15 · AN 23 · AE 30 of 30, scaled, writes `sections.writing` + `levels.writing` straight away) is only a manual backup; a blank writing is 0. **Results 4 days later** (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "we release the results automatically 4 days later"): 📥 Save marks and `tools/web-exam/writing.mjs` set `results/{ls}__assessment.showFrom` (exam day + 4, 8:00); `el-cloud.js` `held()` hides sections, report and the writing level from the student (and the teacher's student view) until then, and the board says when they come out. **Writing corrected like the LS1 exam** (Roger, 17 Nov 2026): `node tools/web-exam/writing.mjs export <key> <out.json>` → Claude corrects each text (rubric C1 8 · C2 4 · C3 4 · C4 4, corrected text, mistakes table, feedback in Roger's voice; Catalan for Base, English for Core) → `import <key> <ls> <marked.json> [--apply]` (live write: only after Roger's "apply"). **Drafts** (Roger, 23 Nov 2026): `import … --draft` writes only `results/{ls}__assessment.wdraft` {score, max, level, at}; `teacher.html` `wxW()` returns it as `draft` when no real `sections.writing` exists, the web exam table shows "≈ 6.5 draft" and `wxMark` adds it to a provisional exam mark (`m.draft`, shown with ≈). Students' pages ignore `wdraft`; the real `--apply` deletes it. **🎮 Games after turning in** (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: hangman etc., points build scaffolding up a building): `shared/js/el-exam-games.js`, content in `data/exam.js` `games` (LS words + a quiz, never exam items), practice only, points in localStorage. Leaving full screen, another tab or app for more than 2 s, or a reload stops it (`status: "out"`, a copy of the answers in `snaps`); Roger types his password on the student's screen to let them back in (only a PBKDF2 hash in `data/exam.js`: set it with `EXAM_PASSWORD=… node tools/web-exam/build.mjs …`; never write the password anywhere). Changes after coming back are logged (`changes`) and the dashboard compares the first snap with the final answers (⚠️ +n right after coming back). `firestore.rules` `match /exams/{key}`: own doc, fixed field list, open for the group, never after done, `ls`/`paper`/`started` fixed, `snaps` append-only. 💾 Save marks writes `results/{ls}__assessment` sections + the report, like the paper exams.
- **💾 Exam marks save themselves, 📣 Publish, corrections after publishing (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "it makes no sense to save the exams, the exams are already saved, I only need a button to publish them when I want… even if published I need to be able to accept questions claude didnt accept or change the writing correction with a button correct again or correct manually"; replaces 📥 Save marks and the "4 days later at 8:00" release above):** `teacher.html` `wxRec` builds what Save marks wrote (sections listening…grammar, a blank writing 0, `version`, `webPaper`, the full report, keeping a corrected `report.writing`) and `wxSync(ls)` writes it for every turned-in exam (or stopped once closed) whose sections / question ticks differ (`wxSig`), never a paper row or a tester; it runs after every `wxLoad` (📝 Exams), after ✓ Accept / ✗ Undo (the key changed, so every student with that question), and from Home via `wxAuto` (render, every 10 min per LS at most): an LS whose results have a corrected writing (`report.writing.rubric`) but no `sections.listening` reads its web exams once and syncs (no reads otherwise). **Release:** a record saved for the first time gets `showFrom = WXHOLD` = `9999-12-31T00:00:00.000Z` (also `writing.mjs import`); `el-cloud.js` `held()` hides it as before and `elHeldDay(v)` (null for that date) lets the board say "soon" instead of a date (`today.html` examView / skills card, `el-ls-home.js`). The 🖥 Web exam box shows 🔒 n not published (or the date already set) and **📣 Publish the results (n)** = `wxPublish` → `showFrom = now` on every record of the group's students; once all are out, 📣 Published + 🔒 Hide again (back to WXHOLD). **Writing after marking:** the student's web exam window has **✏️ Correct it by hand** (`wxEditH` / `wxEditSave`: rubric rows, comment, corrected text → `*…*` with `wxStar` (as `writing.mjs` mark), mistakes "mistake → correction → why" per line; score = rubric total scaled to the section, 5.1 level NA < 5 · AS < 7 · AN < 9 · AE; `report.writing.by = "teacher"`, clears `wredo` / `wdraft`) and **🔄 Correct again** (results `.wredo = {at, note}` + `settings/marking.examRedo["uid__ls"] = {uid, ls, key, group, at, note}`, then `api.mkAsk(true)` starts the marking run). `node tools/web-exam/writing.mjs redo <out.json>` lists them (one read of settings/marking; "No writings to correct again." when none) with the note and the current correction; `import … --apply` clears `wredo` and the examRedo entry. The 5.1 level buttons show only while a writing has no correction (a click replaced Claude's mark, 23 Nov 2026).
- **📧 Turning in failed → a sealed code by email (Roger, 23 Nov 2026: "a window pops up and say, send your exam to your teacher … with their mail (not mine)"; "can we send the mail with the text encripted?" … "students can't give the answers to other classes either"; the 🖨 Print / Save as PDF of PR #845 was removed: "Remove it"):** `turnIn()` in `el-web-exam.js` races `save()` with 12 s (offline, Firestore never answers); on failure `stuckBox()` sets `S.stuck` (local only, not in `payload`): `set()` ignores changes, guards unbound (no lock), and a window offers 📧 Send it to my teacher = `gmailUrl(body)` (mail.google.com compose in the student's own account, to `TEACHER_MAIL`) and 🔄 Try again (`retry`, also every 15 s and on `online`). `mailText()` (async) puts only who / group / paper in clear and the answers as `ELExamSeal.seal({v, ls, key, uid, email, name, group, paper, level, order, answers, started, listenDone, done, sealed, exits, plays})` from **`shared/js/el-exam-seal.js`**: JSON → deflate-raw (`CompressionStream`) → AES-GCM (key = SHA-256 of a fixed passphrase; the site is public, so it is a seal against reading and editing, not a secret) → base64url in 60-char lines between `-----BEGIN ENGLISH LAB EXAM-----` / `-----END ENGLISH LAB EXAM-----`. GCM fails on any changed letter (`open()` throws `broken`). No Web Crypto → no email button (never plain answers). `@media print{body>*{display:none}}`: printing the exam page gives a blank page. `save()` notices a late `done` write that got through (`sentDone && S.stuck` → `stuckDone()` → handedIn). A reload while stuck reopens the window (local copy with `stuck`), not the lock. **Dashboard:** the web exam box's 📥 Open an emailed exam (`wxMailDlg`, `data-wx` mail / mopen / mturn) opens the pasted code, matches the student by uid then email, refuses a code of another exam key, shows the answers with ✓/✗ and the mark (`wxMark`), how many answers differ from the website's last copy, and 🏁 Turn it in writes `students/{uid}/exams/{key}` merge {answers, order, paper, …, status:'done', done, emailed:{sealed, at}} (teacher write; exits and log kept). Cache `el-web-exam.js` / `el-exam-seal.js` `?v=20261123b`.
- **📝 Exam mode + make-up exams (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "when I go exam mode all students get pulled up, I explain the rules, then I press start and they can't exit"; "I must be able to make one student or some students do an exam"):** the 🖥 Web exam box has 📝 Exam mode (whole group) and 👤 Only some students… (ticked by default: no exam mark and no web exam yet). It writes `ls/{id}.webExam[group] = {open, mode, go, only, at}` and `live/{group}.exam = {on, href, ls, only, at, go}` (and `go: null`: class mode stops; with `only`, class mode goes on for the others). `el-cloud.js` `examPull` takes the open pages of the group's students (only the listed emails when `only` is set) to the exam page and keeps them there; on the exam page the student ticks the rules, goes full screen and waits (`el-web-exam.js` `waiting`/`watchGo`); ▶ Start the exam sets `go` and every waiting exam starts at once (leaving full screen while waiting changes nothing). Late arrivals use the Start button. `only` also limits who can start it (page and `firestore.rules` `exOpen`). 🔒 Close / ⏹ End clears both. **4 ESO LS1 make-up** (Elunei): `4eso/my-real-routine/exam.html`, key `ls1Exam4ESO`, source in project files `web-exam-4eso-ls1-makeup/` (same listening as the LS1 exam, its questions from the A–D papers; one version per level: `base-A`, `core-A`, the page falls back to -A). LS1's writing is wtask/wgrammar/wvocab/worg, so it is marked apart (tools/exam-marks), not in the web exam box; the box shows the mark without it (*).
- **Make-ups play their own audio and take the next version (23 Nov 2026)**: in `el-web-exam.js`, `isOpen` sets `MU` when `webExam[group].only` lists the student's email. Then `auMax()` = `X.audio.plays || 2` (the self-play box, rules text and "with headphones" intro), and `begin()` picks `vs[(hash + 1) % vs.length]` instead of `vs[hash % vs.length]`, so a redo never repeats the class version. The exam-mode panel shows no projector audio while `.only` is set. Exams whose data already has `audio.plays` (4ESO LS1 make-up page) are unchanged.
- **A redo after a try that cannot count (25 Nov 2026, 4ESOA LS2 make-up: a student's page reopened the exam done at home)**: the teacher's copy of the old `students/{uid}/exams/{key}` doc goes to project files, the doc is deleted with the service account, and `ls/{ls}.webExam[group].reset[uid]` = the old try's `started`. `el-web-exam.js` `student()` then drops a local copy or a cloud copy with that start, so the student begins a new paper (the make-up's next version). The old try's page must be closed first, or its next save recreates the doc.
- **📝 Exams and class mode (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "Elunei will take the exam during class mode, there mustn't be any problem"; "2ESO exam should be on the agenda and when I go class mode they should all go to the exam"):** on the exam page `el-web-exam.js` sets `window.EL_EXAM_LOCK` (off on "not open yet" and after turning in): `el-cloud.js` `gamePush` then moves nothing (class mode, class games, pull-ups), and `examPull` runs before the class-game pull. The exam page has its own full screen (`ownFs`: never inside class.html, no class full-screen cover; it leaves the shell if it lands there) and no new-day reload. On the exam day of the Time plan (`EL_PLAN[ls].exam`) of an LS with `EL_GRADING[ls].exam.web`, 🎓 Class mode opens the exam page (`teacher.html` `cmTarget`), and 📝 Exam on the 📋 Today agenda is a Go → button to it. On the class tab (`sessionStorage.el_cm`) the exam page's teacher view shows a class panel (`classPanel`): it turns exam mode on for the group (keeps a dashboard `only`), ▶ Start the exam, 🔒 Close the exam, and the class audio player. Questions are never shuffled (the paper's order = the audio's and the text's); options still are. **🔊 Student-played audio:** `audio.plays` (e.g. 2) with `audio.src` in the exam data: the student plays the listening herself, at most that many times, no pause, no seek (`auPlay`; `plays` saved in the exam doc, never goes down: `firestore.rules`). Used by the 4 ESO LS1 make-up (`4eso/my-real-routine/audio/exam-listening.mp3`; its listening questions were rewritten from the recording on 17 Nov 2026, in its order).
  - **Tester (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "the tester should be able to do the exam before to check everything works"):** the tester account (`isTester`) can start the exam while it is 🔒 closed for every group (the rules already let it write), and picks the paper (Base A/B, Core A/B) on the start screen. Its try shows as a 🧪 row at the end of the dashboard's Web exam box in every group, never counted or saved with the marks; 🧪 Tester: start again deletes it.
  - **Same level and difficulty as the learning paths (Roger, 7 Nov 2026):** a checkpoint's questions follow the route ladder like the paths (`r` tags, `forLevel`: Starter 4–6, Base 8, Core 11, Plus 13; Pro its own C2 set), and Starter picks each answer from **three options**, as in its learning paths. The options are item `o` in the page data (the key stays secret), made with the engine's own `choices()` by `tools/checkpoint-keys/starter-options.cjs <keys.json> --write` (authored wrong options in its `D` map); run it again after changing a checkpoint's Starter items. One pick, no hints or retries (it is a checkpoint). Only Starter sees them; the dashboard marks the same way.
- **Practise again after the feedback (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: students may redo a checkpoint "without it counting"):** once the teacher releases the feedback, the checkpoint shows 🔁 Practise again: the student's own questions in a new order, checked on the page against `fb.right`; nothing is saved or sent and the first try stays the mark. New questions are not possible there (the key stays teacher-only). Never say on the page that it does not count.
- **LS mark: the rule for every LS from LS2 on (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "let's make it this way in every LS from now on"; LS1 stays exam 75 / final product 25):** final exam 35 % · final product 25 % · **daily work in the learning paths 40 %**, shared by the amount of work behind each part:
  - block `gv` "Grammar, vocabulary and skills" 35 %, as `gv.parts` weights in % of the LS: **recall checkpoints 10** (first try of all four; a bit more than their share because they are the only scored, uncopyable part) · **recall warm-ups 2.5** (first try of LP1 Getting started–LP6; the recall exception to "first tries never count for the mark": Roger, 22 Nov 2026, "The recall keep the latest but I will use the first try to assess them"; for a few hours that day they counted as done) · **practice, grammar and mixed practice done 5** · **class games 2.5** (`games:true`, no steps: % right in the LS's grammar and vocabulary class games from `gameResults`, all games pooled as the 🧩📚 circles; a game the student missed or answered under half of is left out, never 0, a game played again counts only its latest play (`elLatestGames`, also the 🧩📚 circles; Roger, 11 Nov 2026: "the last version of the answers is the good one"), even with "Missing work counts 0"; the dashboard waits for the group's games before `progSync`; Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "the performance in those games should be counted", 2.5 taken from practice) · **skills tabs done 15** (every read/log/listen/chart/again/shadow/sources/parts/write/speak/talk tab of LP1–LP6: list them all in `time`, check each one exists in `EL_ACTIONS`). **Done late counts the same as on time** (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "ni que les facin tard compta el mateix"; from 10 Nov to 14 Nov it was half): in every "done" part (`time` lists) a tab done after its day counts in full (`LATE_W` = 1 in `teacher.html`; `onTime` still returns `LATE` so the dashboard can show "done late"), a tab not done is 0 (only once ticked in the Time plan and its day has passed) until the student does it; the students' "My LS mark so far" warning says so. Recall warm-ups and checkpoints are unchanged (first try). **Getting started (LP1) counts too** (Roger, 6 Nov 2026, after a 2 ESO student had done all of it, the board said “1 of 1 done” and his LS mark said “not yet”): its warm-up, practice (4 ESO: words) and read/listen/speak tabs are in `warm`, `time` and `skills`, since the board tells students every activity not done by its day counts 0;
  - block `other` "Other competences" 5 %: the integrated competences (CE1, CE6–CE10) the LS assesses, from learning-path work only (steps done + the teacher's level on an LP piece); their levels on final-product pieces go into the final product 25 % as `fp.rows`.
  - Exam sections carry the real exam points; final product rows carry the rubric weights (keep `final-rubric-data.js` pct in step).
  - Key competences are the communicative skills (CE2–CE5), worked equally in the paths and assessed in the exam and final product. Transversal criteria (CC, CD, CPSAA, entrepreneurial) are assessed and shown apart but never count in the LS mark (Roger, 30 Oct 2026).
  - Accuracy only comes from delayed retrieval, never from first tries on brand-new practice, so students can retry practice freely (research audit, Roger 26 Oct 2026: https://claude.ai/artifact/FhBnrKpdnaYA5C4bjDoBbT).
  - Students see these parts in plain words on their 📈 "My LS mark so far" (give each `gv.parts` entry `sn`: "remember" for checkpoints and warm-ups, "practice", "skills"; labels in `SN` in `today.html`), never which activities are the checkpoints or "first try". If an LS's paths differ a lot in amount of work, propose new numbers to Roger before changing them. History: 24 Oct 2026 the split was 35/25/20 G&V/20 other.
- **Competences per term (Roger, 30 Oct 2026, analysis: https://claude.ai/artifact/U59N9aDvHN7AbUExgjCrEZ):** every term assesses the four communicative competences (CE2–CE5) plus the integrated competences (CE1, CE6–CE10) that the term's two LSs assess, usually 2–4, never a fixed number. No Departament rule sets a number per term: assessment is global, continuous and formative, the criteria are the reference, the subject mark (NA–AS–AN–AE) is the level reached in each competence by the end of the year, and an LS lists only the competences it plans activities for (Decret 175/2022 curriculum; DOIGC *Concreció i desenvolupament del currículum: ESO*, 2026, section 3). Both programmes already follow it (2 ESO 6/6/8 competences per term, 4 ESO 7/8/7; all 10 every year). Every competence must appear at least once a year; CE8 (mediation) gets a second small assessed task in another LS of each course when that LS is designed (2 ESO: LS5 or LS6; 4 ESO: LS5). Transversal competences are not part of any English mark (LS, term or year); the dashboard shows them separately (`isTransC` in teacher.html for the LS mark).
- **Integrated-competence gaps to close in every new LS (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "remind me to work on them when we prepare new LS"):** when a new LS is designed (here or in the LS project), first remind Roger of these gaps and plan them into the design. Map (● assessed, ○ implicit, ◇ planned): https://claude.ai/artifact/U59N9aDvHN7AbUExgjCrEZ, section "Mapa per LS". (1) **CE7:** a serial story (one chapter per learning path, read at home, about 10 minutes in class each week; see Extended reading and the thread "Write a serial reading story per course"). (2) **CE8:** a short mediation step (explain a text or video to someone who didn't see it); assessed a second time in 2 ESO LS5 or LS6 and 4 ESO LS5. (3) **CE1:** the only LSs with no CE1 are 2 ESO LS2 and 4 ESO LS4, so give 4 ESO LS4 a small intercultural element. (4) **CE6** is thin in LS3 of both courses: add a source check. After each LS design, update `shared/js/el-comp-map.js` (○ implicit, ◇ planned, the programme criteria per LS; the dashboard's 📆 Whole school year shows it as "Map of the integrated competences", with ● taken from the grading plan) and the doc's map. Its LS names (`name`) are also what students see as "🔜 Coming soon" in 🎓 My progress (today.html#progress) until the LS is on the site and open for their group, so keep them to the programme's names. An LS goes in term ceil(n/2) there (or EL_LS `.term`).
- **📖 Reference tab on the teacher dashboard (Roger, 31 Oct 2026):** English competences and criteria (both bands), cross-curricular competences (54 criteria, link with English), grammar map and lexicon map, 1r–4t ESO, with the LS and term for 2n/4t and only the course for 1r/3r (no LSs programmed yet). Read-only: `shared/js/el-reference.js` renders `shared/js/el-reference-data.js`, which is generated by `python3 tools/reference/build-reference.py <grammar.docx> <lexicon.docx>` from `tools/reference/curriculum.json`, the two `docs/<course>/programme.md` coverage tables and the Drive maps (Teacher Studio › 05). Re-run it after an LS design, a programme change or a map change; never edit the data file by hand. Every course header in its tables shows the Core route's CEFR level (Roger, 6 Nov 2026): 1r A1 · 2n A2 · 3r A2+ · 4t B1 (2n/4t from `EL_CEFR`, 1r/3r from the levels table above, which calls 3r Core "more autonomous A2, towards B1", shown as A2+ so it differs from 2n; `CORE` in `el-reference.js`).
- **🎯 Coverage tile on the group page (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "see if we have worked all the assessment criteria and competences planned for each LS and the year plan. Also, the grammar and the lexicon … so we can add some grammar in other LS if necessary"):** `covPage` in `teacher.html`, this LS or 📆 the whole year, tabs Criteria and competences · Grammar · Lexicon, "Only what is not worked yet". Planned = `EL_REF.prog` (criteria per LS, English and cross-curricular) and the LS numbers in the course column of `EL_REF.grammar` / `.lexicon`. On the site = `EL_STEP_CRITERIA` steps (LS1: activity `criteria`), exam sections and final product rows of `EL_GRADING`, and for grammar/lexicon the tabs in **`shared/js/el-coverage.js` (`EL_COVER[ls].gram/.lex`: start of the map row → ["activity#panel"])**. Worked = tabs ticked in the Time plan (`doneMap`), the exam once its day passed, everything of an LS the group is past. Status ✅ worked · 🕒 not done yet · ✂ only in a dropped path · ⚠ planned, no activity works it · ○ LS not on the site · ➕ worked, not planned. **When an LS is designed, add its `EL_COVER` block** (which tab teaches each grammar and lexicon row it plans); a row with no entry shows ⚠. No Firestore reads.
- **Collecting the data:** the site records completion and first tries automatically; the teacher gives one-click levels only at a few planned moments, on work already read for feedback or a mark. Scores alone never set the degree of a criterion whose indicators describe a task. With two pieces of evidence the later one weighs more; the dashboard suggests, the teacher confirms.
- **Sample exam = optional practice, always open (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I want all the students to be able to practise the sample exam, however I don't want it to appear as homework … always leave the sample exam open but not counting it as homework, to do in class or not done"):** every LS's sample exam has `optional:true` in `EL_CATALOG` (`shared/js/el-catalog.js`, give it to the sample exam of every new LS). `elLpOpen` is always true for it (no open switch, no section locks, no Time plan day, no recall checkpoint first), `elLpLocks` never locks it, `ELWeek.due` gives it no due day (never late, missing or counted in the bonus check), and it is left out of ✅ To do (`elTodoList`), homework (`elHwTargets`, the dashboard's Set as homework), the "next" step and the done/missing counts of the student's learning-path table, which shows it as "💪 Optional · exam practice". Under ✅ To do (board and the floating button) it has its own "🎯 Test yourself (optional)" card with a link, never a to-do item. The dashboard shows "✔ Always open" for it and, on load, rewrites a group's saved `ls/{id}.locked` list once if it still holds it (so `firestore.rules` let students save).
- **Only the final product is marked by the teacher (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “I don't have time to correct answers where I have to read; the idea of this website is that everything gets corrected by itself except for the final product”).** Everything else is self-marked, counted by completion, or given AI feedback (Writing, Speaking, Talk, Reflect, “Com m'ha anat”, “El meu objectiu”: nothing for the teacher to read). The learning-path steps that still need a level (the `moments` of `EL_GRADING` outside the final product: Reading log, Listening chart, Research card / Fact file) are marked from **marking sheets** (`shared/js/el-marking.js`): every hand-in of a group without a level, students as codes (`S-` + the start of the uid, never names), the indicators, and a strict answer format (`EL-MARKS v1 | activity | step | group`, then `code | criterion=level; … | feedback`, then `END`). These learning-path level steps are `atOnce: true` in `EL_GRADING` (Roger, 7 Nov 2026, after 13 Reading logs waited for a Time plan tick: “why is that tick needed?”): each hand-in is ready the day it comes in and is marked at the next Wednesday run, with no tick (students can only hand in what the teacher opened). Moments without `atOnce` (the final product steps) keep the old rule in `ELMark.ready`: ready one week after the step's last session is ticked in the time plan (ticks keep the day: `teacherPlan/{group}.ticks[ls|line] = "YYYY-MM-DD"`). Give every new LS's level steps `atOnce: true`; Claude marks them weekly from the cloud (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: Claude, not ChatGPT); the dialog is the manual backup: it copies or downloads the sheet and reads an answer back (paste or upload), saving the same levels as the level buttons (feedback to students optional). Claude does the same from the cloud with `tools/marking/marks.mjs` (`ready` · `sheet` · `apply --apply [--feedback]`), signed in with the `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` environment secret, as scheduled tasks: marking and corrections **once a week, on Wednesday at about 15:00** (Roger, 1 Nov 2026; it was Monday and Thursday mornings), and `audit` on Friday evenings (❌ work ready before the last Wednesday run and still without a level or correction, `ELMark.late`; ⏰ possible copies waiting 7+ days; push notification to Roger). **Everything else a student writes gets a correction too** (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “if they work more, they can have all the corrections”): writing and speaking panel texts (final version, else the text sent for AI feedback), Talk transcripts and other hand-ins get a correction with no level (`marks.mjs fb-ready` · `fb-sheet` · `fb-apply --apply`; `results/{activity}.replies[step]` + `fbSig[step]`, the version corrected, so every new version is corrected again); **a writing's correction also carries a 5.1 level** (Roger, 21 Nov 2026, after the dashboard's Writing skill and criterion 5.1 stayed empty although 30 writings were corrected): the fb sheet of a writing (mode `writing`, an LS whose `EL_GRADING` has `ind["5.1"]`) asks for `code | 5.1=<level> | <correction>` with the LS's 5.1 indicators and the content file of the task, and `fb-apply` saves `levels[step]["5.1"]` + `marked[step]` with the correction (a flagged copy keeps it in `flags[step].lv`; the dashboard's Accept saves it). The level never changes a mark (no grading block reads learning-path writing levels): it is evidence for 5.1 (`evidence["5.1"]` lists the learning-path writings before the exam and the final product) and the Writing skill of `ELCharts.work`; students never see it as a level. **A speaking's correction also carries a 3.2 level** (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "we said that the speaking should count for the diagram", after a 2ESOB problem report on 🕸 My skills showing Speaking –): Speaking and Talk recordings (fbItems with no mode, step `speak*` / `talk*`) and 🎲 Surprise questions (mode `surprise`) get the LS's 3.x criteria that have indicators (`wCrits(a, mode, step)` in `marks.mjs`; 3.2 in LS2), judged from the Transcript and the Notes from the recording (`LVSP` in `ELMark.fbSheet`); same answer format and saving as writings. It is evidence for 3.2 (`evidence["3.2"]` lists the learning-path speak/talk steps before the final product) and the Speaking skill of `ELCharts.work` (`skillOf` maps every Surprise questions tab to Speaking); no grading block and no page points read it (the engine's `el-levels` only scores `type: 'marked'` panels). Writings and speakings corrected before: `marks.mjs lv-ready` · `lv-sheet` · `lv-apply --apply` (level only, nothing sent); the Reflect card gets a short personal answer instead (no English correction); left out: level moments, the final product, LS1 (kept for next year), and hand-ins the teacher answered by hand. All feedback is written as the teacher, in the first person, never inventing what happened in class (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “you do it, pretending it's me”; 31 Oct: “students think it's me that is correcting”): it never mentions AI, Claude or an automatic correction, is never signed, and is in Catalan for Starter, Base or no route, English for Core, Plus and Pro (the `VOICE` line of every sheet in `el-marking.js`). A hand-in handed in again after the sheet marked it is marked again (`results/{activity}.marked[step]` = when the sheet saved its levels; levels set with the buttons stay); a late hand-in counts from the day it came in. The dashboard's To do shows ❌ *Claude's marking is late* in the same case, so a broken schedule is visible even if nothing runs. New LSs: design these steps so they can be marked from the sheet alone (clear fields, indicators that name what each level needs).
- **Writings and Talk transcripts marked by Claude, from LS3 (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “instead of sending the writing to ChatGPT … the teacher corrects”).** In an LS built on `ls2-engine.js`, a `writing` or `marked` panel with `teacher: true` hands the text in like a step (`state.steps[id]`, no ChatGPT, no points typed by the student); a `marked` panel then takes its points from the teacher level (`EL_DEGREE_MARK`). Add every such panel and every Talk step to the LS's `EL_GRADING` `moments` **with `atOnce: true`** (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: marked in the Wednesday 15:00 run with the rest, as soon as they are handed in; `ELMark.ready` makes them ready at once, no tick needed; late by `ELMark.late` like everything else) (criteria + indicators: CE5 for writing; a Talk transcript is evidence of content, range and interaction only, never pronunciation, so give it a low weight and keep the spoken mark on the final product), and they go into the weekly marking sheets. **Every writing panel hands in to the teacher, LS2 included** (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: “Desa i corregeix amb IA no hauria de sortir ja que ho corregeixes tu … entregar al professor”): `ls2-engine.js` treats a `writing` panel as `teacher: true` unless it says `teacher: false` (button *Entrega al professor* / *Hand it in to my teacher*, a line saying the teacher will correct it); the hand-in carries `w: 1`. Without a level moment it goes to the Wednesday corrections (`fb-*`) in **writing mode**: a short comment, a table of mistakes (their words → correct form → why, at most 8) and their whole text corrected changing only the mistakes, changes in `*…*` (sheet line `comment ## words => right :: why ## … ## TEXT: …`, stored by `ELMark.fbText` as `✗ words → right — why` lines + `✅ text`; `el-corrections.js` shows the table and the text with the changes in bold). The writing log and COPY? apply too: a flagged writing gets `flags[step] = {why, lv: {}, fb, v, f, ty, at}` and no answer; the To do's ⚠️ Possible copy lists every flag of the LS (not only level moments): Accept sends the correction (`fbSig` = that version), Redo sends the note and the next hand-in is corrected again. **The sample exam's `marked` panel gets a table of mistakes from the student's ChatGPT, no mark** (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "they get this kind of feedback (like grades...) and I want them to get this instead" … "I want their chat gpt to do that, not you"): the button opens ChatGPT with a prompt that asks only for a table No. · Criterion (C2 grammar, C3 vocabulary, C4 spelling/punctuation/linkers) · Mistake (struck through) · Correction · Why ([kind] + short reason, in the route's language) and the text corrected; never a mark, level or points (no points box); *My score* counts only the auto-marked sections. **Copies:** every learning-path text box keeps a writing log (`shared/js/el-typing.js`, loaded by `learning-path-support.js`: characters pasted, keys typed, time writing) stored with each hand-in as `ty`; the sheet shows it with the student's route and exam writing marks, and a line ending `| COPY? reason` (pasted, far above their level, translator-like; never “too good” for the Pro route) saves no level: it goes to `results/{activity}.flags[step]` and the To do (⚠️ Possible copy: Accept, or Redo, which sends the student a note). **Students' comments answered by Claude (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: “Claude should answer properly with context”; once a week, Friday at 15:00, since 1 Nov 2026; only comments, the marking is the Wednesday run):** a comment on any hand-in that still needs an answer (`ELMark.open`: not a “no problem” comment, not “No reply needed”, not answered by the teacher; Claude's feedback, `fbBy[step] = "marking"` or `fbSig[step]`, never counts as an answer) goes into the comments sheet, `node tools/marking/marks.mjs comments` (all groups, students as codes, their hand-in, the content file to read for the step). Claude reads the step and its texts and replies in `EL-ANSWERS v1` (`ANSWER`, or `FORYOU` for anything that needs the teacher: handing in again, deadlines, marks, routes, site problems, personal or worrying matters); `marks.mjs answer <file> --apply` saves `answers[step] = {t, q, at}` (a new comment opens it again) or `ask[step] = {why, q, at}` (🙋 in the To do). The student sees Claude's answer as the teacher's, before the correction of the work; the teacher can edit it in the hand-in dialog, and a teacher answer clears `fbBy`/`fbSig`. **Students see the correction where they did the work** (`shared/js/el-corrections.js`, from `el-cloud.js`): a card once per new answer (`students/{uid}.fbSeen`) linking to `page.html#step`, and the answer at the top of that step's tab; never the level (students are not told what is assessed).
- *Transcript part replaced (14 and 16 Nov 2026): speaking and Talk hand in the recording only, next line.* **Speaking and Talk hand in to the teacher too, no ChatGPT, no audio (Roger, 3 Nov 2026: "que no vam dir que els listenings s'enviaven a Claude?"):** in both `ls2-engine.js`, the `speaking` panel's last card is *Entrega al professor* / *Hand it in to my teacher*: it hands in the transcript the student checked as `state.steps[id]` (`f: [{l: 'Transcript', v}]`, writing log as `ty`; at least `p.speakMin` words per route, default 10/15/25/35) and that completes the step (no more draft + final version for speaking). `talk` lost its *Feedback amb IA* button; *Entrega la conversa* is the only way. Both are corrected in the Wednesday run like any other hand-in (`marks.mjs fb-*`, spoken mode: words and grammar, never spelling or pronunciation) and the correction shows at the top of the tab; a better recording handed in again is corrected again. The recording is never saved or sent (Download WAV stays for the student's own use). The 4 ESO LS2 Getting started page (own renderer) does the same (`state.steps.speak`). Left as they were: the sample exam's ChatGPT guide mark, `writing` panels with `teacher: false`, and LS1.
- **Speaking and Talk are recording only, no transcript (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "let's take the transcript option off since Gemini can understand well what they say"; 16 Nov 2026: "Didn't we say that there were not going to be transcripts in the speaking activities?"):** in both `ls2-engine.js` (Speaking and Talk) and the 4 ESO LS2 Getting started, there is no transcript card and no Chrome speech recognition. The student records, listens and hands in: the recording must last about 0.6 s per word of the old word minimum (Speaking at least 8 s, Talk at least 15 s), and the hand-in waits for `el-mic.js` to upload it to the teacher's Drive; the step's fields are Gemini's *Transcript of the recording* and *Notes from the recording* (`rec: 1`), `audio` = the Drive file (🔊 on the dashboard). On the dashboard (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to listen to the audio in the web when I press them and see the feedback"), the 🔊 in a Learning paths cell opens the correction window, which plays the recording inside the page: the school script (v30, action `audio_get`, teacher only, files in the Speaking recordings folder only) sends the file, the browser decodes the WebM/Opus with Web Audio and plays it as WAV in a normal `<audio>` player (iPad Safari cannot play WebM/Opus itself nor open the Drive preview inside another site); "open in Drive" stays as a fallback. Never link a recording only to Drive again. This replaces the transcript hand-in described above. 🎙️ *I can't record* stays. Talk lost its ↺ redo-one-answer buttons (they only re-did the transcript). Speech recognition is still used, feedback only, in the vocabulary *say it* items and in Shadowing. LS1 pages keep their own transcript.
- **The writing of every learning path is scored, from LS2 (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "be careful here, now we will mark the writings"):** "write" is no longer in the catalogue's `skip` for LS2 (each LS2 learning path has one point more; LS1 keeps it not scored), so handing the writing in counts as a step, its criteria show on both dashboards, and it is required for a learning path to be done. Never put `write` in `skip` for a new LS. Results saved with the older points are counted again from the progress on the dashboards (`elScore` in `el-catalog.js`) until the student opens the page again. The teacher dashboard's write cell says where each writing is (`wrState` in `teacher.html`): 📨 Wed (waiting for the Wednesday run) · 📨 new (handed in again after the correction) · ✏️ corrected (this version: `fbSig[step]` = the signature `marks.mjs` computes) · ⚠️ copy? · 🔁 redo · ❌ late (`ELMark.late`) · the level, when the writing is a level moment. Clicking the cell opens it (`wrOpen`, Roger 4 Nov 2026): the correction drawn as the student sees it (`writing()` from `el-corrections.js`), the writing log and any copy flag; ⚠️ copy? opens the To do's Check dialog (`cpOpen`). It uses only data already loaded: no extra reads. *Redo* on a possible copy sends an editable note in the student's language with the reason from the writing log (`cpWhy`: characters pasted, keys typed, minutes); its action is `cpback`, because `cpredo` is the checkpoint's “Let them do it again”. Not “in class” (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: “simply redo”). **Clearly pasted copies are sent back automatically** by the Wednesday run (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: “when this happens and you see it on Wednesdays, you can automatically send redo”): a COPY? line whose writing log is `ELMark.pasted` (≥ 80 % of the text pasted and fewer keys typed than half its length, text of 40+ characters; since 7 Nov 2026 the keys that deleted the pasted surplus, p − n, don't count as typed, because Backspace is counted as a key). **Speaking and Talk transcripts** come from speech recognition, which never pastes: the sheet shows a `[paste log]` when something was pasted and Claude flags it `COPY? pasted, not spoken`, so a clearly pasted transcript is sent back automatically too (Roger, 7 Nov 2026). **Transcript boxes are read-only** (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "they shouldn't be able to write on the script section that is done automatically"): only speech recognition fills them; a wrong or missing transcript means recording again. Every new LS keeps them `readonly` (not the Pro route's typed Talk notes). **Talk keeps one answer per question** (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: a 47 s talk gave one short line): `sp.ans[i]`, shown as numbered lines; recognition restarts when Chrome ends it mid-answer or is still closing the previous one, late results land in their own answer, and *↺ N* plays question N again and replaces only that answer (silence keeps the old one); the hand-in is `question — answer` lines, the minimum counts only the answers is saved as `flags[step] = {…, done: "redo", auto: true}` with the Redo note and its reason (`ELMark.redoNote`) instead of waiting in the To do; never for the Pro route. The cell shows 🔁 redo and the window a *Check it* button to accept it after all. Students get no email at once: the card on their next page, and the Sunday summary lists it as a correction. There, **↩ Send it back** (`wrBack`, Roger 4 Nov 2026) puts a message (Catalan for Starter/Base, English otherwise; ready-made ones for filler letters, too short, fix it) on top of the correction and reopens the writing like Redo (`flags[step] = {done:"redo", back, at}`, `fbSig = "redo"`; cell ↩ sent back until the new hand-in, which the Wednesday run corrects). **↺ Redo on any handed-in step** (`hbBackBox`/`hbBack` in `handIns`, Roger 24 Nov 2026): `flags[step] = {done:"redo", reopen:true, back, at}` + the message on top of `replies[step]`; `elReopenedSteps`/`elReopened` (`el-catalog.js`, called from `elInjectCorrected`) drop the step's done (and doneAt) in memory until `state.steps[step].date` is after `at`, and both `ls2-engine.js` clear it in the student's own progress on `el-result`; `reopen` keeps the older redo flags (copies, writings) counting as before. From the cloud: `node tools/marking/marks.mjs redo <uid or S-code> <activity> <step> --why "…" [--apply]` writes the same flag (plus `why`, `auto: true`) with the route's note; every run uses it at once for a hand-in that is not a real try (Roger, 25 Nov 2026). Not for LS1. LS2 writing and speaking minimums count only real words (`realWords` in `ls2-engine.js`: a letter repeated alone such as "c c c" does not count; "a", "I" and numbers do).
- **A handed-in writing is final and the correction shows the student's own text (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "they should see what they did, and warn them that they won't be able to modify it once handed in … the original text and the corrections with a table: mistakes, corrections and why"):** in both `ls2-engine.js`, a `writing` panel that hands in to the teacher asks for confirmation ("once you hand it in, you can't change it"), then shows the text handed in (`steps[id].f[0].v`) read-only with no hand-in button. It opens again only when the teacher sends it back: Redo on a possible copy (`results.flags[step].done = "redo"` after that hand-in) or Reopen (`results.reopenedAt` after it); `el-corrections.js` sends the activity's result as the `el-result` event. The correction box (`writing()` in `el-corrections.js`) shows the comment, **📝 Your text, as you handed it in** with each mistake underlined in red (`window.elHandedText(step)` from the engine), the table Mistake · Correction · Why, and the corrected text.
- **Degree + indicator under each criterion on the student board (Roger, 10 Nov 2026: "sota cada competència-criteri posar Assoliment + la frase de l'indicador"; "la nota sobre 10 és un percentatge del grau d'assoliment total"):** in 🧩 My competences (`compBox`, My progress) and 📏 My criteria (`summary`, My marks › formative), every criterion with a mark shows the official degree (Assoliment excel·lent / notable / satisfactori / No assoliment) and the indicator sentence of that level from the LS's `EL_GRADING[ls].ind` (`indLine` in `today.html`; Starter and Base read `indCa`, the Catalan version at the end of `el-grading.js`: give every new LS both, in step with its `indicators.md`). The criterion's mark is the same one as 🧩 My competences (`compPieces` → `compOfPieces`, for this LS only in My criteria). Tapping the criterion opens `critPanel` with the criterion's rubric (four levels, each with its share of 10, the student's shaded, `critRubric`). Criteria of an LS without indicators (LS1) show the degree alone.
- **Indicators say how many times (Roger's OK, 10 Nov 2026).** Every LS indicator, and every final product rubric cell, keeps the quality and the help it describes and adds how many times the student must show it, with the cuts of the mark: fewer than half = NA / Level 1, half = AS / Level 2, 7 in 10 = AN / Level 3, 9 in 10 = AE / Level 4 ("3 of my 4 reasons right", "7 in 10 future forms right", "5 of the 6 steps"). Where there is nothing to count, the number of ideas or decisions does the job. Each indicator starts with what the student CAN DO and puts the numbers in brackets as evidence (Roger: "els indicadors han d'explicar què sap fer"). A level needs the number and the quality. Done for both LS2s (`ind`, `indCa`, indicators.md, final-rubric-data.js; Native C2 rows and peer-rated Teamwork left as they were). **New LSs:** write the indicators and rubric cells this way from the start.
- **Degree first, number second (Roger, 10 Nov 2026: "que surti Assoliment notable in a box i el número a la dreta sense ser el protagonista").** On the student board every LS mark (📊 My LS mark so far heading and total row, the LS mark column of 🎓 My progress, the term/year mark, the LS1 LS-mark card) shows the degree in a coloured box (`dgMark` in today.html) with the mark out of 10 small on its right. Part marks stay as numbers. The teacher dashboard's *Assessment criteria in this LS* box and the criterion dialog show each criterion's four indicators (`indRub` in teacher.html; `indCa` first).
- **Scale:** NA 2.5 · AS 5 · AN 7.5 · AE 10 · no evidence 0. Mark to level: under 5 NA · 5–6.9 AS · 7–8.9 AN · 9–10 AE.
- *Replaced (17–19 Nov 2026) by the web exams above; kept as history for LS1.* **Exams (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** students take the LS exam in a **Google Form in quiz mode**, one Form per route (how the Forms are built is still Roger's call; the master copy is a Word document). The repo is public, so **real exam content (texts, scripts, items, keys, audio) is never committed**: it lives in the LS project's files (for example `ls2/exam/`) and Roger's Drive. The Forms are created closed (not accepting responses) and opened in class on the exam day; exam audio is played in class, never published on the site. Everything except the writing is multiple choice or a short gap (Roger, 25 Oct 2026: no open short-sentence answers), so the exam is marked automatically except the writing. Each section of the exam is entered on the dashboard as score / max, plus the level of the writing. The **sample exam** on the site has the same format and points (shown per section and per item), is taken the session before as a diagnostic, and covers all the LS grammar and vocabulary; its writing gets feedback and a guide mark from ChatGPT with the criterion's four levels, always with the warning that ChatGPT can make mistakes and that the teacher marks the real exam.
- **Exam Word format (Roger's model *Exam A · My Real Routine*, 2ESO, 25 Oct 2026):** a cover page in Catalan with the table Apartat · Criteri · Preguntes · Punts, the grade rule (points ÷ 10, 50 passes) and the route choice (00, 0 points); then one section per route that opens with a dark banner naming the exam, the route and its level (4ESO: Insecure · A2/A2+, amb bastides · Safe · B1 orientatiu · Daring · B1/B1+; 2ESO: Insecure · A1+/A2 inicial, amb bastides · Safe · A2 funcional · Daring · A2+/B1 emergent), the name line and the rules; Insecure in Catalan, Safe and Daring in English. Questions are numbered in one sequence (01–35 in the model, 01–43 in LS2 4ESO) with their points in brackets, grouped under section bars with the criterion and sub-parts (for example 1A questions · 1B listening chart); multiple choice has four options A–D with the right letter spread evenly; each gap has a hint line and a «Resposta» / «Answer» line; the writing has its requirements, a plan grid (not marked), ruled lines, a check-before-you-finish list and a word count (Insecure also gets a scaffold box). The answer key is a separate file. The sample exam on the site uses the same numbering and four options.
- **Final products are marked in rounds (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** each rubric row in an LS's `final-rubric-data.js` says when it is marked (`round`: 1 = midway, 2 = after submitting), whether it is a group row (`group: true`) and whether it is judged on the video (`video: true`). **Only the video is marked in groups** (Roger, 25 Oct 2026): every group row is a video row, and everything else (research, email, sources) is marked per student from their own hand-in; the Rubric tab shows the round to students. For each round the teacher needs a marking PDF with every student's answers for that round, each activity followed right below by the rubric row(s) that mark it (video rows: rubric only, one sheet per group). Students paste written pieces that are marked (the email) in their step, not only in Classroom, so they reach the PDF. First case: [4ESO LS2](docs/4eso/ls2-seasons-and-celebrations/final-product-assessment.md#8-marking-in-two-rounds-roger-25-oct-2026).
- **On the site:** each LS with a grading plan has an entry in `shared/js/el-grading.js` (`EL_GRADING[ls]`: block weights, level moments, final product rows, grammar and vocabulary items, the two halves of each other competence, evidence per criterion, indicators). The teacher dashboard builds everything from it, so a new LS adds its entry and its deadlines (`due` in `el-plan.js`); never hard-code weights in `teacher.html`. An entry can hold only some blocks: **LS1 My Real Routine (2ESO and 4ESO, Roger, 25 Oct 2026)** counts only the final exam and the final product (`only: true`; `fp.mark: true` = the final product is the teacher's mark out of max, not levels), its learning paths are practice (`graded:false`), the student board shows only the LS grade for an `only` LS (exam, final product, LS mark and the final product feedback, with no learning paths; since 30 Oct 2026 it keeps its "Go to" button and menu link so students can revise its pages), and the dashboard shows its LS grade first with the learning paths folded below.
- **🔒 Close term and 📏 how daily work counts (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "an option to say close term, so that it only counts what we've done … the rest of the SLP and LP will be included in the next term"; "counts as we said before or only counts if they [have] done it or not … next term I can decide only by pressing a button"):** both in the dashboard's 👥 Students' progress term bar (and 🔒 in each term of 📆 Term and year marks). Close term = `teams/{group}.closed = {t1: day}` per group: every daily-work block (gv, other) that goes in that term is split by the day each step's tab was ticked in the Time plan (doneMap): ticked by the close day counts in the term, the rest in the next one, each side with its share of the block's weight (its steps ÷ the block's steps) and the mark of its own steps (`segSplit`, `S.seg`). Exams and final products stay where `EL_PLAN.report` puts them; each LS's own mark never changes. The board gets the split from `progress.seg` (progSync). 📏 = `teams/{group}.formative = {t2: "done"}` for every group at once: in that term a scored step counts 10 when done and a class game they played counts 10 (missing work = 0 as always: ticked + day passed); default "score" = as before. Both are reversible (reopen / switch back).
- **📬 Students' own notifications and the 🧑‍🎨 mascot in the bar (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "will students see notifications, like you have the exam corrected…"; "can they have the mascot here too? with the numbers"; rule in REQUIREMENTS §3):** `shared/js/el-notify.js` `studentBell({uid, en, ro})` is the 📬 (a mailbox, never the teacher's 🔔: Roger, 25 Nov 2026; `#el-nbell` in `#el-cloud-bar`, list `#el-npanel`); `el-cloud.js` `nbellStart` builds it for students and for the 👁 student view (`ro`: nothing saved, no history) and feeds it: `resultItems` from `myResults` (so `held()` / `soon()` already took out exam results waiting for 📣 Publish and corrections / answers waiting for `fbGo` / `answers.at`) + `myDoc` (`fbSeen`, `examSeen`), using `corrList` (exported from `el-corrections.js`, the same items as its card) and `examSig` (exported from `el-exam-notice.js`); the problem-answer and announcement card loads add their items (`replyItem` / `annItem`) and their OK marks them seen. Problem answers are deleted on OK, so the 📬 keeps a 30-item history in `localStorage el_nt_<uid>` (`log` + `seen`, latest 300 keys; never pruned by what a page has loaded so far). New = not seen on its card/page (`fbSeen` / `examSeen` / OK, `el-fb-seen` and `el-ann-read` events) and not looked at in the list, and less than 21 days old. **No reads of its own** (checked). `el-exam-notice.js` now covers every LS with an exam (not only `EL_GRADING[ls].only`), so LS2 web exams get the card once published.
