Mascot: `mzBar` in `el-cloud.js` loads `el-flashdeck.js` + `el-mascot.js` lazily, draws `ELMascot.badge` + `img` from `el_flash_<uid>` (the 👁 view reads `students/{uid}/flashcards/all` once, kept 30 min in `sessionStorage el_mz_as_<uid>`), on every page, the board too (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "the mascot should be everywhere"), never on `exam.html`, `recall-checkpoint*.html`, `sample-exam.html` or `window.EL_EXAM` (`noCorner`, same for the 📬). The teacher's own bar has the mascot too (his own `el_flash_<uid>`). **Flashcards saves (25 Nov 2026, students' ⚡ stuck at 0):** `el-flashdeck.js` `mount` saves only when something was studied (`dirty`), and every save first merges the stored copy (`sync` = `merge(o.load(), P)`), as does a tab becoming visible again and `reload()`, so a tab left open on an old copy never wipes cards studied in another tab (the old code saved the whole in-memory copy on every `visibilitychange`); practice.html `fdSave` re-reads the account's copy before writing when its last read is over 5 minutes old. `el-cloud.js` `mzSync(uid)` merges `students/{uid}/flashcards/all` into `el_flash_<uid>` at most every 30 minutes per browser (`el_flash_chk_<uid>`) and fires `el-flash-sync` (today.html redraws its mascot), so the ⚡ counts cards studied on another device. On phones the static bar puts the name on its own line (`.elcb-nm`) and keeps 76 px under it so the floating 📓 / ✅ buttons never cover it. Check: `node tools/notify/check.mjs` (real `el-cloud.js`, a fake in-memory Firebase and an invented student: lists, held items hidden, the red number, no extra reads, mascot places, the 👁 view writes nothing; `SHOTS=dir` saves pictures). **Teacher's mascot on a phone** (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "On my cellphone I want this to be at the top right!"): on the dashboard (`body.tph`, ≤640 px) the bar sits at the end of the page, so `el-phone.css` lifts `#el-cloud-bar .elcb-mz` out of it with `position:absolute` against `body` (set `position:relative` there) to the top right, 60 px with the ⚡ badge at its bottom right as on the board, and gives `#loadbar`, `#msg` and `#main>.ph` 68 px of right padding; computers unchanged. `teacher.html` loads `el-phone.css?v=…` (bump it when the file changes).
- **📐 Levels NA–AE from term 2 (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "all the new activities we do from now on will have an assessment NA to AE"; "students will have 2 weeks to turn in, after that they will have a NA"; "Go apply and merge"; rule in REQUIREMENTS §8):** a third 📏 setting, `teams/{group}.formative = {t2: "levels", from: "2026-11-30"}` (📐 levels NA–AE in the term bar; written for every group at once, `from` defaults to `EL_LV_FROM`). `stepMode(id, k, g)` returns `"levels"` only for a step whose tab started on or after `from` (`lvStart`: the earliest of the Time plan agenda class day, the homework day (`elHandInStarts`, el-points.js, the same sources as `elDueBefore`) and the tick day (`stepDay`); a 📖 book chapter starts on its own Book day); earlier tabs of the term count as `"score"`. In `"levels"`: `firstV` gives the level of the first try (recall); `time` parts go through `lvTime` → `lvV` (a step's level: `stepLv` = the English criteria levels of `results.levels[step]`, else the level of `last` (else `tries`) score via `elPctLevel`; not done: left out until `elHandInBy(start, redo at)` = 14 days with `EL_TIMETABLE.off` days skipped, ↺ Redo (`flags[step].done === "redo"`) gives 7 more from its `at`; then 0 (`S.dueZero`) or null; done after the deadline: 0; done with no level yet: left out), averaged per skill (`ELCharts.skillOf`) before the part's average; `otherCrit` auto steps use `lvV` too. `lvChip` shows the level (or ⏳ days left, NA ✗, NA · late, ⌛ level) under each cell of the Learning paths and activities table. Beltran: `wCrits` gives Shadowing hand-ins (fbItems mode `shadow`) a 3.2 level with `ELMark.SHADOW_IND` (how close to the model); the level sheet asks `| REDO? reason` for a hand-in that is not a real try, and `marks.mjs apply` saves its NA and sends it back like the dashboard's ↺ Redo (`flags[step] = {done: "redo", reopen: true, back, why, at, auto: true}`, the note in `replies[step]`). From 30 Nov the weekday run applies level sheets with `--feedback`, so students read the correction with its level. Students: `el-corrections.js` shows the degree box (`lvBox`, English + Catalan in brackets, Catalan only for Starter) above each correction marked on or after `EL_LV_FROM`; `today.html` shows "hand it in by …" (`lvBy`/`lvTag`) under each tab of ✅ To do, the rule (`lvRule`) in My LS mark so far, and a 📐 New chip on the home for the first two weeks until My marks is opened (`el_lv_seen`). Demo: `EL_DEMO_LV=1 node tools/site-video/teacher-demo.mjs --serve` (levels on in term 1, 4 ESO A LP1–LP3 on the agenda on 5 Nov).
- **Exam by criterion (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** an exam section may carry its criteria, `[key, name, max, [criteria]]`, as on the exam's cover (Apartat · Criteri · Punts). `elExamByCrit` (el-grading.js) sums each criterion's sections, so a section shared by two criteria (vocabulary: 4.1 and 5.1) counts in both; the exam mark stays all points ÷ 10 (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: “leave the exam as it is”). **Use of English is reading and writing (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: “even though students see that section as Use of English … the competences are reading and writing”):** vocabulary and grammar sections count for reading and writing alike (LS2: both 4.1 and 5.1); LS1 keeps the criteria printed on its exam covers. The same holds in the learning paths (Roger, same day): a vocabulary or grammar step with no criteria of its own (recall, words, cards, practice, grammar, mixed, vocab) counts for 4.1 and 5.1 (end of `el-catalog.js`), so the whole-year view's “how much it is worked” counts it as reading and writing. **The final product counts 25 % in every LS** (Roger, 30 Oct 2026; LS1: exam 75 · final product 25, it was 50/50). The dashboard's Exam marks table and a student's LS grade dialog show a mark /10 per criterion, and the student board (LS1 view) shows "Your exam by criteria". 4ESO LS1 splits the writing into its rubric rows (task 8, grammar 4, vocabulary 4 → 5.1; organisation 4 → 5.2). 2ESO LS1 follows its exam cover per item type: vocabulary choose 16 → 4.1, complete 12 → 5.1; grammar choose 14 → 5.1 and 4.1, complete 18 → 5.1; writing as in 4ESO. Typed marks are kept between 0 and the section maximum and the criteria, exam mark and LS mark update as they are typed (`liveExam`); saving refuses a score above its maximum. Typed exam marks save by themselves when the box is left (`autoSaveExam`, "not saved yet" / "✓ saved" next to the name; leaving the page with unsaved marks asks first): on 30 Oct 2026 marks typed but never saved showed on the dashboard while the student board said "no mark yet". The student board only reads saved marks. Marks from a spreadsheet: `tools/exam-marks/import-exam-marks.mjs <csv> <ls> [--apply]` on Roger's PC (CSV outside the repo, `email` + section keys). **A part not done is left out, not 0 (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: the 2ESO students who sat LS1 late had no listening, “I won't take it into account”):** a `-` cell in the CSV writes `sections[k] = {excused: true, max}`; the dashboard leaves that part out even with “missing work counts 0” on (the box shows “left out”), the student board says “Not done: it doesn't count”, the exam mark is the other parts' points rescaled to 10, and the “results are ready” card is not held back by it. Typing a score in that box replaces it. Both import scripts (`exam-marks`, `exam-report`) sign in with `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` when it is set (cloud), else the Firebase CLI login. Exam version (Roger, 30 Oct 2026): `.version` (insecure/safe/daring) in `results/{ls}__assessment` says which exam version the student took; the Exam marks table and the grade dialog have a selector (saves on change), the LS grade table shows it under the exam mark, and the import takes an optional `version` column. The Exam marks table also shows the current route next to the version, with ⚠️ when they differ (also in the LS grade table and the grade dialog). **The harder version counts more (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: LS1 “students who took the daring exam … should be rewarded, since it was more difficult … count a 20 % more”):** `exam.boost = {daring: 1.2}` in EL_GRADING (LS1, both courses). The saved points never change; `elExamBoost(exam, assessment)` gives the student's factor and `elBoosted(mark, f)` multiplies every mark worked out from the points (exam mark, each part, each criterion, so the LS mark and the whole-year/term marks) and caps it at 10. Dashboard and board say “× 1.2”; the board shows a ⭐ line with the sum. Routes are never moved by it: the routes tool suggests from the raw points (`examMark().raw`). Changing a student's exam version changes the factor at once.
- **The board is the LS report (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "the idea is that I don't need to make [the LS1 reports] again since all the info could be on their student board"):** `today.html` has a big LS picker card (`lsCard`) with three tabs per LS: 🗓 My week (week plan, homework, summary, practice; hidden for an `only` LS with no plan), 📈 My formative assessment (learning-path grids and "Summary of my criteria", in an orange frame so it stands out; an `only` LS shows its final product) and 📝 My exams (`examView`; the exam is shown only there). Hashes `#week`, `#formative`, `#exam` open a tab (the exam notice links `?ls=…#exam`). My exams: LS grade (`only` LSs: exam + final product + LS mark; other LSs: exam mark and its weight, the rest given by the teacher), 1 results per part, 2 exam by criteria, then the report from `results/{ls}__assessment .report` = `{model, lang, questions:[{q,part,pts,got,text,mine,right,why,lp:{t,href}}], writing:{original, corrected (changes in *…*), rubric:[{name,score,max}] (Firestore allows no array in an array; the old [name,score,max] form is still read), feedback, errors:[{type,orig,fix,why}], note}, review:[{text,lp}]}`: 3 questions to review, 4 the writing (rubric, comment, Mistake / Correction / Why table as in `el-corrections.js`, own text next to the corrected one; `note` = a teacher's note such as the reliability check), 5 what to review with links. Before the exam is marked the tab says so and gives the exam day. Report texts are in the student's language at the exam (Catalan for Insecure, English otherwise). LS1: `tools/exam-report/build-ls1-reports.py {4eso|4eso-six|2eso} <workbook> <out.json> [why-en.json]` turns Roger's marked workbooks (the ones his Apps Script made PDFs from) into `{email: report}`; `node tools/exam-report/import-exam-report.mjs <json> <ls> [--apply]` writes only `.report` (dry run without --apply; on Roger's computer, JSON outside the repo). Any later exam pipeline only needs to write the same `.report`. **Whole exam, question by question (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "students … want to see what they did well too", with the reading text):** a report with `full: true` holds every question of the student's own exam (model + route): the right ones as `{q,part,pts,got,text,mine,ok:true}` (no why/lp), each question with the paper's choices `opts:["A. …",…]` and `hint`, plus `exam:{listening:{about,text (transcript, if any)},reading:{title,body},writing:{title,task,points}}`; box 3 becomes "Your exam, question by question" (`examWhole`): parts with their text (reading always shown, transcript in a fold, writing task), green/red/amber cards with the choices (theirs and the right one marked), why + review link on the lost ones, and an All / Right / To review filter (CSS `:has`, no state). Reports without `full` keep the old table. Built with `build-ls1-reports.py … --exam exam-texts.json` (exam texts parsed from the Word papers in Drive, kept in project files, never in this public repo), written with `node tools/exam-report/add-full-exam.mjs <json> <ls> [--apply]`, which adds only the right questions, opts/hint and `exam` to the report already on the board and skips a student whose questions to review differ (sign-in: `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` or the Firebase CLI). Future web exams should store the same shape. **Writing rubric explained (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "aquí falta explicar bé els criteris d'avaluació"):** rows named `C1`–`C4` (task and content, grammar, vocabulary, organisation and cohesion) are drawn as cards (`WRUB` in `today.html`), in the board's language whatever the report's: what the row checks, its official criterion (from the exam sections `wtask`/`wgrammar`/`wvocab`/`worg` in `EL_GRADING`), four levels shown as the row's mark out of 4 (Excellent 4/4, Good 3/4, Needs work 2/4, Not enough 1/4; Roger 2 Nov 2026: "hauria de ser 3/4 i si vols la ponderació") with a ⚖️ weight line (the row's % of the writing and the points it gives); the level comes from the points, each level showing its point range (Excellent = full marks only, Good from ¾ of the points, Needs work from ⅜: out of 8 = 8 · 6–7 · 3–5 · 0–2, out of 4 = 4 · 3 · 2 · 0–1; `wCuts`; Roger 2 Nov 2026: 7/8 as Excellent made no sense) with the student's marked, and the numbers of the mistakes of that row (`WTYPE`, by the first word of the mistake type; mixed types get none; C1 points to the teacher's comment). Other row names are shown as they come. A later exam keeps this by naming its writing rows C1–C4.
- **Exam reports in the teacher dashboard (Roger, 14 Nov 2026: "when seeing the reports of the exams, I don't want to go to student's view, however I want to see the same report"; "a section with only exams … in all LS similar to Final products but with students reports"):** the report is never redrawn in `teacher.html`: it shows `today.html?embed=exam&student=…&ls=…[&lang=ca|en]` in a frame. In that mode the board takes the student's data from the dashboard (`parent.elExamEmbed`: results, levels and LS info already loaded, so no Firestore read, no sign-in and no student view), runs as demo mode and draws only `examView` of that LS (links open in a new tab; Esc closes the dashboard's overlay). The 📝 Exam mark in 👥 Students' progress and 🔍 › 📄 open it on top of the dashboard (`xrOpen`: ‹ › go through the group, 🌐 switches Catalan/English); the group tile **📝 Exams** (next to 🎬 Final product, any LS with an exam) lists each student's version, marking (not yet · n of m parts · marked; an `excused` part is "left out"), mark and report (Full = question by question, To review), with the report on the right as the final product's side panel. A new student in the same frame uses `location.replace`, so the browser's Back stays in the dashboard. Any change to the student's exam report shows in both places.
- **Simple student board and learning paths (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "the menus have little information and when you go to a place you can find the info that you need"; students use Chromebooks, so design for ~1366×768 first):** `today.html` home shows only a greeting (first name, group · LS · days to the exam), red alerts (missing activities, unread announcements), one big **Continue** card (`nextFor`: the first open, not done, not dropped activity in plan order, or a missing checkpoint) and four tiles: 🏠 Homework `#homework` (work count, late list, week plan), 📊 My marks `#marks` (LS chips + the formative / final product / exams tabs above), 📚 Learning Situations `#units` (LS cards, level, study and practice) and ❓ Help `#help` (the picture guide, Problem/Idea, hard reload). Each place has a ‹ Home back link; `#news` holds announcements; old hashes `#formative`, `#final`, `#exam` open My marks and `#week` opens Homework. The LS2 learning-path page (`ls2-engine.js`, both courses) folds the warm-up note and "how it works" into one ⓘ fold, puts the study guide next to it, and on wide screens lists the steps on the left (numbers, ✓ when done, NEXT on the first one not done). New student screens follow the same rule: little on the menu, the details inside each place. LS1 pages are unchanged. **Phone view (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "so that students can use it on their cellphones"):** at ≤640px the board hides the top menu and shows a fixed bottom bar (`bnav`: Home, Homework, Marks, LS, Help, with the missing count), the home is compact, the learning-paths table becomes one card per path, and the LS place carries the links the top menu had (My course, Practise, English Lab); on LS2 learning paths the top bar scrolls away and the strip of steps sticks to the top of the screen, keeps the open step in view and jumps to the start of the step when tapped. Check new student screens at 390×844 too. **Study on your phone (Roger, 6 Nov 2026):** a dismissible card on the board home ("Study on your phone too", hidden once closed via `localStorage.el_phone_seen` and when opened from the home screen) and in Help leads to `#phone`: the board and a learning path on a phone, a QR code to `today.html` (`shared/img/phone/qr-board.svg`, hidden on phones), Android Chrome and iPhone Safari step pictures in Catalan and English (`shared/img/phone/*.webp`, the device's own steps open first) and Chrome's Install button when the browser offers it (`beforeinstallprompt`). If the board changes a lot, retake the pictures (they are made, not drawn by hand: `node tools/phone-guide/board.mjs C && node tools/phone-guide/lp.mjs C && node tools/phone-guide/make.mjs C`, with C a scratch folder; real phone-size screens of the board and a learning path in demo mode with the invented sample students, inside drawn phones with Chrome's and Safari's bars; iPhone = iOS 26 Safari, ••• → Share → Add to Home Screen → Add, with a line for older iPhones; bump `?v=` in `phoneBox` after a retake; 7 Nov 2026).
- **The whole site as an app (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "I want everything to look like an app for them even though they connect through a Chromebook"; "these things should look like buttons"; "it's not my activities but my dashboard"):** the board is called **My dashboard / El meu tauler** everywhere (menus, ‹ back links, `menuLink` in `el-cloud.js`, page title); the "📋 My activities" box on it keeps its name. Every menu item is a button with an icon (`shared/css/el-app.css`: `.side .nv` with `.ic` + `.lb` spans; the board, the LS home via `ls-home.css`, English Lab). `shared/js/el-app.js` (loaded by `el-cloud.js` on every page, and at once by `ls2-engine.js`) gives every other student page the same menu, 🏠 My dashboard · 📚 LSn (the page's LS, else the group's by `ELWeek.now`) · 🗂 All LS · 🧪 English Lab · 🎯 Practise · ❓ Help (teacher: 📊 Dashboard, no Help): on a computer the LS home's side menu on the left (`.elrail`, 240 px: brand + the same wide `.nv` buttons with labels and ›, the LS by its title; Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "the format should be the same (buttons)"; 641–980 px a strip of icon buttons, 92 px) on learning paths (LS2 on: every page with the engine's `#sessionJump`), course pages and vocabulary games; English Lab and Practise keep their side menu, rebuilt as buttons (`.elside`); a phone gets the bottom bar (`.elbar`, ≤640 px; the account bar moves to the page end). On learning paths the dark top bar becomes a light one (‹ LS button, path selector) and, on a computer (≥1000 px; Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "use a column on the left with info of the LP, and it must be super easy to understand"), the page is two columns: on the left (`.ellpside`, sticky, scrolls on its own) the path's title and what it is, Your progress (level and steps done), the teacher's card (class game, level buttons, and the sections bar `el-cloud.js` draws into `.eltsec-host`: sections open, pull up, full screen, locked warning; its long help text and the Teacher mode strip are hidden), the steps, then How it works, the study guide and My goal; the step is on the right. The grid starts right after the menu (not centred, max 1600 px) and nothing in the column may be wider than it (`overflow-x:hidden`, the open study guide in one column; Roger, 7 Nov 2026: the column slid under the menu after a sideways trackpad swipe, and a wide screen left a big gap). `lpLayout` in `el-app.js` moves the hero pieces once and the engine's `.lp-top`, goal card and steps nav after every redraw of `#lp`; a phone and narrow windows keep the page's own layout. A student's single level card goes. A learning path page that does not use the engine (4 ESO LS2 Getting started) must have the same parts in `main#lp`: `.lp-top` (How it works fold + study guide), then the goal `.plan-card` right after it, then `.lp-body` with `nav.tabs` (steps as `.tn` number + `.tl` label, `done`/`next` classes) and the panels; otherwise it keeps the old hero and tab layout (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "this doesn't match the new style"). Course pages and English Lab keep the public look when signed out. **The home page (`index.html`) is only a welcome page to sign in (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "it makes no sense that this menu exists since we all go to the dashboards. Maybe, we should have a welcome page to sign in"):** no menu and no account chip for a signed-out visitor: what the app is (Spanish and Catalan curriculum, learning situations, competences, structured grammar and vocabulary), one Sign in button and a "What's inside" list of plain one-sentence bullets (Roger, 7 Nov 2026, about the cards: "make a list of sentences I don't want this"), **no cards, icons, screenshots or pictures of the app** (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I don't want anybody to copy my work so maybe we should not show images in the homepage, only a list of the main characteristics for now"; the pictures of PR #352 were removed; keep the sentences true when features change; never mention the full screen mode, in class or in exams, Roger: "don't reveal the full screen mode, I think this is unique and a good trick"); whoever is signed in goes to their dashboard (teacher `teacher.html`, students and the test account `today.html`), at once from `el_who` in the page head and again once Firebase answers. Signed out on `teacher.html` or `today.html` (sign-in ended, a bookmark) goes back to this welcome page (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "this should be the page"); `el-cloud.js` removes `el_who` before `onUser` runs, so there is no loop. Only the teacher's and the test account's Courses and Learning Situations lists stay (`index.html#courses`, `#learning`, el-app.js mode `home`: 📊 Dashboard · 🏫 Courses · 📚 Learning Situations · 🧪 English Lab · 🎯 Practise). The other LS1 pages, the board and LS homes (their own full menu), class games and model slideshows are left out. **LS1 learning paths look like LS2 (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "Make LP and SLP of the LS1 look like the LS2"):** the 12 LS1 pages with the path selector (both courses, final product included) load `shared/js/ls1-lp.js` right after `section-next.js` (and el-app.css, el-route-names.js, el-catalog.js + el-app.js like LS2, `html.elgate`). It turns `main.wrap` into `main#lp` with `.lp-top` (hero extras: warm note, class slides link, sample slides and rubric), `.lp-body` > `nav.tabs` (`.tn` number + `.tl` label, rewrapped when a page rewrites a tab for the route; the page's own number, a tab without one gets a star) + `.lp-panels`, and marks steps done / NEXT from the page's own `sectionNext` done() functions. Content, answers and saving are unchanged; el-app.js treats them as mode lp (back button "LS1 · My Real Routine"). No Firestore reads. A new student page gets the menu by matching one of the modes in `el-app.js`. **All Learning Situations (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "aquest no m'agrada"):** for a signed-in user the course page (`2eso/`, `4eso/`) is replaced by `allLs` in `el-app.js`: "My Learning Situations", one row per LS the group can open (the one the group is doing now first and highlighted, chips Now / Open / Closed · to review, Hidden for the teacher), each with its number, what it is about (the page's own card text) and one Open button; statuses come from the cached `ELCloud.lsNow`. **English Lab by level, not by course (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "hauria d'estar per nivells no per cursos"; he chose one ladder):** `elLadder()` in `el-catalog.js` groups every [course, route] by its CEFR level from `EL_CEFR` (A1 = 2 ESO Starter + 4 ESO Starter, A1+, A2 = 2 ESO Core + 4 ESO Base, A2+, B1, B1+, C2 = 4 ESO Pro). `lab/index.html` opens a student straight on their level ("Your level: A2", no course or level buttons) and shows only their own course's games (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "here the same only their course"; no "You also get the games of 4 ESO" line); the teacher picks a step (A1 … C2) and sees every course of it. **`lab/practice.html` is own course only (Roger, 7 Nov 2026, thread "Check Practise only shows student's course": the other course's topics were "a good idea but too much information"):** a student gets only their own course's LSs and opened paths at their route (no 4 ESO Base topics, slides or words for a 2 ESO Core student); the teacher picks one course + route at a time (buttons "A2 · 2 ESO Core", "A2 · 4 ESO Base"…), and English Lab's `?level=A2` opens that step's first pair. A new course or route joins the ladder through `EL_CEFR` alone.
- **The phone is its own design (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "Al chrome es veu molt bé i no vull cap canvi però la visió del mòbil necessita una gran remodelació … no ha de tenir exactament el mateix que la visió web però sí la mateixa funcionalitat"):** everything for phones lives in `shared/css/el-phone.css` (imported by `el-app.css`), and **every rule that changes a page is inside `@media (max-width:640px)`**: a computer or a Chromebook (≥641 px) must stay pixel-identical (compare full-page screenshots at 1366×768, 1440×900 and 800 px before and after, in demo mode). Students on a phone have ONE bottom tab bar on every page, `elPhoneTabs()` in `el-catalog.js` (Home · To do · LSn · Practise · Me, SVG icons, To do badge kept in localStorage `el_ptabs` by the board, no reads); it replaces the old bars (`#bnav`, `.elbar`). Me = the board's `#me` place (`meBox` in `today.html`: name, level with ✋ Ask for a level change, marks, formative, Games and theory, LSs, announcements, books, grammar, games, help, phone, ⚠️ Problem / 💡 Idea). To do and Homework share the To do tab (switch `.pseg`); Practise and Games share the Practise tab (switch `.elpseg`). Inputs are 16 px on a phone so iPhone Safari never zooms. A learning path on a phone is one screen for the step (`lpPhone()` in `el-app.js`, section 7 of `el-phone.css`): a slim bar (‹ back to the LS, the path's name with LPn · level · progress, ⓘ, ⋯ Problem / Idea), the steps as chips under it, the step's "Mark section as done" button as a bar at the bottom, relabelled "Next step ›" / "Finish and continue ›" (it records nothing: it only opens the next step once every activity is checked; no manual ticks), no tab bar; ⓘ (`body.elpinfo`) shows the About view (the hero, how it works, the study guide, my goal, the other paths). It only hides and shows, never moves anything, because the engine redraws `#lp` on every route change. The home shows only today's things (Continue, To do, Homework, the paths still left); the LS page starts with Continue. The teacher dashboard on a phone (`phoneBar()` in `teacher.html`, which links `el-phone.css`; section 8): the same tab bar, Home · Groups · Corrections · Messages · More, instead of the row of menu buttons; a 2A · 2B · 4A · 4B switch at the top of a group's pages (the place stays); More is a sheet with the rest of the menu. Its buttons reuse the dashboard's own `data-do` actions. A new student page gets the tab bar by calling `elPhoneTabs`; a new phone rule goes in `el-phone.css`, never in a desktop rule.
- **On an LS the student's side menu is a strip of icons (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "when students are on the LS, to see better the screen, I want the menu to hide and only show the emoji or logos of each side. If the mouse goes on the section the menu gets bigger"):** on a computer (≥981 px) a student (and the teacher's student view) on an LS home (`el-ls-home.js`, `aside.side`) or its learning paths and vocabulary games (`el-app.js`, `.elrail`) gets `body.elmini`: a 72 px strip with ☰ and the icons (LS1, LS2 in their boxes), which opens over the page to 300 px with the labels when the mouse rests on it (0.15 s pause; closes 0.3 s after the mouse leaves), with a tap on ☰ (touch screens; a tap outside or Esc closes it) or with the keyboard. Buttons keep their height open and closed. `elMiniRail` in `el-catalog.js`, CSS in `el-app.css` section 3. The board, course pages, English Lab, Practise, the teacher and testers keep the wide menu; narrower windows and phones are unchanged. Every LS, LS1's home too (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "the menu should collapse in every LS"; menu only, LS1 content unchanged; LS1 learning paths have no side menu).
- **Encouraging sentences (Roger, 6 Nov 2026):** `shared/js/el-cheer.js` (no Firestore reads, the pick comes from the date). The dashboard home shows one under "Hi, Roger" (same all day, new each day; Monday, Wednesday, Friday and the weekend start with their own line; ↻ shows another). The student board greeting is "Hi Pau, " + a sentence from `STUDENT` (Catalan for Starter/Base, English for Core/Plus): each student starts at their own place (hash of email or name) and moves one a day, so no repeats until the whole list has come round. Add sentences at the end of a list; student ones short, A2-friendly and gender-neutral in Catalan.
- **📝 Exam practice (Roger, 19 Nov 2026: "un botó a l'esquerra que sigui practica per l'examen i que sigui el sample exam, que vegin LS, entrin a cada una i allà ha d'haver el o els samples exams"):** a side-menu item under 📊 My marks (`elStudentMenu`, so on every student page) opens the board's `#exam-practice` place (`examPrep` in `today.html`): the student's LSs as cards, newest first; one opens its practice exams (`optional:true` in `EL_CATALOG`, i.e. every sample exam) with Start / Continue and parts done. A new LS's sample exam shows up there by itself. My marks never says how many activities are "not late yet" (Roger: students must not know).
- **📊 My marks on ONE screen (Roger, 19 Nov 2026: "aquesta secció té molta informació i molt desordenada, a més això no crec que ni s'ho mirin"):** `today.html#marks` (`marksHome`) shows only: the LS mark so far (`lsMarks`, `dgMark`) with one bar split by weight and its parts as rows (📚 Everyday work, 🎬 Final product, 📝 Exam, 🎯 My checkpoints), each row opening its details (`S.tab` "daily" = `progressBox`, "fp", "exam", "cp"; ‹ My marks goes back; hashes #work, #final, #exam, #checkpoints); ⏰ the late activities with Do it (`workData`, max 5, the rest in ✅ To do; on a phone this card comes first; each late tab name is a link to its tab and Do it goes to the first tab still to do: `leftTabs` / `lateHref` / `leftLinks` → `tabHref(a, step)`, also on the board's `lateBox`. Before 22 Nov 2026 Do it went to `a.path`, the path opened on tab 1 (done ✓) and a student thought the late tabs were done) or 🎉 All up to date; 🏆 points as one line (`pointsLine`, rules folded). No tab bar, no learning-paths table (the road on the board shows done/missing) and no criteria on this page (🎓 My progress › 🧩 My competences has them). Keep it one Chromebook screen: new things go inside a part's details, not on the summary. Reads nothing new from Firestore.
- **📊 My marks as visual cards (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "this section would be better with a style like this", pointing at ⓘ About this LS):** `today.html#marks` (My activities, My points, the 📈 tab's learning paths, LS mark so far and criteria) uses `fvHead`/`fvStat`/`fvPart` and the `.fv-*` CSS: a heading with an icon, soft cards with a coloured top border and a big number, missing activities as numbered cards with Do it now, the three parts of the LS mark as cards joined by +, criteria as competence-coloured cards (tap = `critPanel`). Competence colours are the About this LS ones (CE1 teal, CE2 blue, CE3 orange, CE4 green, CE5 purple, CE6 amber, CD indigo, CC pink), also on the board's `.pill`; course colour from `body[data-course]`. Since 7 Nov 2026 ("make sure all LS's sections like this have the same style") the 🎬 Final product tab (mark/share/steps cards, steps as cards, rubric parts as weight cards) and the 📝 Exams tab (LS1: exam + final product = LS mark cards; results per part as cards; exam by criteria as competence cards; what to review as numbered cards) use it too, for every LS (LS1 included: board only, LS1 pages untouched). Same data as before; keep this style for new boxes on the board.

Never in class (`liveTick` does that) or in the demo. The 🔄 buttons stay.

Stored as `ls/{id}.push[group] = {a, p, pn, t, at}` (activity, panel id or "", tab name, label from `elLpLabel`, ms) or null; no rules change (ls is teacher-written). Pulling up a locked path or tab opens it for the group. a student already on that path is never moved or reloaded (Roger, 3 Nov 2026), only gets a bar "Your teacher is on the tab: … Go there" when the teacher chose another tab; never out of a recall checkpoint, the sample exam or a followed presentation (`?live=`). **🔒 Kept there until released (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: "once I pull students in a section or LP, they mustn't be able to go to another part of the website until I say so"):** every pull-up carries `lock: true`; while it lasts (until "🔓 Release them", which sets `go` and `push` to null, or `LOCK_MS` = 70 minutes after it), any other page that loads `el-cloud.js` sends the student back (2-second notice after the first time), links leaving the path are blocked (PDF/audio/video files allowed) and, with a tab chosen, the other tabs too except the one the student was already on (`lockFrom`); a bar on the path says they are kept there; release shows "Your teacher has let you go". **Released at the end of the class (Roger, 5 Nov 2026: "when I finish a class, this should be released"):** a pull-up made during the group's class by `EL_TIMETABLE` (from 5 minutes before it starts) carries `until` = the end of that class (`elClassEnd` in `el-plan.js`); then "make the selected group go full screen" from the top right of the dashboard, "it should affect all the website"):** a "⛶ Full screen <group>: on/off" button at the top right of the dashboard (`#fsctl`, `fsDraw`, for the group tab open) and in the teacher bar of the learning path page (`fsRow`) writes `live/{group}.fs` = {on, at, until} (until = end of the class by `elClassEnd`, else 70 minutes) or null; it does not need a pull-up. Every page that loads `el-cloud.js` (gamePush already listens to `live/{group}`) runs `fsCheck`: a cover until the student taps (browsers only go full screen after a tap), then Esc locked (Keyboard Lock: only holding Esc leaves on Chrome/Chromebooks); leaving full screen brings the cover back and writes `live/{group}/fsLeft/{email}` = {name, at, pull: fs.at, n} (firestore.rules: own doc, teacher reads); moving to another page of the site (link, pull-up, reload: `beforeunload`/`pagehide`) is not counted. **🖥 One tap per class (Roger, 16 Nov 2026):** a browser leaves full screen whenever the whole tab changes page, so while the class full screen is on a student's page reopens itself inside `class.html` (one window-sized frame `#el-shell`, `window.EL_SHELL`; no Firebase, no reads; `?u=` the page so a reload reopens it) and the frame moves from page to page (links, pull-up, game and back) while `class.html` stays in full screen. el-cloud.js `shellEnter`/`shellLeave`, `FW` = the window whose full screen counts; play.js does the same. The page leaves the frame (one reload) when the group's full screen ends, for a recall checkpoint or the sample exam (their own full screen), or for a non-student. **↩ Back at the first class page (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "sometimes when they go back they exit fullscreen, but not on purpose"):** the pages before `class.html` are top-level entries, so the browser's back from the first page in the frame loaded one of them as a new page and the full screen ended. `class.html` now pushes one entry of its own (`history.state.elShell`) once it is in full screen (on the tap, and again on a tap or key in the frame if missing; `sync`'s replaceState keeps that state); a `popstate` to an entry below it pushes it back at once and, the first time, opens `?b=` (the page the student came from: `document.referrer`, passed by `shellEnter`) inside the frame with a normal navigation (never `location.replace`, which gives the two entries different frame pages, and a push during that frame traversal is lost). Back between pages of the frame was always kept. **Game end in class mode:** ■ End and Finish on both boards write `cmBack` too (the students go back to the class page); the player also lets them go after the game has been off for 10 s (board closed or an old board). The panel is fixed at the top right on every dashboard tab and while scrolling (the group tab open, else the last group shown or the group in class), and on the LP page `#el-fsfloat` while it is on (Roger, 5 Nov 2026: "fixed everywhere I go and always visible … even if I scroll down"); who left stays listed after they go back. Switching off or the end of the class leaves full screen. A real no-exit lock needs Google Admin on managed Chromebooks; a website cannot do it. `el-checkpoint.js` `goFull` locks Esc also when the page is already in full screen. Checkpoints, the sample exam and followed presentations stay exempt. **Pull-up, class games and 📴 not connected (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "when they are playing a game, I want them to be in full screen too; if I pull them they should be in full screen. The class game player (`lab/game/play.js`) is covered until the student taps ("Tap to join the game"), then full screen with Esc locked, from the lobby to the last question; leaving it writes `fsLeft` (pull = `fs.at` when the group's full screen is on, else the game's `sid`); `el-cloud.js` sends `&ca=1|0` with the redirect (Catalan only for Starter/Base; without it the player shows both). `host.js` writes `live/{group}` with `mergeFields` (only the game's fields; q/th/ok deleted) so `go` and `fs` survive a game. Presence: while full screen is on (every `el-cloud.js` page, `hereBeat`) or a game is on (the player), students write `live/{group}/here/{email}` = {name, seen, off} every `EL_BEAT_MS` (60 s) and at once with off:true when the page is hidden (not when going to another page of the site); firestore.rules: own doc, teacher reads. `elClassWatch` in `el-catalog.js` gives the lists (session = `fs.at` and/or `sid`; not connected = off or no beat for `EL_HERE_MS`, 150 s): the dashboard's top-right panel and the LP teacher bar show "⚠️ Left full screen" and "📴 Not connected" while full screen or a game is on; the game board shows the counts as pills in its top bar and the names only when the teacher taps them (it is on the projector). About one write and one teacher read per student per minute. **In and out, the whole group (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "I want to see not who's left but who is in and who is out … I need to be able to minimise the message but every time somebody gets out it gets bigger again"):** the beat also carries `fs` (in full screen, or on a page that needs none) and is sent at once when it changes; `elClassRoll` puts every student on the group's list (roster) in **In** (connected and in full screen) or **Out** (out of full screen with when they left and how many times, screen closed or another window, lost connection, not connected). The dashboard panel shows "✓ n in · ✗ n out of N" and the names; ▾ makes it small (`el_t_fsmin`) and it opens again by itself when a student who was in goes out (also on the LP page float). The game board shows the same counts in one pill and the names only when tapped (projector). **Full screen ending after a few minutes (Roger, 6 Nov 2026):** the game board (every slide and every minute) and class presentations (`el-deck-points.js`, every step) used to overwrite the whole `live/{group}` document, wiping `fs` and `go`; both now write only their own fields (`mergeFields`). **🔓 Release all groups (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "to make sure students can work when I'm not there"):** a button next to the full screen button at the top right of the dashboard (`relAll`, one confirm) sets `live/{group}.go` and `.fs` to null for every group and `ls/{id}.push[group]` to null in every LS loaded (not LS1): every pull-up, its lock and every full screen end at once. Learning paths and tabs opened or locked per group, recall checkpoints and class games are not touched. Pages without `el-cloud.js` (other websites) cannot be blocked. Not out of recall checkpoints, the sample exam or followed presentations. The class tab's first move (`cmTeacher` send) also writes `live/{group}.fs` = {on, at, until: class mode's until, cm: s}; every ⏹ Stop class mode (class tab chip, dashboard, LP bar) writes `{go:null, fs:null}`, so full screen always ends with class mode. **📋 Today's agenda in the class tab (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "in class mode, I should have the day of the agenda so that I can click it and students can see it on the board"):** the chip has a 📋 Today button (`shared/js/el-cm-agenda.js`, loaded by `cmTeacher`): a panel docked in the left side menu in place of its buttons (Roger: "I wanted fixed not floating", "we don't need this menu in class mode"; `.elrail` on LP pages, `aside.side` on the LS home; floating above the chip only without a wide side menu) with the group's class day from the Time plan agenda (`elAgendaOf` on the course's LS docs from LS2 on, the cached `lsInfo` read, only while it is open; today, else the next day with items), in the dashboard's order (`man`, tabs in path order) with the ⏱ minutes. - **🎓 Other windows, tabs and apps during class (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "And the same in class mode or when students have class"):** `shared/js/el-away.js` (`awayWatch`), used by `el-cloud.js` (while the group's class full screen is on: class mode only since 24 Nov 2026) and `lab/game/play.js` (during a class game). A page cannot stop a window switch, so leaving the page for more than 0.4 s (`document.hasFocus()` false or the page hidden) covers it ("Come back to the class!"), coming back needs a tap on 🎓 Back to the class (it also puts the full screen back), and each leave is counted in `live/{group}/fsLeft/{email}.aw` / `.awMs` (one write per leave; every fsLeft write carries `n`, `aw` and `awMs`). The ↩ list (`elWhoLine`, `elAwayNote` in `el-catalog.js`) shows it. - **Class mode: several steps at once, only today's steps, wrap-up (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "let me enable more than one SLP at once … me and they should only see the SLP planned for the class … a button to add other SLP … touch a SLP I say that that's for homework … when I finish the class mode, a window should pop up and let me say the SLP FINISHED and the ones to continue next class"):** in the class tab's 📋 Today (`shared/js/el-cm-agenda.js`) each of today's steps has 👥 (students may work on it too; kept in sessionStorage `el_cm_en` for that class mode) and ⋯ (🏠 Homework for next class = the same homework as the Time plan's 🏠 switch, `ls/{ls}.homework[group][id]` with `next:true`; ⏭ Next class = moved to the group's next class day on the agenda). ➕ Add a step puts any tab of the course's LSs on today's agenda (taken off a later day), opens it and turns 👥 on. The class tab writes `go.ps` = [{u, p, n}] (👥 steps) and `go.vis` = today's steps ("path#tab") on `live/{group}.go` with every move (always both: `go` is merged). - **⚡ I've finished: book, then pending tasks, then games (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "I want to allow doing further activities planned on the agenda for fast finishers"; "yes add I've finished but that brings them to the book section"; "they should go to the next chapter to do, the idea is that the books are available during class and they can read from the last chapter they read"; 21 Nov 2026: "In class I want fast finisher to read the book, if they are done, they can do pending tasks (button to go to pending task), only if they have no pending task, I want them to go to games (include the ones of the exam)"):** the class tab opens the LS's 📖 Book path for the group (rules level, `ensureOpen`, once) and writes `go.ff` = [book path] (`el-cm-agenda.js` plan/bookOpen). In class mode every student's 🎓 bar has ⚡ I've finished (always, not only on Book days). It opens the **⚡ Finished early?** card (`el-cloud.js` ffGo / ffState), which works out the order for that student: 0) an open class step (cmEn without the ⚡ ones) not done on their progress → "First finish today's work" with a button per step; 1) 📖 the Book until **every chapter is read** (all its questions answered, the reader's own done; it opens where they stopped; no row when today's Time plan has no Book); 2) ✅ their pending tasks = the ✅ To do list (`el-todo.js` sets `window.elTodoReady`, a Promise of `elTodoPending()` → [{u, p, n}] from the list's own todo() and the homework not done), a button per task (6 shown, "and N more in ✅ To do"); 3) only with nothing pending, 🎲 Games: the Games page (`lab/`) with the LS vocabulary games and the **🏗 Exam games** (the web exam's Hangman and Quick quiz, `shared/js/el-exam-games.js`, loaded from `<ls>/data/exam-games.js`, which `tools/web-exam/build.mjs` writes next to exam.js so the Games page never loads exam questions; same points store as after the exam). Done stages are green, the current one yellow. A button gives that stage to the student alone while this class mode lasts (sessionStorage `el_cm_ff` = {s, until, k, gs: [{u, p, n, hide}]}; the older {u, ps} is still read): page-only, counted as one more cmEn step (a chip on the bar; `hide` = the vocabulary games pages the Games cards open, no chip of their own), not moved by the teacher's next move, freed with elSecFree / EL_CM_FREE on load; each stage replaces the one before. It never makes or opens new work: only steps that exist and are open (the week-by-week rule). After class the steps follow the Time plan again (🏫 read-only until their day, answers kept).
- **🧑‍🎓 Self study in class mode (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "in class mode I want to have the option of self study to work on whatever they need to"):** 🧑‍🎓 Self study in the class tab's 📋 Today (`selfBtn`/`selfSet` in `shared/js/el-cm-agenda.js`, sessionStorage `el_cm_self`) writes `live/{group}.go.self = {s, at}` (merged into go, so the tab's moves keep it; ⏹ Stop clears go). Students (`cmSelf` in `el-cloud.js`, called in `goTo` right after `cmReport`): `LOCK` is let go (no 🎓 bar, no moves, every page open; no full screen and no away cover: `SELF_ON` turns off `fsWanted`, takes the page out of class.html and stops `awayWatch`, Roger 24 Nov 2026: "in selfstudy students don't need to be in full screen"; the here beats go on), a bar `#el-self` at the top (counted in `lockPad`) with ✅ My pending tasks (`el-todo.js remind({links:true})`, opened once per `go.self.at`, marked seen only once it showed), 📖 the course's latest LS book and 🎲 Games (`lab/`). Lasts 90 minutes at most. When go.self goes, `cmGo` takes them back to the teacher's step. No extra reads, no rules change. **📖 The book is open in self study** (problem report, 2ESOB, 24 Nov 2026): the bar's 📖 The book frees the Book tab on that page (`EL_CM_FREE` + `elSecFree(["book"])` in `cmSelf`, as ⚡ I've finished does with `ffGrant`), so a Book that is not on the Time plan is not hidden or read-only there; after self study it follows the Time plan again.
- **🔥 Warm-up game at the start of every class (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "start every class with a mini game like ahorcado with the grammar and vocabulary of the unit", "but not always ahorcado", "they say letters and I press them so I don't even have to know the answer", "so the class mode the game appears automatically"):** `shared/js/el-warmup.js`, a full-screen board over the class tab, opened by itself once when class mode starts (`sessionStorage el_cm_wu`, set with `el_cm_ag`; `cmTeacher` → `warmOpen(true)` in `el-cm-agenda.js`, skipped when there is no class game to use) and again from 🔥 Warm-up in 📋 Today. Projector only: the students call out, the teacher taps; no Firestore reads or writes, no marks, students stay where they are. Words and sentences come from the class games (`lab/game/decks/<id>.js` + `decks/more/`) of today's learning paths (`warmDecks`: by learning path title, games on today too; else the latest earlier class day; else the latest learning path with games). Four games take turns, a different one each class per group (`localStorage el_wu`, the teacher's browser): 🔤 Hangman (vocabulary answer, letter keyboard; its sentence hidden at first, 💡 ½ Half the sentence then 📝 The whole sentence with its picture (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "students can guess it super fast with the sentence"), the whole sentence shows anyway once the round ends + physical keys, a hangman drawn in 8 parts, one per wrong letter; Roger 13 Nov: "I don't see a hangman"), 🔀 Word order (a sentence in shuffled tiles, tap in order), 🕵️ Spot the mistake (a grammar sentence with a wrong option, tap the wrong word), 🅰 No vowels (💡 adds a vowel). Three rounds, 👁 Show answer, game chips to switch, ▶ Start the class (or Esc) closes it. A new deck needs nothing more. **Open it again (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "if I close it I want to come back again"):** 🔥 Warm-up on the class mode chip and in 📋 Today; within the same class mode it goes on with the same game and round (`sessionStorage el_wu_cur` = {s, game, round, items}). **💬 Saying of the day (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "add a proverb or saying like Deeds are your monuments"):** one English proverb at the top of the warm-up, the same all day per group (hash of day + group), from the LS topic list first (`SAY_TOPIC` by deck prefix, e.g. `4eso-ls2`) else `SAY_GEN`; 💡 What does it mean? shows a simple meaning and the Catalan saying when there is one. Proverbs only, no quotes put in anyone's mouth. **✅ Finish all your activities (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** the warm-up's last slide, once per class mode (`sessionStorage el_wu_rem` = class mode start): ▶ Start the class (or the end of the game) shows it instead of closing (Esc still closes); a class with no warm-up game gets only this slide (`warmOpen` passes `only:"rem"`). Left: each learning path's average share done and "n/N finished"; right: the stops of the students' map (`elVoyage.model`) with the % of students who finished each. The numbers come from the dashboard's copy in the same browser (`remSave` in `teacher.html`, written each time a group's card is drawn: `localStorage el_rem_<group>` = {at, g, gname, ls, N, lps, steps}, only tabs due before today or set as homework, `elDueBefore`; older than 12 h = message only), so no reads. Showing it calls `remind()` in `el-cm-agenda.js`: one write, `live/{group}.go.rem = {s, at}`; each student page in that class mode (`cmRemind` in `el-cloud.js`, once per class mode via `localStorage el_rem_seen`, for 20 minutes) opens `remind()` in `el-todo.js`: the student's own bars (due steps done ÷ due steps per learning path) and the tabs still to do (`elTodoList`), homework and the exam day, no links, from the To do list's own reads. **🏁 Race card on the board (same day):** `vyRace` in `today.html` fills the map's `.vy-ovl` once a day (`localStorage el_race_<uid>`, set when closed) when ✅ To do has something, never in class time or class mode; its one button (OK) calls `vyPend`: the `.vy-steps` column gets `vypend` (not `pend`: a board class), a "👉 Look here: your pending activities (n)" bar with Show all my steps, and only the stops on ✅ To do (`todoItems`, like `lsBeds`' thirsty ones) marked `pd`, the rest hidden; `S.vyPend` keeps it through redraws; no column (≤900 px) = it only closes. **No repeated info (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "we are repeating the info"; he chose the website, not class):** in the courses with a race map (`voyC()`), `warmOpen` passes `onRace: mapOpen`, so the end of the games (`toRem` in `el-warmup.js`) closes the warm-up and opens the class map with `mapOpen({fin:true})`: a "✅ Finish all your activities!" pill in its header and ▶ Start the class to close it; no slide and no `go.rem` write. Other courses keep the slide. On the board, `vyRace` now opens `remind({race:true, links:true, done})` from `el-todo.js` (the student's bars, a race line and the pending tabs as links, `REM()` gives each its `href`); closing it runs `vyPend`; `vyRace0` (the old short card) is the fallback and the teacher's 👁 student view. **🏆 Your place (Roger, 25 Nov 2026: "tell them their position"):** `vyPos(n)` in `today.html` → `{line, up}` from `S.vy` (your `elVoyage.model(V).done` vs the `mates`' `s` of the road document; place = 1 + classmates with more steps, so ties share it; 1st 🥇, 2nd/3rd podium, last quarter "right now, but you can still catch up!"; `up` = the activities that lift you a place, only when ≤ the pending count; Catalan "a la posició N", no gender), passed as `remind({pos})` (a pill in `#el-rem .hd`, `p.pos`) and shown in `vyRace0` (`.vy-pos`); no classmates on the road = no line. `vyRace` waits for the road document (`RD[ls]`) before opening the card (the board draws again when it arrives, also after a failed read). The map's back-to-my-vehicle button (`.vy-down`) shows the vehicle and **You / Tu**. **Readable on any page (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "I can't read all the letters well here"):** the card's injected CSS gives its `h2` and header `p` `color:#fff` and its rows `#0f172a`, because pages set their own `h2{color:…}` (green/navy) that used to win over the inherited white; the same for `#el-look` (el-cloud.js) and `#el-cmwrap h2` (el-cm-agenda.js). Any new overlay sets each text's colour itself. **✅ To do (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** the floating button opens the same `remind({links:true})` card when `REM` has data (else the old box); the board's `todoBox` rows are a `.tdbar` bar in `elPctCol` with its %, "Et falta:" and only the missing tabs as `<a>` chips (📝 homework, `late` class on the row).
- **The class tab has no side menu (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "when I stop the class this appears and I don't need it"):** `el-catalog.js` (loaded before any menu is drawn) marks the tab opened with `?elcm=` (sessionStorage `el_cmtab`, for the tab's life) with `html.elcmtab`, and `html.elcmon` while class mode runs: the menu buttons are never shown there; the left column is only the place of 📋 Today while class mode is on, and goes when it stops (`quit` in `el-cloud.js` removes `elcmon`). With 📋 Today closed (✕), the column shows the menu buttons again instead of staying empty (`html.elcmag` = 📋 Today wanted, sessionStorage `el_cm_ag`, toggled by `want()` in `el-cm-agenda.js`) (teacher report, 2ESOB, 19 Nov 2026). Only today's steps stay listed there without flashing: the students' once-a-second tab check (`cmStudentTabs` in `el-cloud.js`) leaves the class tab alone while `html.elcmon` is on, as `cmTeacher` hides its tabs itself (it gave every step back each second, so the path's column jumped; teacher report, 2ESOB, 19 Nov 2026: "the left menu kept moving, things appeared and disappeared"). Other tabs keep the menu.
- **Who left stays on screen, also over a video (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I need to know who disconnects, and I need their names to be there … even when we are watching a video"):** the dashboard panel (`#fsctl`, teacher.html `fsDraw`) and the class tab's panel (`#el-fsfloat`, el-cloud.js `fsFloat`) always name who is out now (red: out of full screen, screen closed, lost connection) and who left full screen in this class and came back (orange, with the time and how many times; `elWhoLeft` in el-catalog.js), also when made small with ▾ (then the In list and the not-connected names become a count). The class game board (lab/game/host.js `top`) shows the same two lists in a white strip under its top bar, without a tap (Roger: "and even in game mode"). Videos: on a teacher page with the panel showing, a video's own ⛶ full screen is turned into the page's full screen with the video filling it (`.el-bigvid`, ✕ Smaller or Esc), so the panel stays on top; `elPlayVideo(src)` plays a video over the page with its own ⛶ Full screen (the class tab's 📋 Today 🎬 Shadowing, and links marked `data-elvid`: the dashboard agenda's ▶ and Materials' shadowing video). No extra reads or writes.
- **🙋 Attendance (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I only want to know if they left. At the beginning of the class I can check the people out and accept it … it's a way of taking attendance … I want a register of the attendance from now on in my dashboard"):** the class panels count ✗ only who left; students with no beat in this class are ⚪ "not here". During a class the panel (dashboard and class tab) lists them as chips (tap = here after all) with 🙋 Save attendance → `attendance/{group}_{yyyy-mm-dd}_{hhmm}` = {group, day, start, absent {email: name}, late {email: ms}, names, n, at} (shared/js/el-attend.js; teacher-only rule). A student saved absent who connects later becomes late by itself (one write). ✎ Change reopens the list. Dashboard group page tile 🙋 Attendance (`attPage`): one column per class, ✓ / ✗ / ⏰ time, totals; a tap on a cell cycles ✓ → ✗ → ⏰. Reads: the class's doc while the panel is on, the group's docs once when the register opens.
- **One line of counts, names on a tap (Roger, 10 Nov 2026: "this was too big and I couldn't see the screen! And during the games students played to log in and out so that their name appeared. I only need … who is in the class and who is not and see the numbers (then if I click I see the names?)"; "the menu of the games was moving up and down when the info changed"):** replaces the always-named lists above. The dashboard panel (`#fsctl`) and the class tab's panel (`#el-fsfloat`) show only `✓ n in · ✗ n left · ↩ n · ⚪ n not here (🙋 n absent once saved) of N`, each count a button (`elWhoLine` in el-catalog.js, `data-wl`); a tap opens that count's names under the line (who left with why, who left and came back, the In list, or the 🙋 attendance chips + Save / the saved list with ✎ Change), a second tap closes it. Nothing opens by itself (▾ and the auto-reopen are gone). The game board has no names strip any more (it made the top bar jump): only the pill, the names in its pop-up on a tap. The LP teacher bar's full screen row shows counts only. **✕ Hide** (Roger, same day: "can I hide the message?"): on the dashboard (`data-do="fshide"`, kept in `localStorage.el_t_fshide`) only the top buttons stay, with a `👥 in/of` button that shows the panel again; on the class tab (`data-hide`, this tab only) the panel shrinks to that one chip. No extra reads or writes.
- **The class tab always shows its own group, on every page and over Correct together (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "I need to see who is in class during class mode and during the activities done together (grammar and reading)"):** the panel is one factory, `fsPanel(getG, redraw, owns)` in `shared/js/el-cloud.js`. The learning path's bar (`teacherSections`) makes one for the group picked there; the class tab (`cmTeacher`) makes one for its class mode's group, which owns `#el-fsfloat` while class mode is on, so it also shows on pages without the bar (recall checkpoints…). The bar now starts on the class mode's group too (a class mode started from the dashboard used to show the group last picked in the bar). While ✅ Correct together is open (`shared/correct/open.js` fires `el-correct` on open and close) the line becomes a strip at the top and the frame starts under it; with ✕ Hide only the 👥 chip stays, bottom right. Listeners on the same documents are shared by the SDK and the roster is read once per page. The floating panel and the 👥 chip can be dragged anywhere (a press that moves more than 6 px drags instead of pressing; Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to move this icon"); the spot is kept in localStorage `el_t_fspos` and used on every page, clamped on screen.
- **Teacher view bar and learning-path table (Roger, 2 Nov 2026):** in `today.html?student={uid}` the yellow bar stays on top with the LS chips of the student's group and the board tabs (`asBar`); it opens on 📈 Learning paths and mark unless the link has `#exam`. Students' boards also open on 📈 My formative assessment, the first card (Roger 2 Nov 2026: "the formative tab is the main one"); a closed LS and LS1 open on My exams. The 📈 tab holds `lpStatus`, "My LS mark so far" and the criteria summary (`summary`: My criteria, steps done per criterion with a bar; since 5 Nov 2026 no "My average mark" and no mark out of 10 there, because it showed 3.1 next to an LS mark so far of 9.9: it counted steps not due yet. The only mark on the board is "My LS mark so far"); the old per-path grids are gone (Roger 2 Nov 2026: the table has the same, the score cell still opens the detail dialog) (the exams tab only the exam, Roger 2 Nov 2026); from LS2 on a 4th card 🎬 My final product (`fpView`, `#final`): mark, due day, each step handed in with self-assessment and teacher answers, rubric rows with their share of the LS mark (teacher view adds each row's mark from progress.parts.fp). LS1 keeps its final product on 📈.: every learning path of the LS for that student in time-plan order, each tab ✔ / amber (done after its `stepDue`/`due` day, from the page state `doneAt`) / ✗ missing past its day / 🔒, the state, the score and a "counted" tag for the ones in `elDueWork` ("X of Y done this term"). Students get the same table on their 📈 tab without the "counted" tags (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "click the learning paths you haven't done yet and it proposes them"): every tab not done of an open path is a link to `path#panel` (`tabHref`, cards/practice → `vocab`), and "👉 Next for you" proposes the first missing tab, oldest day first.
- **Student view = exactly what the student sees (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I need to see what students really see, I don't want to see this yellow thing, maybe a red line surrounding the screen"):** no yellow bars any more: the student view shows a 4 px red frame round the screen (`#el-as-frame`, clicks pass through) with a small red name tag at the top (click = exit), and the bottom bar is the student's own (Problem, Idea, which do nothing here) plus a red ✕ Exit. On the board the teacher's extras (every tab, "counted" tags, rubric row marks, teacher notes, `asBar`) are off: `today.html` `TX` (always null) gates them, `AS` only chooses whose data is read. Students never get any of it (`AS` is set only for the teacher account).
- **Criteria in simple words (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "els criteris haurien de sortir explicats, no només els números"):** `shared/js/el-criteria-easy.js` holds a hand-written student-friendly explanation of every criterion, per course, [Catalan, English] (`elCriterionEasy(course, code, en)`). The student board (`today.html`) shows it in "Your exam by criteria", "My criteria", the learning-path criteria legend and the pill tooltip; tapping a code opens the dialog with the simple text plus the official Decret 175/2022 text. `el-criteria.js` stays literal (generated). When a programme adds a criterion, add its simple line too. The teacher dashboard keeps the official texts.
- **Exam results notice (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: “I want students to receive a notification that they have their exam results on their dashboard”):** `shared/js/el-exam-notice.js`, started by `el-cloud.js` on every page for a student, shows a card once when `results/{ls}__assessment` has a score in **every** section of `EL_GRADING[ls].exam` (an exam half typed on the dashboard or a partial import triggers nothing), only for LSs whose board shows the exam (`only`) and that the board lists for the group (not Hidden, assigned). “See my results →” saves the marks as one string in `students/{uid}.examSeen[ls]` and opens `today.html?ls={ls}#exam` (board on that LS, “Your exam by criteria” open); “Later” hides it until the browser closes. Marks that change later (a correction, a re-import with other numbers) bring it back as “results have changed”. Catalan for Beginners, Insecure and no route, English otherwise (`repEn`). No rules change: students already update their own profile. No email is sent. An LS2 exam needs the board to show its exam before it gets the notice.

## Term report (Roger, 23 Nov 2026; rule in docs/REQUIREMENTS.md § 3 "📄 Term report")

- **What we worked on**: `shared/js/el-term-report.js` `EL_TREP[term][course]` = Roger's points per LS (ca + en) and the sources that measure each (`{p}` latest panel score, `{d}` done, `{x}` exam sections once released, `{fp}` final product, `{g}` latest class game of a deck). `elTrepPct` gives a point's %; the page and the tool share it.
- **Drafts**: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node tools/term-report/draft.mjs --out /mnt/project-files/alumnes/term1-reports [--save|--data cache.json] [--apply]`. Per student: each point's % and one line (latest wrong answer, exam question, writing error or FP ✔/➜ line; students' own text has no tags), timing (due = tabs ticked on the Time plan by the end of term; in the class week = done within 7 days of the tick, over due; last minute = late ones done in the last week of term or the 3 days before the exam, over done; steady ≥ 70 % in the week, last ≥ 40 % last minute, else behind), the weekly bars and the comment. Writes `drafts.json` + `preview.md`; `--apply` (only on Roger's "apply") writes `termReports/{email} = {t1}` and `termReportsT/{email}` (first tries vs latest, teacher only) with `lib.mjs` `set`.
- **Page**: `today.html#report` (`reportPlace`): the marks, criteria (`compBox`, term 1) and 📈 How my skills grow are worked out live; the % lines, timing and comment come from `termReports`. While it draws, `S.inRep` makes `degOf`/`dgName` add the Catalan level name in brackets in English.
- **Order and Why this level (25 Nov 2026):** `reportPlace` puts 🎓 Your marks first, right under the Draft line. `repWhy` writes one `.cpwr` grid line per piece (LS · piece · mark · `wdg` chip: English degree + one-word Catalan, Catalan alone for Starter) and sits after `.cpm` inside the wrapping `.cpcr` button, so it uses the card's whole width; phone rules (`.trep …`) in `el-phone.css` stack the mark and chip under the piece and stop the degree names pushing criteria cards past 390 px. `progressPlace` opens with `.pgrep` (📄 My Term 1 report card, when `repOk()`); `elStudentMenu` order: board · marks · progress · flashcards · examprep · practise (the phone's Me list the same).
- **Hidden until class**: `repOk()` = demo, the teacher's student view (`AS`), teacher/tester, or `window.EL_REP_ON`. The class tab's **📄 Show draft reports** (`el-cm-agenda.js` `repSet`) writes `live/{group}.go.report = {s, at}`; `el-cloud.js` `cmReport` (in `goTo` and `cmGo`) sets `EL_REP_ON`, fires `el-report` and keeps class-mode students on `today.html#report`. `firestore.rules`: a student `get`s their own `termReports/{email}` only while `go.report.at` is under 90 minutes old. 👁 Student view › 📄 Term report opens `today.html?student=uid#report`. The group's **📄 Term reports** tile (`teacher.html` place `reports`, `repView`) reads `termReports` once per session (one read per report) and lists each student's timing, comment and a link to the report. **25 Nov 2026**: `el-cloud.js` `myResults` keeps the unheld results in `window.EL_RS_RAW`; `today.html` builds `D.rsRep` (by activity, without `showFrom`/`held`; the student view takes `D.rs` before `elHeld`) and `placeHtml` swaps it into `D.rs` only while `reportPlace` renders, so the report counts held exams. `EL_TREP` LS entries with `fpLater` leave their final product out (`S.repNoFp`: `compPieces` skips the fp block, `lsTile` drops the 🎬 part). `draft.mjs`: `held()` is always false, and writing errors are kept per point by `WKIND(type)` against `WKEY` (wtask/wgrammar/wvocab/worg); an example with no answer (own-example questions) is never quoted. `repWhy(c,C)` (report only, under `indLine`): `compOfPieces` keeps each criterion's pieces (`C.pcs`), grouped by source and LS with their weighted mark; an LS1 final product piece reads its row (`EL_FP_RUBRICS[fp.a].rows[].c === c`) from the teacher's feedback lines and quotes that row's descriptor. Its **📣 Publish the draft** / **🔒 Hide the drafts again** (`repPublish`) writes `teams/{group}.repPub.t1` (ISO time or null); `repPub()` in `today.html` reads it from the `teams` doc the board already loads (no new read), lets `repOk()` through and shows the **📄 My Term 1 report (draft)** chip (`repChip`) on the board; `firestore.rules` `reportPublished()` lets the group's students `get` their own report while it is set.

## Content-first workflow
- Read the applicable LS guidance and current course overview (containing all three routes). Implement only content explicitly approved by the user; website content and extracted drafts are not automatically approved. Preserve previously agreed requirements and the last approved content separately from proposed revisions. Flag missing information or contradictions and resolve them with the user before implementing the affected part. Technical and layout decisions may follow existing project rules, but agents must not invent or change pedagogical content during implementation.

## Teacher dashboard (`teacher.html`)
- `teacher.html` is for Roger only, written **only in English** (also the teacher and tester banners on LS pages), and is maintained by the **English Lab project** (site code). The LS project must not edit it; ask the English Lab project for dashboard changes.
- **Simple dashboard (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "make the teacher's board style like the students"):** a left side menu (📣 Announcement, 🏠 Home, the groups, Questions, Materials, Reference, How it works; a scrolling row under 980 px) and, for Home and each group, a page with only a few red alerts, **one big card for the class** (now or next by `EL_TIMETABLE`, its session from the time plan with its tabs, how many students finished it, 📌 Pull it up / 🔓 Release them, Open it) and tiles. Each tile opens a place (`S.place`, ‹ back link at the top) holding the old boxes unchanged: group → 📅 Time plan (weekend check, this week, teaching plan) · ✅ To do (checkpoints, To do) · 📈 Learning paths (pull up, open and lock, progress grid, criteria legend, LS status) · 📊 Marks (LS grade, criteria result, self-assessment, Marking PDF) · 👥 Students (levels, team points, student list; see below) · 💬 Messages (announcements, problems, ideas); Home (`S.g` "plan") → a tile per group, 📅 Time plan of all groups, 💬 Messages. **Browser Back (Roger, 7 Nov 2026):** every change of place, group, LS or year view adds a history step (`navSync()` in `render()`, `popstate` restores `S.g/place/ls/year`), so Back goes to the screen before (e.g. a place → its group's tiles) and the ‹ link calls `history.back()` when that is the screen it opens; live updates add no step. A new dashboard box goes into one of these places, never on the tiles page; long explanations go in an `ⓘ How it works` fold (`details.how`).
- **Tile names (Roger, 11 Nov 2026: "change Marks for assessment details and Students for students' progress" … "everywhere", in the teacher's dashboard):** the 📊 `marks` place is shown as **Assessment details** and the 👥 `students` place as **Students' progress** (PLACES, the group tiles, the Home class box heading, tooltips and help text). Place ids and code names stay `marks`/`students`; older notes below still say Marks/Students for the same places. The student board's 📊 Marks/Notes is unchanged.
- **👥 Students is the group's place across LSs and terms (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "choose the LS, the term (1, 2, or 3) and in each term the points in the same table. When I press a result I want to … see what the students answered (all even check point and exam) … a transversal place not only of the LS"; "when I enter in each button in there I need to be able to change the LS"):** every LS place (Time plan, To do, Learning paths, Final product, Marks, Coverage) has LS chips under its title (`lsChips`, `data-do="lspick"`); Students, Attendance and Messages link back to the group only. Students has **Term** chips (`stuterm`, sets `S.term`, and moves to an LS of that term) and the LS chips of that term (`stuBar`; an LS is in term `lsTerm`). One table (`progress(studs, list, true)`): Route · **Points − n +, All work, Bonus** of the term (`ptCols`; the explanation and ⬇ Export are in the “How points work” fold, `ptHow`) · **🧩 📚 Class games** of the LS in their own column (Roger, 9 Nov 2026; "none saved in this LS yet" when empty; no longer next to the name here) · ✗ n tabs missing opens `ptMissDlg` (each missing tab links to the student view) · the learning paths · **📝 Exam** of the LS (`exCell` → `exDlg`: points per section, by criterion, report link; exams are on paper, so no answers) · Total. **Exam beside Total (Roger, 11 Nov 2026: "where is the exam?")**: the 📝 Exam column sits after the learning paths, next to Total, so ◧ Hide Route, Points… never hides it; for an LS whose grading is only exam + final product (LS1, `G().only`) Total is the **LS mark** /10 by the block weights (exam 75 · FP 25, `grade()`), with its degree, "–" until both are in. The Exam heading shows the exam's criteria pills (`exPills`, from its sections); a click on the mark opens the student's 📄 exam report (`today.html?student=…&ls=…#exam`, new tab) and 🔍 next to it opens `exDlg` (sections, mark per criterion). **Every tab cell opens what the student answered** (`stepPanel`): steps done/first try/latest, what they handed in (fields, 🔊), every question with their **first try** (`state.items`) and **now** (`state.ans`) with ✓/✗ and the right answer (questions from the page's data file via `ELItems.bank`, cached in `QA`; no Firestore reads), and **👁 See it as they do** (`today.html?student=…&go=<page>#<tab>`, read-only student view). A secure checkpoint cell opens its answers (`cpDlg`). LS1 pages keep answers inside the page, so LS1 cells show the steps and the 👁 link. A term with no LS yet shows the points only.
- **👥 Students: Both LSs, final product score only, the rubric in the final product dialog (Roger, 9 Nov 2026).** In a term with more than one LS, the **Both LSs** chip (S.stuBoth, data-do="lsboth") shows every LS of the term in one table: the term's Points/All work/Bonus once, then one summary block per LS (Class games + 📝, 📝 Exam, Total: graded points or steps done), then each LS's learning paths in order (`progress(studs,list,stu,ids)`; a dark left border `lsb` where each LS starts, an LS tag on each learning path). A click on another LS's cell makes it S.ls first (`bothLs`), so the dialogs read its grading. In Students the final product (`fpAct`) shows only its Score column, never its steps. In the final product dialog, `fpStuRub` shows the student's rubric as their 📊 My marks shows it (LS1: the parts of the saved feedback shaded on EL_FP_RUBRICS, as fbRubric in today.html; LS2: your levels on the rows with the indicators) and `fpGemRub` shows Gemini's draft on the same rubric (blue). Keep `fbParts` in step with fbBox in today.html.
- **✏️ Corrections (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "another section called corrections where I can see when you correct the writings and listenings and where I can order to correct everything that's waiting"):** a side-menu item and a Home tile (`corrPage` in `teacher.html`), every group: **Waiting** in plain words, **by student** by default (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "if there is work to correct, even if you correct it, I wanna see, Pau is waiting for these corrections and a button to say correct it": each student, what they handed in, ✏️ Correct it now) or by activity (what the next run corrects, by the same rules as `marks.mjs fb-ready` and `ready`; marked steps not ready yet folded apart; possible copies waiting for the teacher), **Last corrections** by day (from `results.fbAt[step]`, saved by `fb-apply` since 6 Nov 2026, else the doc's `updated`; `marked[step]`; `flags[step].at`; `answers[step].at`), each name opening that correction, and **ⓘ What runs on its own and what needs Claude**. The To do's marked-step lines point to Corrections (✏️ See in Corrections); the 🤖 Marking sheet stays only as a small "mark it yourself" button. **✏️ Correct everything waiting now** saves `settings/marking.request = {at, by}` (teacher only); at once the dashboard asks the school Apps Script (`tools/notify/school-mail.gs`, `action: "mark"`, teacher's sign-in) to start the scheduled task "English Lab: mark and correct" through its API trigger (the token, `MARK_TASK_TOKEN`, lives only in the teacher's copy of the script; the script starts each request once, never twice within 10 min) and saves `request.fired` (or `request.err`, why it did not start, shown on the card after a reload). Each new request clears the last one's `fired`/`err`, and a `fired` older than the request is ignored (9 Nov 2026: a merge kept the `fired` of 7 Nov, so a press that never started showed "Claude got it at 17:53" and the backup skipped it). Backup (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: the first press waited 45 min, GitHub runs `*/15` schedules only every few hours): the free GitHub Action *Marking on request* (`.github/workflows/marking-request.yml`, `tools/marking/request.mjs`, read-only) asks the same script with `SCHOOL_MAIL_KEY`, then tries the `MARK_TASK_TOKEN` secret; it skips a request with `fired`. If nothing could start, the card says so at once and links to Run now on claude.ai. `marks.mjs ready` saves `settings/marking.run = {started, via}` and `audit` saves `run.done` (only for a run started in the last 12 h), so the card shows asked → correcting → done. Reads: only `settings/marking` (1 document). **Fresh after every run** (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: the page showed 25 pieces waiting from a browser copy of 2 Nov that Claude had already corrected): the waiting list, the side-menu badge and the Home tile come from the browser copy (CACHE), so a group whose copy is older than `run.started`/`run.done` is fetched again by itself (`corrFresh`, on open and on the Corrections page, once per run: `AT.corr`), and until it is in they show ⏳ Checking, never an old number; a failed fetch shows the yellow "These numbers may be old" note with Update all and retries after 5 min. **Keep marking separable** (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: if the site is ever sold, teachers would plug in their own Claude or ChatGPT, or mark by hand following the model): the page and the students' cards only read the saved fields (`replies`, `fbSig`, `fbAt`, `marked`, `levels`, `flags`), so any marker that writes them (another AI, or the teacher with the 🤖 Marking sheet or a hand-in's answer box) works the same; never tie a page to how the marking is done.
- **Students' comments answered at once, and Roger edits or removes the answer (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I want you to answer straight away but I want to see what you said and be able to edit … or remove it"):** when a student's page saves a new comment on a hand-in (not only "no problem": `elNoComment`), `el-cloud.js` sets `students/{uid}.commented` and starts the scheduled task "English Lab: problem reports (instant)" (school Apps Script action `problem`, same limits), which runs `marks.mjs comments --new` and `answer <file> --new --apply` (only those students; `settings/marking.cmtRun`): see `docs/general/problem-reports-auto.md`. The Friday 15:00 task still answers any left. The dashboard shows each answer in the group To do ("🗨️ Claude answered N comments this week", Open), in the 👥 Students tab cell (✉️ comment / 🗨️ answered) and in the activity panel: ✏️ Update Claude's answer or 🗑 Remove (`answers[step] = {t: "", q, removed}`: the student sees nothing, that comment is not answered again, a new comment opens it again; no student email for it).
- **Loading (Roger, 31 Oct 2026, slow on the phone):** `load()` in `teacher.html` sends all its Firestore reads at once (`Promise.all`), never one after another; a new collection joins that list (`soft(...)` if the dashboard can open without it).
