While loading, `#main` shows "⏳ Loading…"; if a needed read fails it shows "Try again" instead of a blank page.
- Organised by **class group**: 2ESO A, 2ESO B, 4ESO A, 4ESO B (`EL_GROUPS` in `shared/js/el-catalog.js`).
- **Student list (roster):** `roster/{email in lower case}` = `{email, group, surname1, surname2, first, tutor}` (`pending-…` id for a student without an email yet). Roger imports it from Excel in the dashboard (Student list → Import from Excel: one sheet per group, columns Correu, Cognom 1, Cognom 2, Nom, Tutor/a; it replaces the whole list) and can add, edit or remove one student from the student's window. **It is personal data: never commit it to this repository.** Only school accounts on the list, plus the tester, can sign in (`onRoster`/`refuse` in `el-cloud.js`, `signedIn()` in `firestore.rules`), and the list sets the group (`ensureGroup` copies it to `students/{uid}.group`; `lsForMe` reads the list). Testers still pick a group once. If the list cannot be read, the site falls back to the old behaviour (the student picks the group).
- Each group tab shows every student on the list, sorted by first surname (“Surname1 Surname2, First”), signed-in / listed count on the tab; students who have not signed in yet are grey rows. Email and tutor only appear in the student's window; a Tutor filter sits next to the Route filter. Signed-in accounts not on the list appear under "Not on the list". **Unknown accounts (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "I don't know who these people are"):** work saved under a uid with no `students/{uid}` doc (e.g. a mark imported for an account that never signed in; the teacher's own uid is never listed) shows as "❔ Unknown account" with its work (`workOf`); **Link** picks a list entry not signed in yet and writes `students/{uid}` = `{email, name, group, linked:true}` (what a first sign-in writes), **Hide** saves `settings/accounts.hidden[uid]` (nothing deleted). An import tool that writes marks by uid must also write that `students/{uid}` doc. Key, criteria legend, result per criterion, teaching plan, LS settings and the student list are folded sections that stay as Roger leaves them.
- Per group and LS (the course's latest open LS by default; an "Open LS ↗" button next to the LS name or selector opens that LS's `index.html` in a new tab, same path as the student board's LS buttons): To do (final product comments to answer, route requests left from before 26 Oct 2026, and one line counting unmarked final products; submissions without feedback are not listed, by Roger's choice: the table's ✔/💬 show them) → teaching plan (folded) → learning paths × steps table (foldable per learning path) → result per criterion → LS settings (folded). Marks, feedback, reopening and final-product hand-ins are in a dialog, not in `prompt()`.
- **Back to the tab you came from (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** the dashboard remembers its last tab in `localStorage` `el_t_last` (tab, last group per course, LS within 3 h). The teacher's Dashboard link on course and LS pages (`dashHere` in `el-cloud.js`) and the game's ← Dashboard (`lab/game/host.js`) add `?course=` (and `?ls=`); the dashboard then opens that course's last-used group (and that LS) and removes the query from the address. Without them it reopens the last tab; with no LS remembered or passed, the group's current LS by the time plan as before.
- **Criteria are readable:** every criterion pill shows its text on hover and opens it on click (with the learning paths and steps that give evidence), and each LS has a legend under the grid, on the dashboard and on the student board. Texts live in `shared/js/el-criteria.js`, generated by `node tools/gen-criteria.js .` from `docs/4eso/programme.md` and `docs/2eso/programme.md` (official wording; the 2ESO LS1 guide summaries fill in what the programme does not list); regenerate it when a programme changes, never edit the wording by hand.
- **Every step shows its assessment criterion.** Learning-path criteria are `criteria` in `el-catalog.js`; step criteria are `EL_STEP_CRITERIA[activity][step]`, falling back to `EL_EVIDENCE[step]`; steps come from `EL_ACTIONS`. Each LS documents the map in its `assessment-criteria.md`. When you add or change a learning path, update `EL_ACTIONS`, the step criteria and that file together.
- **Whole school year by terms (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: "see all the school year by terms … (2 LS per each) that update as I create the LS and the items to assess"):** the last option of a group's LS dropdown on the dashboard, "📆 Whole school year (by terms)" (`yearView` in `teacher.html`). Nothing names an LS: LS n of a course (its order in `EL_LS`) goes in term ceil(n/2) (an `EL_LS` entry may set `term`), an empty slot says "not created yet", and an LS's assessed items are the blocks of `EL_GRADING[ls]` with their weights ("no assessed items yet" without an entry). Each item counts in the term `EL_PLAN[ls].report[block]` says (LS2: exam and grammar and vocabulary in term 1, final product and other competences in term 2, as in its `terms`), else the term of its date (exam day, last due day of its activities), else its LS's term. **Marks by competence (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: the term mark is an average of the criteria, each linked to its competence, weighted by how much each competence is worked):** each competence's share of an LS is **how much it is worked** (Roger chose it, 30 Oct 2026, after seeing listening at 5–7 %): steps of the LS activities per competence from `EL_STEP_CRITERIA` (`lsWork`/`byWork`; a step with two criteria counts half each; only competences the LS assesses; an LS with no step criteria, like LS1, keeps the grading plan shares; a chip switches to “by the grading plan”). Inside a competence, every item is split into pieces, one per criterion (`pieces` in `teacher.html`), weighted by its share of the LS mark: exam sections by points (criteria from the section, else from `evidence`, else the grammar and vocabulary criteria), final product rubric rows by weight (or the activity's criteria equally with `fp.mark`), grammar and vocabulary by the criteria of its steps (`EL_STEP_CRITERIA`), other competences equally. Each LS weighs 100, so a competence assessed in two LSs counts twice. Competence = criterion code (`5.1` → CE5, `CD2 2.2` → CD2); process and teamwork rows have none and are left out. A degree confirmed in the LS (`final[c]`) replaces that criterion's marks in that LS. Competence mark = its pieces weighted; term and year mark = competences weighted by the share counted so far. A coloured bar shows each competence's share in each LS, term and the year; clicking a mark lists its criteria and items. The term's points bonus is shown next to the term mark. **Transversal competences out of the English mark (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** criteria of CC, CD, CPSAA and the entrepreneurial CE n (`compOf`: a key that is not a plain number, so “CE3 3.3” is never English CE3) are left out of the shares, the bars and the term and year marks; each term and the year show them apart (“Transversal competences · not in the English mark”) with their own marks. **Year mark = continuous assessment (same day):** each competence's year mark is its term marks weighted by the term number (×1, ×2, ×3), only the terms with a mark for it (`yearOf`); the year mark is the competences weighted by their share of the year. An item whose day has not come yet (no day: its LS is Hidden) never counts 0. A new LS only needs its `EL_LS`, `EL_GRADING` and `EL_PLAN` entries (with `report`).
- **Degree counts in 📆 Term and year marks (Roger, 23 Nov 2026: "here I need a statistic of the NA, AS, AE, AN"):** `degStats` in `teacher.html` draws, above each term table and the Whole year table of `yearView`, the number and % of students per degree (`elDegree` of the term mark `byComp(...).mark` or year mark `yearOf(...).mark`; AE first, `LVCOL` colours, `.dstat` styles), a bar and "n passed". It counts `live` = signed-in students that are not `tester` (the teacher's own account is `tester` too); students with no mark yet are left out and named. The group averages (`grpAvg`, "group average") use the same `live`, so Roger's 0 rows no longer lower them. No new Firestore reads.
- Teaching plan: `shared/js/el-plan.js` (`EL_PLAN[ls]`, one entry per LS id with terms, assessment per term and dated sessions per group in `groups` + `g`; the dashboard shows the selected group's column). Update it when a plan changes; term 1 ends 20 Nov, term 2 ends 26 Feb. Optional `stepDue` (same shape as `due`, per step: `{activity:{step:[dates]}}`) gives a step an earlier deadline than its activity, for the on-time count of the grammar and vocabulary block (`stepDueOf` in `teacher.html`); 2 ESO LS2 LP6 uses it (due Thu 26 so Speaking and Talk go after the exam, practice and grammar still by Thu 19). **Homework and calendar (Roger, 31 Oct 2026):** homework is a session line `Homework by Tue 10 · …` in the week row of the class that sets it (the day it is due; the activity's `due` is that day too, and `stepDue` keeps practice, grammar and mixed practice on time by the path's last class). `ELWeek.tasks` reads these lines, the `· self-study` classes and the exam day; students see them in a 🏠 box on their board and in My week, the dashboard shows "Homework to remind" (next 7 days) on the Time plan, and `calendar/<group id>.ics` is the group's calendar feed students subscribe to once ("📅 Add it to my Google Calendar"). **After any change to `el-plan.js`, run `node tools/gen-calendar.js .` and commit the `.ics` files.** **Links to the learning paths (Roger, 1 Nov 2026):** every session and homework line on the dashboard Time plan shows "↗ LP3" (or "↗ Recall checkpoint 2") links that open the activities it names, matched like My week (`lineLinks` in `teacher.html`). **Set as homework (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "if I can't do sth with my students, let me say: set as homework"):** every session line on the dashboard Time plan has "🏠 Set as homework" (text and the day of the next class, editable), saved as `ls/{ls}.homework[group][id] = {text, by, set, at, line}`; it joins the plan's homework in `ELWeek.tasks` (`extra:true`), so students see it in the 🏠 box and My week with a 📅 link that adds that one item to their calendar (the `.ics` feed is static and only has the plan's lines), and the dashboard lists it under "Homework to remind" with ✕ to remove it. **Ready-to-send message (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "when I set as homework I want a message ready for the students telling instructions"):** the dialog writes a message for students (`hwMsg` in `teacher.html`: group, day, numbered steps with the learning path, which steps (the ones the line names, else the first half of the page's tabs for `(1/2)`, the rest for `(2/2)`, all otherwise, from `EL_SECTIONS`) and its full link; English + Catalan by default, or one of them), editable, with 📋 Copy for Classroom; it is saved as `msg` and students open it under the item ("Teacher's instructions") on the board and in My week. Every item of "Homework to remind" has 📋 Message too. **Tabs as homework (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to set tabs of the learning paths as homework"):** the dialog has "Which tabs?" chips (the page tabs from `EL_SECTIONS`) for every learning path the text names; ticked tabs rewrite the text as `LP3 Title: Speaking, Talk` (until it is edited by hand), the message lists only those tabs and its link opens the first one (`page.html#panel`); none ticked = the old rule. Besides the Time plan lines, each learning path heading in the group's "Learning paths and activities" table has "🏠 Homework" (`lphw`, `hwOpen`, `hwTabs`, `hwTabsDone` in `teacher.html`), shown when the group's plan has that LS. **Before an exam when time is tight (Roger, 31 Oct 2026):** the class session runs without the final Blooket, the "together" part is the path's own listening or reading (students answer that step right after), the writing piece stays in class, and Speaking, Talk, Listen again and one go of Shadowing are homework; the per-session plan is the Doc "LS2 classes until the exams" (https://claude.ai/artifact/V3j69yzUEw7Pogz8zHsSPw).
- **Plans around the teacher's absences (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** plans follow Roger's medical appointments in his Google Calendar (a scheduled check every morning). He is away from 2 h before to 1 h after each one; a class he misses becomes a "self-study" session (students work on the Learning Path on their own with the substitute and finish it as homework; tip `self-study`). Never plan checkpoints, forming groups, the sample exam or rehearsals on those days, and ask Roger before planning around an appointment. Session lines are shown to students, so they never name the reason. Exam dates do not move: when time is short, suggest what to make homework or drop.
- **Time plan and week plan (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** each plan in `el-plan.js` also has `exam` (exam day per group, same order as `groups`), `from` on every week row (that week's Monday) and `due` for every activity of the plan (the student board lists an activity in the week of its due date). `shared/js/el-week.js` (`ELWeek`) reads them for both pages. Dashboard: a "📅 Time plan" tab with every group (exam day with countdown, this week and next week) and, at the top of each group, the time plan of the LS on screen with how many students have done each activity due so far ("n late" lists the names on hover); the Teaching plan highlights this week. Student board: "El meu pla setmanal / My week plan" for the LS on screen (exam day, this week's classes and activities with ✔ / Pendent / Endarrerida, the whole plan folded) and a red reminder on top of every late activity (due date past, LS open, activity enabled, not done). "Done" = submitted or marked as done, or every scored step done; the final product (Classroom) is listed but never reminded. Session lines are shown as written (English) with Catalan weekdays for Catalan students. A new plan (2 ESO LS2) only needs its `EL_PLAN` entry with these fields; groups without one show "No time plan yet". Final-product hand-ins: catalogue `steps` + `pieces`, read from the student's synced progress doc. Recall checkpoints show the first try (`parts.first`) next to the score.
- **Teacher checklist, week browser and tips (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** every session line of a plan is a checkbox for the teacher (dashboard Time plan tab and the top of each group), saved in `teacherPlan/{group}.ticks["ls|session line"]` (teacher only, `firestore.rules`); past sessions not ticked show "⚠ n earlier sessions not ticked". **Carry over and weekend check (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: "if I don't tick sth it should appear in the next week and a warning … on Saturday morning"):** a class session before today that is not ticked shows again at the top of the first later week that is not over ("↪ not ticked · Thu 5 Nov", on Saturday and Sunday that is next week); ticking it there ticks the session itself. On Saturday and Sunday a "⚠ Weekend check" box (Time plan tab: every group; group tab: that group) lists them with tick boxes and "🏠 Set as homework". Not carried: `Homework by …` lines, `… moved to …` notes, `· self-study` classes, and sessions set as homework (`homework[group][id].line` = the session line, saved by Set as homework). Code: `pending`, `carryTo`, `lineRow`, `satBox` in `teacher.html`. The Time plan tab has week chips (this week, each later week, "All upcoming") so the teacher can plan ahead. **No 💡 tips on the dashboard any more (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "No tips and I decide what to do")**: `EL_PLAN[ls].tips` is no longer shown; a new LS does not need it.
- **Tabs per session and moved sessions (Roger, 1 Nov 2026):** under each Time plan line the dashboard lists the tabs (`EL_SECTIONS`) of every learning path it names (`dayTabs` in `teacher.html`): tabs named in the group's "Homework by …" lines go home; the rest is the class part, "(1/2)" its first half, "(2/2)" the rest. ✏️ Change moves a line to another day and/or picks other tabs, saved in `ls/{ls}.planEdits[group][original line] = {to, tabs:["activity#panel"]}` (students read it). `ELWeek.applyEdits` rewrites `EL_PLAN[ls]` in place (called by `ELCloud.lsInfo`, `el-todo.js`, the notify scripts and `marks.mjs`): the line gets its new day in that day's week, and `due`/`stepDue` on the old class day move with it. Ticks and 🏠 homework of a moved line keep the ORIGINAL line as key (`origOf`). The static calendar feed (.ics) keeps the plan's days. Set as homework from a line pre-ticks that session's tabs.
- **Tab ticks and ⏭/⏮ (Roger, 1 Nov 2026):** every tab of a class session has a tick box, saved in `teacherPlan/{group}.ticks["ls|line#activity#panel"]`; all tabs ticked = session ticked. "⏭ Next class" / "⏮ Back" send the session to the group's next/previous class in the plan: nothing ticked = the whole line moves (`to`); some ticked = the line keeps the ticked tabs, is ticked, and the rest becomes an added line `planEdits[group]["+id"] = {add, to, from, tabs, shift}` ("LP3 … · not finished on Wed 4"; due days of `shift` activities on `from` move to `to`). Added lines use the "+id" key for ticks/tabs (`origOf`, `p._added`).
- **Single tabs and dropped learning paths (Roger, 1 Nov 2026):** ‹ › next to each unticked tab sends that tab alone to the previous/next class (`sendTo(..., only)`). ✏️ Change → "✂ Drop LPn for <group>" stores `planEdits[group]["drop:<activity id>"] = {drop:true}`: `applyEdits` clears its `due`/`stepDue`/`optional` for that column, removes its "Homework by" lines and lists it in `p._dropped[gi]`; the dashboard's `gradingFor(ls, group)` (G() and the year view) leaves its steps out of every grade part; the Time plan and the student board say "not in the exam and not counted". The exam itself (Forms) is Roger's to adjust; checkpoint locks are unchanged.
- **What we've done (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: "Instead of having a to do list or what we'll do during the week, I want them [to] see what LP and section we've done so far, when I tick them off in my planning"):** the student board's 🗓 My week tab is now "✅ What we've done" (not on LS1, which keeps its old week plan): exam day, dropped paths, "🕘 Last class" and every learning path with the tabs done in class and their day (green ✔ = the student has done it too, otherwise a link to the tab), then the 🏠 homework box. "This week" and the whole plan are gone from it. Data: the dashboard copies the Time plan ticks to `ls/{ls}.done[group] = {"activity#panel": "YYYY-MM-DD"}` (`"activity#"` = an activity without tabs in a ticked session, like a recall checkpoint; day = the session's day) with `doneMap`/`doneSync` in `teacher.html`, 1.5 s after each render, writing only when it changes and only when `teacherPlan` loaded (`tkOk`). Students can't read `teacherPlan`; the ls doc is read anyway, so no extra reads. Games are left out.
- **✅ To do and 📝 Homework (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "When I open a section it must be set as to do … They are only homework if I say so (I can say that a LP is homework or only a section)"; then "I don't [want] students [to] see anything as to do nor the week. I say when to do sth since otherwise is too much info"):** students' To do is ONLY what the teacher has opened for the group (learning paths and tabs, `ls/{id}.open` + `secLocked`/`secOpened`) and they have not finished: `elTodoList` in `el-catalog.js`, used by the board's ✅ To do place (`#todo`, tile + phone bar; also holds "What we've done in class") and by the floating button (`el-todo.js`, now "✅ To do", no week plan, no plan dates). Homework is ONLY what the teacher sets with 🏠 Set as homework (`ELWeek.studentHw` = dashboard items, `extra`): the board's 📝 Homework place, the floating button, the Sunday email and the calendar feed (`gen-calendar.js` = exam + dashboard homework with `--live`). The plan's "Homework by …" and self-study lines are teacher-side ideas only (💡 under "Homework to remind", with a 🏠 Set as homework button). Set as homework saves what it covers, `homework[group][id].tabs = {activity: [panel ids]}` ([] = the whole path; `hwCover`), and opens that path/tabs if they are locked (`hwUnlock`); older items are read from their text (`elHwTargets`). "📋 My activities" (missing work by the plan, for the mark) moved to 📊 My marks. LS1 unchanged. **Same counts everywhere (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "I dont understand why the activities pending dont match", then "here only late activities"):** every count of work to do is in activities (tabs): ✅ To do tile, phone bar and floating button = open tabs not done (`todoTabs`); 📋 My activities shows ONLY ⏰ late work (past its plan day: `elMissing` + `lateList`, the home banner's number) and one line "Plus N activities opened that are not late yet → ✅ To do", so late + opened = the To do number. In ✅ To do (place and floating button) each path has a tag: ⏰ late, 📝 homework by <day> (set as homework), else 🏫 from class. **Only what was due before today's class (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "it only should be on the list what was due before today's class. What I plan to do in class and I put it on the agenda and stays open they think that it's homework and that they have to do it"):** when the group has a Time plan agenda, `elTodoList` keeps only the open tabs that were on a class day BEFORE today (agenda `planEdits[group].agenda.days`, or ticked in class `ls/{id}.done[group]`) or set as homework (from its set day): `elDueBefore` in `el-catalog.js`. Open tabs planned for today, a later day or no day are not To do (they appear in To do the day after their class). A group with no agenda keeps "opened = To do". **Not planned yet = for class (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "If it's to do in class it shouldn't appear here and if they browse it and see it open they can only see it not do it"; "In the to do list only what's done in class and not done and homework"):** with an agenda, a tab of an open path that is on NO class day (nor ticked in class, set as homework, pulled up, or the path planned whole) is treated like a tab for a later class (`laterTabs` in `el-catalog.js`, used by `elSecLockedFor`, `elSecSoon`, `elSecPending`): shown read-only with 🏫 "We'll do it in class" and no day, never To do. **📅 Class agenda (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I understand to do as sth you do on your own … we can add an agenda … where they will see what they are going to do in class, the exam day and whatever I add on the teacher's agenda"):** `agendaBox` in `today.html`, in the ✅ To do place under To do: the group's agenda from today on (learning-path tabs, class games, the teacher's notes) and the exam day. The 🏫 labels say "We'll do it in class", never "To do".
- **Self-study class taught after all (Roger, 2 Nov 2026):** ✕ on a 📚 item in Homework to remind (or ✏️ Change → "Not a self-study class") stores `planEdits[group]["noself:<original line>"] = {noSelf:true}`; `applyEdits` drops "· self-study" from that line (moved or not, `_moved` keeps the original so ticks and tabs stay), so it is a normal class on every board, list and email.
- **Every homework reminder can be deleted (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "I should be able to delete the homework"):** every item of Homework to remind has ✕. Homework set from the dashboard is deleted (`ls/{ls}.homework`); a plan's `Homework by …` line stores `planEdits[group]["nohw:<original line>"] = {noHw:true}` and `applyEdits` removes that line (moved or not) for that group, so it leaves the board, My week, the emails and the feed; `p._nohw[gi]` lists them for "Removed: … ↩ Bring back" under the box (until their day passes).
- **Calendar feeds follow the dashboard (2 Nov 2026):** `.github/workflows/calendar-sync.yml` runs `node tools/gen-calendar.js . --live` every hour 6–21 UTC: it applies every `ls/{id}.planEdits` (removed homework, moved sessions, self-study taught in class, dropped LPs; one Firestore read per LS) and commits `calendar/*.ics` to main only when they changed. Running it without `--live` (after an `el-plan.js` change) writes the plan only; the next sync puts the edits back. Homework set from the dashboard stays out of the feed (students add it with its own 📅 link).
- **📅 Add to your calendar, only on homework, exam and deadlines (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "a button (for the student view) add it to your calendar only on the homework and the exam's and a final product deadline … the old options add it to your calendar no need anymore"):** the students' 📅 Class agenda (`agendaBox` in `today.html`) lists, on their day, the homework the teacher set (🏠), the exam (📝) and every ⏰ deadline, each with a 📅 Add to your calendar button (`ELWeek.gcalDay`: one all-day Google Calendar event). Class work and notes get no button. The teacher adds a deadline with **⏰ Deadline** on any class day of the Time plan (a note `note:⏰ Deadline: …`, default text "Final product"; it moves and deletes like any note). The old "📅 Add it to my Google Calendar" subscribe link and the per-homework 📅 icon are gone from the student board; the .ics feeds still update for anyone already subscribed.
- **📅 Time plan = drag-and-drop agenda, LS2 on (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "on the left I want a list of the LP and SLP (sections of the learning path) and Final Product and exam … on the right my agenda, and I can drag the LP or SLP to the right"; "I should be able to move things around the days and exams and FP too"):** the group's 📅 Time plan place (`agPage` in `teacher.html`) has two columns. Left (`agList`, sticky): the exam and every activity of the LS in catalogue order with its tabs (`EL_SECTIONS`) and games, each tab showing its day, ✓ done, 🏠 homework or a dashed "not planned"; ✂/↩ drops a learning path. Right: the group's class days by `EL_TIMETABLE` week by week (holidays greyed, earlier weeks folded, "⚠ Not ticked yet" for earlier weeks, ＋ one more week), each day a drop zone with its items (tick box, 🔓/🔒 and ✕ on hover), 🏠 Homework, ⏭ Next class (what is not ticked), ✎ Note. Drag a learning path (all its tabs not ticked), one tab, a day's learning path, a note or the exam. **Data:** `ls/{ls}.planEdits[group].agenda` = a JSON string `{v, days: {"YYYY-MM-DD": ["activity#panel", "activity#" (no tabs), "note:text", "self"]}, exam, lab, base, due0, opt0, step0}`, made from the plan the first time a group's Time plan (or Time plan · all groups, or the weekend check) is opened (`agSeed`/`agEnsure`; only when `teacherPlan` loaded); `ELWeek.applyEdits` then rebuilds that group's whole column from it (one line per day, labels from `lab`, "EXAM" on the exam day, `p._tabs[gi]["@day"]`, key `"@day"` in `p._added`, so every reader that already applies planEdits follows: boards, el-todo, emails, marks.mjs, calendar). The plan's "Homework by …" lines and the other edits of that group (moves, added lines, nohw, noself) are folded in and no longer used; `drop:` still works. The exam day students see is `agenda.exam`. Due days: an activity whose last agenda day moved has its `due0`/`opt0`/`step0` days moved by as many of the group's classes (`ELWeek.shiftClass`, `classDays`, `isClass`). Ticks: `teacherPlan/{group}.ticks["ls|@day#item"]` (`"ls|@day#exam"` for the exam) and `"ls|@day"` = every item of the day ticked (`agSave`/`agTick` keep it right, so `pending`, the Saturday email and `ELMark.ready` work unchanged; `ready` falls back to the last class that names the activity before its due day). **Competence bars on top (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "sometimes there will be sections that won't be done … 4 colors in a bar, one for each communicative skill … so if a teacher runs out of time, they can make sure we work those competences equally, and another bar below with the other competences of the LS"):** `agBars` counts the steps of every tab on the agenda (done or to do, dropped learning paths left out) like the whole-year view (`agWork`: a step with n criteria gives 1/n to each, `EL_STEP_CRITERIA`, `elSecSteps`): bar 1 Listening · Speaking · Reading · Writing (CE2–CE5) with ⚠ under 20 %, bar 2 the LS's other competences (CE1, CE6–CE10, transversals); each legend item also says how much of that competence's work in the whole LS is still on the agenda. **Tags per tab (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "could it be possible to know what is worked in each SLP?"):** every tab of the left list and of the class days shows small tags in the bar colours, L S R W for the four skills and the code of the other competences (`agTags`, same count; hover gives names and shares). **👤/👥 Individual or group work (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "a tick in each SLP on the right where I can switch from individual to grupal so that I organise my classes"):** every tab on a class day (and each learning path's header, for all its tabs that day) has a 👤/👥 switch; group tabs are amber. Since 9 Nov 2026 ("I want individually, in pairs and in group and whole class (games are whole class as a default)") the icon opens a small menu of four (SVG icons, `WKS` in teacher.html): individually (white), in pairs (blue), in groups (amber), whole class (purple). `agenda.wk["day|item"]` = "p" | "g" | "c" | "i", saved only when it differs from the default (individually; a class game whole class), so the old "g" values stay groups. Teacher only: students never see it. Same day (Roger: "count homework as individually autonatically and I need a % of the kind of activity on top" + "I need cercles"): a homework tab shows a dashed 👤 (no menu) and counts as individual (agHw/agWkX); a third card on top, "How they work" (wkPie), is a circle with the share of class minutes per grouping: each tab on a day with its ⏱ minutes or 10, 🎬 shadowing whole class with its minutes or 5, dropped learning paths left out. Shadowing = whole class (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "shadowing with the whole class should count as a whole activity"): a learning path's Shadowing tab (panel `shadow`, or `shadowing` in the sample exam; `isShd`) defaults to whole class like a class game (`wkDef`; the menu can still change it, homework still individual), and the 🎬 class shadowing note shows a fixed whole-class icon. The 🔥 Warm-up line on a class day counts as whole-class work too (Roger, 16 Nov 2026: "the warm up activity should be counted as group work (class)"): `wuOn`/`wuMin`/`wuSel` in teacher.html give it a fixed whole-class icon and a ⏱ menu (5 min unless changed, stored as `min["day|warmup"]`, kept by agSave's clean-up while the day has a warm-up), and its minutes go into the day's "of 60 min planned" and into the circle as whole class. Stored in the agenda as `wk: {"day|item": "g"}` (individual = no key), per group and day; `agMove` carries it when an item moves, `agSave` drops keys of items no longer on that day. Teacher only (`ELWeek.applyEdits` ignores it). **🏠 Homework on the class days (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "even if I said that they were homework they should appear there"; "I could say at the end of the class, this SLP is homework for next class … another switch"):** the tabs of a homework (`ls/{ls}.homework[group]`) that are on no class day are put once on the class day it was set (`set`, or the last class before it; `agHwSync`, ids kept in `agenda.hwIn` so a tab taken off later stays off). Every tab on a day has a 🏠 switch: on = a homework `{text, by: next class, set: that day, tabs: {activity: [tab]}, next: true}` (opens the tab like Set as homework); clicking it again deletes that homework; a tab already in another homework shows 🏠 on and points to Homework to remind. **🎬 Group shadowing (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "make it possible to add shadowing grupal (the video of the unit)"):** the left list has "🎬 Shadowing · whole class" (only when the LS has `EL_LS[].shadowing`), dragged to as many days as wanted; on a day it is the note `SHD` ("note:🎬 Shadowing with the whole class", never with " · " since plan lines split on it) with a ▶ link to the video, and the day counts as a shadowing day in bar 1. Since 13 Nov 2026 (Roger: "I want to copy it not moved … I do shadowing almost every day") dragging the 🎬 Shadowing from a class day to another day of the same group COPIES it (with its ⏱ minutes; `agDrop`), the original stays; ✕ or dragging it back to the list on the left takes it off. A new LS needs nothing extra. LS1 keeps the old list.
- **Order inside a class day (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to change the order here by dragging up or down"):** a game, a note (🎬 Shadowing, ✎ Note) or a learning path block dragged up or down inside its own day goes where the blue line shows (agReorder). Tabs (SLPs) never move on their own: on every day they show in the learning path's order (agTabOrder; Roger: "SLP should always be in order, the only thing that I can move are the LP and games"). The day then keeps that order: agenda `man: {"YYYY-MM-DD": 1}` and `days[d]` in that order; a learning path with something between its tabs shows its heading twice. Other days still group tabs in the path's order (agRuns). The students' line for the day follows the order. Dragging to another day works as before.
- **A tab out of its box + ⧉ Copy to the other group (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to take a SLP out of the box, in this case the shadowing … and have a copy button that only paste onto the other group of the same level"):** a tab dragged up or down inside its own day leaves its learning path's box and stands on its own where the blue line shows ("LP3 · Shadowing", dashed; agenda `solo: {"day|item":1}`, goes with the tab to other days); ⤺ puts it back. This is the only exception to "tabs keep the path's order". "⧉ Copy to <other group>" on a class day (and ⧉ on a learning path heading) opens a small window: tick what to copy and the other group's class (default = the same class of the week, 2nd ↔ 2nd). Only the other group of the same course (EL_GROUPS course); copies keep order, ⏱ minutes, 👤/👥 and "out of the box", not ticks or homework; items already there are skipped; copied paths and tabs open for that group, like a drop (agCpDlg/agCpGo in teacher.html).
- **Minutes per item on a class day (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to set the time I want to spend on each SLP … like 5 minute review"):** a ⏱ menu right after each item's tick box (5–60 min or Other…); agenda `min: {"day|item": minutes}`, moves with the item. The day shows "⏱ N of 60 min planned" (EL_TIMETABLE.len), orange when over; a learning path heading shows its tabs' total. Teacher only.
- **📋 Plan both groups side by side (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "edit both courses at the same time … the learning path on the left for 2ESO A and on the right for 2ESO B"; "I see it like it is and then I go to a board"):** the group Time plan stays as it is; "📋 Plan 2ESO A + 2ESO B side by side" above it and "📋 Plan <course>" buttons in Time plan · all groups open place `plan2` (`tp2Page` in `teacher.html`): each group of the course is a column with its own full agenda (`agPage`, list + class days, every rule unchanged), side menu hidden (`body.tp2on`), ⛶ browser full screen, ✕ Close back to the group Time plan. Drags only drop inside their own group (`data-gid`). Same LS for both (the group's current LS). **Exception (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "when I drag them from a group to another it makes a copy"):** a note on a class day (✎ Note, 🎬 Shadowing) dropped on a class day of the other group is COPIED there where the blue line shows (with its ⏱ minutes; `agCopy`), the original stays; the same note already on that day = nothing. Learning paths, tabs, games and the exam never cross groups.
- **📊 Student progress beside the plan (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I need the option to see on the left what I already see and on the right this"):** the 📋 board has "📊 Student progress beside" (`S.tp2prog`): left the plan (chips Plan · Both / each group, `S.tp2one`), right the Learning paths and activities table of 👥 Students (the same `lpAll` box, students mode) for one group (chips Students · switch `S.g`, as the table reads the group on screen), sticky with its own scroll and the Size / Fold tools. A group's 📅 Time plan has "📊 Plan with student progress" (opens it with that group alone). ✕ Close turns it off. **◧ Hide Route, Points… columns (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "I need to be able to hide these columns"):** one button in that table hides every column between the name and the first learning path (Route, Points, All work, Bonus, Class games, Exam; the LS summaries in Both LSs), kept per computer in `localStorage.el_t_nosum`; `sumApply()` marks the cells after each render by their place in the header (`.sumh`, `table.nosum`), so new summary columns are hidden too as long as they sit before the first `th.lp`. **🔤 Short names (Roger, 9 Nov 2026):** a second button turns the Student column into a narrow square cell with the first name and the first letter of the surname (`shortName(s).short`, full name on hover), without 📝 notes or game circles; kept in `localStorage.el_t_shortnm` (`table.shortnm`). **🏫 Only what we've done (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "here, I only want the activities that we've done"):** by default the table shows only the tabs done in class, the same test as the missing work and ⏰ late counts (`elDueBefore` in `el-catalog.js`: a class day of the group's Time plan agenda before today, ticked as done in class, or set as homework; today's class about an hour after it opens). `lpTaught(a,dbs)` in `teacher.html` gives null (every column: no agenda for that LS, the path planned whole, LS1, the final product, or 📋 All activities), false (nothing done yet: the path leaves the table, and a note above it says how many) or the Set of panel ids; `lpVis` keeps those columns with their index in `lpCols` (the cell's `data-c`, so the step dialog opens the right tab). A path shown in part gets "🏫 N of M tabs done in class so far" under its title and its Score counts only those tabs' steps (`scDone`; ✅ when all of them are done; the whole path in the tooltip). Total and the LS summaries still count every path. "📋 All activities" / "🏫 Only done in class" switches it, kept per computer in `localStorage.el_t_allacts`. **LS1 learning paths hidden (Roger, 23 Nov 2026):** in students mode `progress()` drops every LS1 (`isLS1`) activity but its final product; in Both LSs an LS with `gradingFor(...).only` shows the LS mark as its summary Total (`lsCell(s,g)` under `withLs`). **🗓 Term mark column (Roger, 23 Nov 2026):** in 👥 Students (`S.place==="students"`, term `TN` from `S.term`) the table is drawn from entries `{a, cols, part, r}` plus `{mark:true}`: each learning path's visible columns (`lpVis`) are split by the term of each tab (`tabDay`: the earliest tick day from `tickDays(ls,g)` for its step keys or panel; `dayTerm`: a closed term ends on its `closeDay`, else the calendar term `termOf`; a tab not ticked takes today's term). Columns of term `TN` go left of the mark, later ones right (header "→ Term n"), earlier ones are left out; a path split in two gets a Score per part (`scDone(…, cols, label)`). The final product goes by its grading item's term (`lsItems`, `EL_PLAN[ls].report.fp`); columns with no tabs stay left. The mark is `byComp(marksOf(s,items))` of the term's English items, as `yearView`. `termLss(lss,t,gid)` adds to a term's LS chips (and Both LSs, `stuterm`, `lspick`) an earlier LS with a grading item in that term, so LS2 shows again in Term 2 with only its Term 2 parts. `sumApply` stops at `th.tmk` too. **Weeks scroll under the list (Roger, 9 Nov 2026: "scroll up and that the weeks go up and see further days and seeing this"):** beside the table each group column (`.tp2s .tp2col`) is one screen high and sticky; its skills bars and LP list stay on top and only `.agr` (the weeks) scrolls, so an LP can be dragged to a later day. A redraw keeps the scroll of each `.agr` and of the table (`inTop` in `render`).
- **🏠 on a tab that is part of a bigger homework (Roger, 7 Nov 2026):** clicking it takes only that tab off, no alert: the homework is saved again under a new id with its other tabs (a merge write cannot drop a key) and the tab name is taken out of an "LPn …: a, b, c" text; no tabs left = the homework goes.
- **No class work shown to students (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "students still see to do activities and then they see activities in blue, because we have to do them in class, this shouldnt appear"):** replaces the blue 🏫 look below. A tab for a later class (`elSecSoon`) is now HIDDEN on the learning path (`lockSections` in `el-cloud.js`: `wait`, like a locked tab) until its class opens it (the 5-minutes-before timer, the every-minute check, class mode `elSecFree`); its steps still count in the path's progress (not in `EL_HIDDEN_STEPS`), so a path never looks finished with tabs to come. A tab the student had started and the teacher moved later stays shown, frozen, with a plain grey note (no blue). On the board: 📈 My learning paths table shows no 🏫 chips or legend to students (a path with only class tabs left is not listed; state "▶ Open" / "✔ Up to date" instead of "We'll do it in class"; the teacher view keeps 🏫); the 📅 Agenda under To do shows only the exam, homework, ⏰ deadlines and the teacher's 📌 notes (no 📘 learning paths or 🎲 class games); the floating ✅ To do has no "🏫 from class" tag.
- **🏫 To do in class (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "students can see it but can't write any answer nor play any video until it's class time"; "a blue shadow and a legend saying to do in class"; "if I make changes, and students did sth beforehand, we froze the page until the calendar allows to edit but their answers get saved"):** inside an open learning path, a tab the Time plan puts on a later class day (`elPlanLater` tabs) is **shown, not hidden**: `elSecSoon(a, info, gid, done)` (el-catalog.js) gives `{panel: day}` and `lockSections` (el-cloud.js) draws it with a blue shadow, 🏫 on its tab and the note "To do in class · Tue 10 Nov", read-only, with no audio, video or YouTube playing (also when the student already answered some of it: their answers stay and open again on that day). It counts in the path's tabs (LP page progress, LS page, 📊 My marks chips "🏫 Listening · Tue 10 Nov"), so a path never looks done with tabs still to come; it is never in ✅ To do or the "next" step until its day (`elSecLockedFor` still lists it). Tabs locked with the section switches stay hidden. The 📊 My marks state of a path still to do says "🏫 To do in class", or "📝 Homework by …" only when the teacher set it as homework; the plan's day shows in the teacher's view only. **5 minutes before the class (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "cannot be unfrozen 5 minutes before the class?"):** what the agenda puts on today opens 5 minutes before the group's first class that day by its timetable (`elClassOpenAt` / `elTTFor` in el-catalog.js: live/{group}.tt, kept by el-cloud.js in `window.EL_LIVE_TT` and `localStorage el_tt_<group>`, else `EL_TIMETABLE`), not in the morning; until then it shows "🏫 To do in class · today at 10:30". An open LP page unfreezes those tabs by itself at that moment (no reload). A student page left open from an earlier day reloads when it is shown again (`newDayReload` in el-cloud.js, pending work sent first; not the dashboard, checkpoints, sample exam or followed presentations), so it runs the latest code (problem report, 2ESOA, 13 Nov 2026). No class that day by the timetable: it opens with the day, as before. A whole learning path planned only for later is still hidden.
- **Homework planned ahead (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I want to plan that that will be homework"):** 🏠 on a tab of a later class day saves the homework with set = that day; students get it (board, To do, Sunday email, calendar, tabs freed by elPlanLater) only from that day (ELWeek.studentHw and elPlanLater skip set > today). The dashboard says "planned: students see it from …".
- **Save indicator on the teacher dashboard (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I was organizing the week and it didn't hit firebase"):** every Firestore write goes through SAVE.track (wrapped F.setDoc/updateDoc/deleteDoc/batch commit); bottom-left pill "Saving…" → "✓ Saved", orange "⚠ Not saved yet · don't reload" when a write is unconfirmed after 4 s; beforeunload asks while anything is unsaved; offline (navigator.onLine false) shows "📴 Offline · changes are not saved until you are back online".
- **No ticks needed (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "If a student do sth that's all. It's done and ticked. And they can do it if it's open. Now the SLPs will be automatically open the day the group has class and it's on my plan"; "If I dont do sth in class I drag it to the next day and that's all"):** (1) **Opening on the class day:** dragging a learning path or tab onto a day still opens it at rules level (`agOpenSync`), but students see it closed until that day: `elPlanLater` (el-catalog.js) reads the group's agenda from `ls/{id}.planEdits[group].agenda` and `elLpOpen` / `elSecLockedFor` / `elSecPending` treat a path or tab whose agenda items are all on later days as not open yet (page-only, like section switches). Homework set from the dashboard (`homework[group][id].tabs`) and a pull-up open it at once; the sample exam is never held back (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "I want them to see the sample exam"). Anything that writes the open switches must use the raw state: `elLpOpen(a, info, gid, true)`, and the dashboard sets `window.EL_RAW_OPEN = true`. (2) **Past days are done:** `ELWeek.autoTicks` counts every item still on an agenda day before today as ticked (in memory, never written; a box unticked by hand saves false and stays unticked). The dashboard (`autoTicks()` before each render, `S.autoTk` so moving a not-done item never carries a tick), `notify.mjs` (Saturday email) and `marks.mjs` use it, and the board's "What we've done" follows through `doneSync`. What was not done is dragged to a later day. (3) Learning-path level steps are `atOnce` (see the marking rule above).
- **Groups shown in Time plan · all groups (Roger, 2 Nov 2026):** chips All / 2 ESO / 4 ESO / one per group (`S.tpg`, kept in localStorage `el_t_tpg`, at least one group).
- **Class games in the Time plan (Roger, 1 Nov 2026):** each learning path's games (`EL_GAMES` in `lab/game/decks/index.js`, matched by course, LS title and path name: `gamesOf`) are items "game:<deck>" next to its tabs, in the path's first class ((1/2) or no half, never homework): tick, ‹ ›, ✏️ Change like tabs; the link opens `lab/game/host.html?deck=`. A new deck with `ls`/`lp` set appears by itself. Never in student messages. Since 16 Nov 2026 (Roger: "here, the new format of the game should appear") the items keep their `game:<deck>` keys but show as 🧩 Grammar · LSn / 📚 Vocabulary · LSn (grammar first) and open the LS game, `host.html?deck=<deck>&ls=<EL_LS id>&kind=` (also from 📋 Today in class mode, `el-cm-agenda.js`); ✓ played = `ls/{id}.played[group][deck]`, i.e. the LS game played with this path as its newest ticked path. No 🕵️ Code Breakers item is offered any more (the board asks Quiz / Code Breakers); an old `game:cb-` item on a class day still shows and opens.
- **Floating weekly to-do list (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** `shared/js/el-todo.js`, loaded by `el-cloud.js` for every signed-in student and the tester, so it is on every page that loads `el-cloud.js` (board, course pages, LS pages, activities, English Lab). A "📋 My week / La meva setmana" button (bottom right, above the bottom bar, with the number of things to do) opens the current week of the plan the group follows now (`ELWeek.now`): exam countdown, late activities from earlier weeks, then one heading per class day with the activities worked on that day (named in the session line, by title or as `LPn` next to a title elsewhere in the plan, with `(1/2)`) or due that day (✔ Done / To do / Late, Open), and the rest of the line as "In class:". Optional activities go in `optional` in `el-plan.js` (same shape as `due`): shown as “homework” (never “optional”, since students are not told how activities count), never late, not counted. Shown only while the LS is open; Catalan for Insecure; it opens by itself once each new week and otherwise remembers open or closed (`el_todo_open`, `el_todo_week`). A new LS needs nothing extra beyond its `EL_PLAN` entry; write session lines as `Mon 2 · LP2 Title (1/2) · …` so the days and activities are found.
- **Assessment on the dashboard (LSs in `EL_GRADING`):** level buttons NA · AS · AN · AE (indicator on hover; a second click clears) in the activity dialog for each level moment, saved at once as `results/{activity}.levels[step][criterion]`. Final product group rows: the group name is `results/{fp}.team`; a group level is written to every member's `levels.group[criterion]`, a new member copies the group's levels, and `down[criterion]` gives one student one level less. Exam marks per section and the writing level, and the degrees the teacher confirms, live in one record per student and LS: `results/{ls}__assessment` (`activity: "{ls}/assessment"`, `sections[k] = {score, max}`, `levels.writing`, `final[criterion]`). The LS grade box (blocks and weighted total, with a "Missing work: counts 0 / not counted yet" switch) and the result per criterion (suggested degree, confirmed with ✓) read them. LS2 pages record each step's first check (`state.tries[step]`, plus `first` for recall warm-ups and checkpoints) and when it was completed (`state.doneAt[step]`) in the synced progress; "on time" = done by the end of the activity's day in `EL_PLAN[ls].due`. **Retries (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** the first try counts; LS2 pages also save each step's latest check (`state.last[step]`, recall too), and the dashboard shows it in the step cell as "first → latest" (for example 1/8 → 7/8; one score when they are equal) and in the activity dialog's "Latest" column. It is never counted. **Students are never told which activities are assessed (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: “I don’t want students to know that checkpoints are the ones I will use”, so they don’t copy or cheat in them):** every LS2 page shows the same note above its tabs (`trackNote` in `ls2-engine.js`, Catalan for Insecure, English for Safe and Daring): the teacher keeps track of all the activities, some will be assessed and make up their continuous assessment, which is part of the mark; do each activity on your own and without notes the first time; finish on time. The student board (`today.html`) says the same under the title. Never show a per-step or per-activity “this counts for your mark”, “optional: counts only if it raises your mark” or “first try” next to a checkpoint on student pages; the weight of continuous assessment in the mark is still to be decided with Roger, so never write a percentage. The teacher dashboard, the plan tips and the docs keep saying which steps count. **Recall checkpoints open the way (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** an activity that comes after a recall checkpoint in its LS opens only when every earlier checkpoint is done (`checkpoint:true` in `el-catalog.js`, `elCheckpointMissing`; catalogue order = LS page order). `el-cloud.js` blocks the page with an “Open Recall checkpoint n →” button and the board shows 🔒 instead of “Open”. A checkpoint the teacher has not enabled never blocks, so enable it together with the Learning Path after it; the final product is never blocked; teacher and testers (outside “View as”) are never blocked. Checkpoints 2 and 3 are “homework” for students, not “optional” (in the mark they still only count if they raise it). On the dashboard a not-graded learning path's Score cell is steps done / steps, not a mark. The teacher writes all of this, so `firestore.rules` needed no change. **Learning paths open per group (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: “so they don't do them when I don't ask for it”):** every activity of an LS (warm-up, learning paths, checkpoints, sample exam, final product, reflect) is Open or 🔒 Locked for each group, set on the dashboard in “🔓 Learning paths open for <group>” at the top of the group's tab (one switch per activity, Open all / Lock all), stored as `ls/{id}.open[group]` (activity ids). It replaces the old per-LS “enabled” switch of graded activities: a group without the list keeps the old rule (practice open, graded only if in `ls/{id}.enabled`), and the first click saves the full list for that group. `elLpOpen` in `el-catalog.js` is the single check (board, week plan, reminders, page gate, checkpoints: a locked checkpoint does not block, so open it together with the learning path after it). A locked activity shows “🔒 encara no està oberta / not open yet” on the board and a block on its page, which saves nothing. The dashboard also writes `locked[group]` and `lockedKeys[group]` (`elLpLocks`: the activity's keys and key + "Level"); `firestore.rules` refuse a result for a locked activity and a progress write that changes a locked key, and `el-cloud.js` never sends those keys. Testers outside “View as” and a Closed LS (review) are not affected. **Sections inside a learning path (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: “even in the learning path, I want to open these sections as I teach”):** the same box has a “Sections” column with one ✔/🔒 chip per section tab (open all / lock all per learning path), stored as `ls/{id}.secLocked[group][activity id with "__"]` = panel ids; none listed = every section open. The section list comes from `shared/js/el-sections.js` (`EL_SECTIONS`), generated by `node tools/gen-sections.js .` from each page's tabs (`data/*.js` panels for LS2 engine pages, `.tab[data-panel]` buttons otherwise): **run it again whenever a page adds, removes or renames a section, and for every new LS.** **Locked sections are read-only, not hidden (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: “if they are locked they only see them, and if they already did them they see the last answered version with the correction … like their notes”):** `lockSections` in `el-cloud.js` puts 🔒 on the tab (it still opens), a note on top of the panel, and disables everything in it (inputs, Check, Do it again, cards) except audio, video, speed buttons and hints, re-applied when the page re-renders; the board shows 🔒 on those steps (a `vocab` section holds the cards and practice steps, `elSecSteps`). **Opened sections stay on the to-do (Roger, same day: “students should have these sections in the to do list until they finish”):** the dashboard also keeps every section ever opened, `secOpened[group][activity]`; a section opened before and locked again stays doable for a student who has not finished it (`elSecLockedFor`), and the board's learning-paths table on 📈 links every tab not done of an open section to `page.html#panel` (`openHashTab`); the old “📌 Sections to finish” box was removed (Roger, 2 Nov 2026; `elSecPending` in el-catalog.js is no longer used by the board). Progress is one JSON string per page, so `firestore.rules` cannot check sections: the lock is on the page only.
- **Open and close sections (Roger, 26 Oct + 1 Nov 2026):** three switches write the same data, `ls/{id}.secLocked[group][activity with __]` (locked panel ids) and `secOpened` (every section opened so far): the Sections column of "🔓 Learning paths open for …" on the dashboard, the ✔/🔒 next to each tab in the Time plan (`secSw`, teacher.html; ✔ = open, like the Sections column, since the open padlock 🔓 read as "still locked" (Roger, 11 Nov 2026); no switch on an optional path such as the Sample exam, which is always open), and, in teacher mode on a learning path page, the "Sections open for" bar with a 🔓/🔒 on every tab (`teacherSections`, el-cloud.js; it loads el-sections.js itself, warns and offers "Open it" when the whole LP is locked for the group). LS1 pages get no bar.
- **What each learning path covers, and its tabs on 🧭 Learning situations (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "here, I also need to see the SLP of each LP"; "and here too with a summary of the grammar, vocab, and things we do like there columns on both places"):** "🔓 Learning paths open for <group>" (`lpBox`) and 🧭 Learning situations › one LS (`lssPage`) have three columns, **Vocabulary · Grammar · What we do** (`lpSum`, `lpSumCells`, `LPSUM_TH` in `teacher.html`), built at run time from the step titles of the learning path page (`EL_STEP_TITLES`, `tools/gen-sections.js`): Vocabulary = the `vocab` step (+ 💬 `vocabEx`), Grammar = every typed `grammar*` step, What we do = 📖 reading, 🎧 listening, 📋 hand-in steps, ☑️ other quizzes, ✍️ writing, 🗣 speaking (recall, mixed, listen again, shadowing and Talk are left out); a checkpoint shows its own title; LS1 pages, the book, the sample exam, the final product and Reflect show "–". A renamed step shows at once after `node tools/gen-sections.js .`. The Learning situations table also has a **Sections** column (Roger, 21 Nov 2026, after a first version with coloured chips and 🔒/🏫 icons: "I dont understand the colors not the "homework" labels"): one line per tab (`EL_SECTIONS`) with its state in plain words, `stW`: **Open** (green), **Locked** (grey, the section switches), **In class + day** or **Not planned yet** (blue, kept by the Time plan for a later class or on no class day, hidden from students until then); one word when every group has the same state, else "A: Open B: Locked"; only the groups that have the path open; a path open for no group lists its tabs in grey; the sample exam says "Every tab always open". State = `tabState` (`elSecLocked` + `elLaterTabs`, asked on purpose because the dashboard reads the switches raw with `EL_RAW_OPEN`). A legend line sits above the table (`.lslegend`). No new Firestore reads.
- **Student page (`today.html`):** **language by route (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** Catalan for Insecure, English for Safe and Daring, following the student's route in the LS on screen (another LS's route while it has none; Catalan with no route at all); the teacher and testers see it in English (`t(ca,en)` in the page). Criteria texts stay in their official wording. The same menu as Home and English Lab (side menu on a computer, top menu on a phone): the board, the student's course page with the LSs they can open, and English Lab (teacher and tester: both courses); a box "Les meves Learning Situations" with one button per LS the student can open. LS tabs (assigned LSs, open first), one grid per learning path (its steps with their criteria, a row of ✔, the score cell that opens a detail window with steps, feedback and submit, and an "Obre" button), the LS total; each grid has a folded legend with only the criteria that appear in it. A second tab, "Resum dels meus criteris", lists each criterion with its text, points and a mark out of 10 (points of the graded activities linked to it), and the average of those marks as an orientative mark. **Study summary (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** an LS with `study` in `EL_LS` (start of the PDF path; the board adds the route + `.pdf`) shows a green box under the LS bar, "Obre el resum del vocabulari i la gramàtica per estudiar-los! / Open the summary of the vocabulary and grammar to study them!", with only the student's route PDF (nothing until they have a route; teacher and testers get all three). It is optional and not graded. **The same PDF is always on the LS page (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** the *Language & Knowledge* tab of `<ls>/index.html` has an empty `<article class="bigcard" data-el-study hidden>` that `el-cloud.js` (`studyCard`) fills with the student's route PDF (teacher and testers: all three); every new LS with a summary adds that card and `study` in `EL_LS`. Under the final product it shows each hand-in's self-assessment and comment with the teacher's answer, which the teacher writes in the dashboard (stored as `results/{activity}.replies[step]`; unanswered comments appear in the teacher's To do and as ✉️). **Comments that need no answer (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** a comment that only says there is no problem ("no", "none", "I don't have any problem", "cap", "no tinc cap problema"…, `elNoComment` in `el-catalog.js`) is not listed and gets no ✉️; "No reply needed" (To do and the hand-in dialog) stores `results/{activity}.seen[step]` and takes any other comment off the list without telling the student. The box on handed-in steps asks for a question or problem and says to leave it empty if everything went well.
- **Public home (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** signed-out visitors see only Home and English Lab in the home nav; Home and `lab/` share one menu style (side menu on a computer, top menu on a phone). Courses and Learning Situations (`data-private` in `index.html` and `lab/index.html`) appear only for the teacher and the tester (not in a group's student view). Home has two buttons, 2 ESO and 4 ESO, that open the sign-in; the student then lands on their board, with its menu to their course and LSs (the clicked course is kept as `el_pick`; if it is not the student's course, the board says which one is). Course pages (`2eso/`, `4eso/`) show the "Sign in" overlay to signed-out visitors. English Lab has its own page, `lab/index.html` (old `index.html#lab` links redirect there): a visitor chooses a level (2 ESO / 4 ESO, kept as `el_lab_level`) and signs in; a student then sees the English Lab of their own course, and the teacher and the tester can switch level. Students stay on `lab/` after signing in (`onLab` in `el-cloud.js`) and reach it from the bottom bar. **Student side menu (Roger, 4 Nov 2026):** on the side and phone menus (`lab/`, `lab/practice.html`) a student, the teacher's student view and the tester in a group's view get Current Learning Situation (the board's current LS: `ELWeek.now` by the time plan, else the first open one the group can open), All Learning Situations (their course page, whose LS cards `lsMenu` also trims to the group's), My activities and English Lab; Home, Courses and Learning Situations are hidden (`sideMenu` in `el-cloud.js`, same cached `ls/{id}` reads, no new ones). The teacher's student view on `lab/` shows the student's course, not the level switch.
- **Student sign-in (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** students sign in with their **school email and a password** (Firebase Email/Password), not with Google: the school's Google sign-in failed for them and Roger does not want to depend on the school. The window is `shared/js/el-signin.js` (`signIn({auth,A,domain})`, used by `el-cloud.js` and `el-deck-points.js`): email (the part before @ is enough; the domain is added) + password, errors in English and Catalan, and a "Teacher: sign in with Google" link (the teacher's Gmail keeps Google; any other Google account is signed out and told to use its password). On `teacher.html` the Sign in button opens Google directly (`teacherLogin` in `el-cloud.js`). The accounts come from the student list: `tools/student-accounts/student-accounts.mjs` (run on Roger's PC, signed in to the Firebase CLI as the project owner) reads `roster/`, creates an account per listed email plus the testers (an email that already had a Google account keeps its uid and progress and only gets the password; accounts are marked verified because the rules need `email_verified`), turns Email/Password on, and writes in a folder **outside the repo** (`C:\Developer\dades-alumnes`): `student-passwords.csv` (kept between runs: a password only changes if its row is deleted), `send-passwords.gs` (Apps Script run from roger.beltran@ietramuntana.cat that emails each student their login and password once) and `password-slips.html` (printable slips per group, copyright stamped). A new student on the list: import the list, run the script again, send/print only theirs. **The CSV and the generated files are personal data: never commit them.** Rules and group mapping are unchanged (`roster/{email}` still decides who can sign in and their group).
- **Student access:** right after signing in, a student (the tester too, in any view) goes to their board (`today.html`), except on English Lab (`lab/`), where they stay; the teacher goes from Home to the dashboard and stays on any other page (`login` in `el-cloud.js`). **Board link on every page (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** `menuLink` in `el-cloud.js` adds “My activities” (“Les meves activitats” on an Insecure LS page) right after the Home link of each page's own menu for a signed-in student, and “Dashboard” for the teacher: header menu (`.navlinks`, course and LS pages), side and phone menus (`.nav-tabs`, `.mobile-tabs`: Home, English Lab), top bar of activity pages (`.lp-home-link`) and `.site-header .home-link` (vocabulary games). A new page gets it for free if its menu uses one of these classes and it loads `el-cloud.js`. Students can open only their board, English Lab, their own course page and the LSs assigned to their group; any other page sends them back to the board, and an LS not assigned shows a block. The teacher assigns LSs per group in the dashboard (LS settings → "Groups that can use it", stored as `ls/{id}.groups`; without it, every group of the LS course). `elLsForGroup` in `el-catalog.js` is the single check, and `firestore.rules` (`lsForMe`) enforces it for progress and results. There is no Public status any more (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: every student has their own account, and a Public LS saved nothing): the dashboard offers Hidden, Open and Closed, and an LS still stored as `public` counts as Open (`lsSt` in `el-cloud.js`, `el-todo.js`, `lsOpen` in `firestore.rules`). **Tester account** `roger.beltran@ietramuntana.cat` (`TESTERS` in `shared/js/el-firebase.js` and `isTester()` in `firestore.rules`): signs in as a student without being on the student list and, by default, opens every page and every LS, including hidden ones and learning paths not open yet, so new LSs need no extra setup for it. A selector in its bottom bar (“View as 2ESO A…”) switches it to the student view of one group: the board, course pages and LS gate then behave exactly as for a listed student of that group (`viewGroup`/`seesAll` in `el-cloud.js`; kept in that browser as `el_view`, no rules change). In a group's view a second selector picks a level ("2ESO Starter (A1)"…, Pro only for 4 ESO; Roger, 1 Nov 2026): it replaces the tester's own route everywhere (`viewLevel` → `window.EL_VIEW_LEVEL`, read first by `elRouteOf`; kept as `el_view_level`, "" = own route). It appears in every group on the dashboard as "Beltran Porres, Roger" (`TESTER_NAME` in `teacher.html`, since it is not on the list) and is not counted in the totals. Pages that do not load `el-cloud.js` (flashcards, lab, examples, rubrics) are not gated. A signed-out visitor on an LS page gets a full-screen "Sign in" overlay with its own sign-in button (`signInBlock`), removed on sign-in; the bottom bar (`#el-cloud-bar`, z-index 10002) stays above every overlay so sign-in, sign-out and the tester's view switch are always reachable.
- **Routes (Roger, 26 Oct 2026; 30 Oct 2026: "I am deciding it now and they can't change that, only ask for it through their dashboards"):** the teacher sets every route, from each student's **LS1 exam mark**; students never choose one. One route per student and LS in `students/{uid}/levels/{ls}` = `{level}`. **Route requests (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** on the board (`today.html`, next to "El teu nivell") a student presses "Demana canviar de ruta", picks any route but Pro (`EL_LEVELS` without `ask:false`) and can add a reason; it is saved in the LS on screen as `request`, `reason`, `requestedAt` (merge, so a doc may hold only a request: `elRouteOf` ignores docs without `level`). The board then shows "pending" (or "declined" after a refusal). The dashboard shows 🔁 in the Route cell and a line in To do with Accept / Decline: accepting sets the route (`setLevel`, which clears the request and sends the level notice), declining keeps it and saves `declined`. `firestore.rules`: a student may only create or update their own `levels/{ls}` touching `request`, `reason`, `requestedAt` (route beginners/insecure/safe/daring, reason ≤ 300 chars, LS open and theirs); level, notice fields and `by` are teacher-only. **Carry-over:** an LS with no route of its own takes the latest route set in an earlier LS of the same course (`elRouteOf(lv, ls)` in `el-catalog.js`, EL_LS order), so keep `EL_LS` in teaching order per course; every reader (`el-cloud.js`, `today.html`, `teacher.html`, `el-todo.js`, `el-deck-points.js`) uses it. **No route yet:** the LS page shows the Base (insecure) version in Catalan with a banner saying the teacher will set the level (nothing is saved); the board shows "Encara no en tens…"; testers see every route button. **Route before the first sign-in (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** a student on the list who has not signed in yet gets a clickable Route cell in the dashboard grid; the route is saved on the list as `roster/{email}.route = {level, ls}` (the LS on screen; ⏳ while waiting; kept by a list re-import). On their first sign-in `el-cloud.js` writes `levels/{ls}` = `{level, changed, was:null, why:null}` if that doc does not exist yet, so the level notice shows; `firestore.rules` lets a student create that doc only when it matches their roster entry exactly. **Old student choices:** from 26 to 30 Oct 2026 2 ESO students without a route could pick Base, Core or Plus (`{level, by:"student"}` in the course's first LS). That choice, the "✋" mark and the rule allowing it were removed on 30 Oct 2026; a leftover `by:"student"` route still counts (`elRouteOf` returns `own:true`, shown nowhere) until the teacher sets one, and a route the teacher set in any LS of the course wins over it. Every teacher write (`setLevel`, `setLevels`) deletes `by`. 4 ESO students never chose. **Dashboard:** the folded box "🧭 Routes from the LS1 exam" in each group tab takes the course's first LS with an exam in `EL_GRADING` as source (exam mark = sum of section scores ÷ sum of maxes × 10), suggests a route with the course's cut-offs (`EL_ROUTE_CUTS` in `el-catalog.js`, Roger 30 Oct 2026: Starter under 3, Base under 6, Core under 8, Plus from 8; Pro is never given by a mark, a Pro student keeps Pro unless Roger picks another route; Roger can change them there, saved in `settings/routes` = `{2eso:{beginners,insecure,safe}, 4eso:{…}}`) and shows a preview (exam mark, route now, new route he can change, a tick per student: ticked when it changes and all exam sections are entered); "Apply and notify" writes the ticked ones to the chosen later LS in one batch; "Tick everyone with an exam mark" also ticks the unchanged ones so they are told their level. **Level notice (Roger, 30 Oct 2026: "if I ever change a level students need to receive a notification"):** every teacher route write (`setLevel`, `setLevels`) adds `changed` (server time), `was` (route before, `elRouteOf`) and `why` (`"exam"` from this box) to `levels/{ls}`; `shared/js/el-level-notice.js` (started by `el-cloud.js`) shows the student a card once for the latest one in their course, in the new route's language, and "Got it" saves `students/{uid}.levelSeen[course]` = that time; the dashboard's route window shows "🔔 Notice sent … · seen ✓ / not seen yet". A tool that writes routes outside the dashboard must add the same fields or students are not told. One student's route is still set by clicking their Route cell (↩ = carried over from an earlier LS). **A route change mid-LS keeps earlier work:** steps already completed keep their marks from the old route, and new steps are done and corrected at the new route. This holds because points and ✔ count every completed step in the progress, whatever route it was done in (`elDone`/`elDoneSet` walk every `done` object, including LS1 pages' per-route state), and LS2 pages share `state.done` across routes and keep the route of each first try (`tries[step].level`). Keep it that way: never reset `done` when the route changes. **Checked answers follow the question (Roger, 3 Nov 2026: "si faig un canvi de nivell a mig LP l'alumne ha de guardar les activitats que hagi fet"):** each route sees its own list of questions, so answers saved by position landed on other questions after a level change (a Base step done 9 / 9 showed 6 / 17 with "Not yet" at Core). `state.ans[id]` = `{v, tries, l: route, q: [question id or text]}` (`keep`/`kept`/`typed` in both `ls2-engine.js`; `keepAns`/`moveAns` in 4 ESO `getting-started.html`); a restore at another route puts each answer back on the same question, leaves the route's new questions empty and adds a 🔁 note (a done step stays "complete ✓"). Older saves without `q` are placed with the route of their check (`last`/`tries` `.level`). Unchecked answers (`state.typing[id_route]`) are carried the same way; "Do it again" and "Redo this section" clear every route's. A new answer panel must pass its whole list to `stays(…, src)`.
- **Progress in the browser and in the cloud (26 Oct 2026):** activity pages read their state from `localStorage` when they load, before `el-cloud.js` knows who is signed in, so `el-cloud.js` reloads the page once (`reloadIfStale`, guarded by `sessionStorage.el_reload`) when it has wiped the browser or when `pull()` changed one of the page's catalogue `keys`. A student's sign-in wipes the browser whenever `el_uid` is not theirs, including the first sign-in on that browser (the old data was the teacher's, a visitor's or another student's). Without this a Daring student saw "2 / 5" from someone else's steps, and a Safe or Daring student on a new device had their cloud progress overwritten by the empty page when the route button was clicked. New pages need nothing extra if they read `localStorage` at load and list their keys in `EL_CATALOG`. At the end of the gate (LS open, route set) `el-cloud.js` pushes once, so work left in the browser that the cloud lacks (for example done while the LS was still Public) reaches the cloud when the student opens that page again on the same device. **Students always see their own answers (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** LS2 engine pages (both courses) and Getting Started keep checked answers with their feedback (`state.ans`) and answers not checked yet (`state.typing`, saved as the student types or chooses), plus the speaking plan and transcript (`state.speak`); a recording is never saved. `el-cloud.js` never pushes before the page has pulled (`pulled`), keeps keys saved in the browser but not yet in the cloud in `el_dirty` (a pull never overwrites them; a 4ESO B student lost the recall after opening the Autumn words presentation within a second), sends at once on `pagehide`, and reloads after a pull that changed any key (the final product and Reflect read other activities' keys). A locked learning path, or one waiting for a checkpoint, opens read-only with a banner when the student has saved work there (`hasWork`); with nothing saved it still shows the block. LS1 pages keep only their done ticks, writings and speaking plans. The dashboard shows a red warning on a group tab whose LS is Hidden or not assigned to that group (`lsWarn` in `teacher.html`; Roger, 26 Oct 2026: 4ESO A worked a morning in a Public LS assigned only to 4ESO B and nothing reached the board), with a one-click “Open this LS” / “Assign it to <group>” button in the warning; the learning paths box repeats it while the LS is Hidden, the LS menu marks Hidden LSs “(Hidden)”, and a group tab first shows the group's current LS by the time plan (`ELWeek.now`), even if it is still Hidden, then the latest Open one; the student board (`today.html`) also opens on that LS when the student can open it (Roger, 27 Oct 2026: he opened the learning paths, the LS stayed Hidden, and his Open click went to LS1, which the tab showed by default).
- **No submitting from LS2 on (6 Nov 2026):** `el-cloud.js` applies the submit lock (`mode:"once"`: "Mark as done" / Submit banner, a locked result opens read-only, "Practica de nou" empties the keys and saves nothing) only to LS1 (`-my-real-routine`). LS2+ learning paths always pull and save, and an old `el_practice_<id>` flag is dropped. A 4 ESO student had submitted Celebrations around the world at 0 before doing it: the page froze, "Practica de nou" erased her answers twice, nothing reached the results, and she could not hand in the Speaking. Old locked LS2 results are unlocked in Firestore (as the dashboard's Reopen: `locked:false, markedDone:false`), because firestore.rules refuse a student's update on a locked result.
- **Steps hand themselves in, no Hand in button (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "I don't want them to have to press any button, the system needs to understand it as done"; "I want the button hand in out"):** `R.step` in the three `ls2-engine.js` (Reading log, research cards, final-product steps) has no button. `hand()` runs 0.7 s after each change and when the panel binds: once every non-optional field, every self-assessment rating and the teammates (`p.peers`) are filled and `p.check` passes, it saves `state.steps[id]`, `done[id]` and the product piece; later changes update the hand-in. Until then `#<id>Msg` says what is still missing. A draft written before this (problem report, 2ESOA, 24 Nov: Reading log written, never handed in) counts the next time the student opens the page. Writings (`R.writing`), Speaking and Talk keep their own send buttons.
- **Own work follows the route (Roger, 2 Nov 2026, 4ESO B problem report: a writing done on Daring looked lost after the teacher moved the student to Safe):** LS2 engine pages save texts, speaking plans and transcripts, Talk answers, drafts, final versions and the goal/review cards per route (`<panel>_<route>`, `plan[route]`). `carryRoute()` in both `ls2-engine.js` runs at every render: when the route on screen has nothing for a panel, it copies the newest (else longest) entry from another route; it never overwrites. Hand-ins (`steps`) and `done` are not per route, so they need nothing. A new per-route store of the student's own text goes into its list.
- **Points and class quiz (Roger, 25–26 Oct 2026):** in a class presentation the teacher (signed in) opens 📡 Class and chooses the class group ("▶ Start the class…"): that starts the class. **A presentation is the teacher's:** anyone who is not signed in as the teacher only follows it (`el-deck-points.js` locks the deck on the first slide, `ELDeck.show(LOCK)`, and covers it with the player), so the answers stay on the board until the teacher shows them. The screen publishes where it is to `live/{group}` (slide, step, revealed answers, option order; every step and every minute; off when the group changes or the page closes) and listens to `live/{group}/answers/{email}` = `{sid, a:{question: value}, at}`. Students join from the Learning Path: for a signed-in student the deck link (`a.deck-link`) reads "📺 Join the class" (`shared/js/el-live-join.js`, loaded by `el-cloud.js`; it pulses with the title while the teacher presents that deck) and opens the deck with `?live={group}` (a signed-in student without it follows their group on the student list); the device waits for the teacher, then follows the slides and steps underneath the **player** (`.elplay`): the current slide's title and each question that can be answered now with its options as big buttons (a poll's `.opt` buttons, or a card's choices `data-ch="-s|no -s"`, Catalan in `data-chca`), nothing else; an Insecure student gets the Catalan help. Every answerable question has `data-q` and its right value in `data-okv` (a poll: the option's `data-i`; a card: the choice's position), set by the deck engine. The projector shows "📡 12 joined" and who (the part of the email before @) on the strip, "✋ 9 / 12" on each question until the answer is shown, then "✓ 7 / 9" and the votes per option; the student's player shows their option green or red with "✓ Right! Look at the board." or "✗ Not this time. Look at the board.": the reason (`.why`/`.ans`, which every question must have) is on the board only. **Points:** when the teacher shows the answer, every student who tapped the right one gets +1 (`points/{email}.{term}`, written by the teacher's page once per question and class session; late answers count, an answer cannot change once shown) and the group's best goes to `teams/{group}.best.{term}`.
