They are a **bonus on the term grade of up to +0.5**: 0.5 × the student's points ÷ the best student's points in the group that term (`elBonus` in `shared/js/el-points.js`; terms and dates in `EL_TERMS`), so it only adds and starts again every term. **Only with all the work done (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** a student with any activity missing gets +0 that term (`elMissing` in `el-points.js`): every activity in the group's `EL_PLAN` `due` whose due day is in the term and already past, in an LS that is not Hidden, open for the group (`elLpOpen`) and with no section still locked; done late counts; the final product (`manual`) and `optional` homework never count. It follows the time plan, so the dashboard's Points box (column “All work”, hover for the list, CSV columns) and the student board's 🏆 box (a green ✓ / red ✗ tile next to the bonus and the missing activities with “Do it now”) always agree. **The bonus is in proportion to the best student with all the work done** (Roger, 30 Oct 2026), not the best of everyone: the dashboard works it out (only it sees every student's work) and saves it in `teams/{group}.bestOk.{term}` whenever it renders and the value changed (`ptSync` in `teacher.html`); the student board uses it (falling back to `best` until it exists). `best` (everyone) is still written by presentations and games. No teams, no buzzer (removed 26 Oct 2026). Every class presentation has a reminder slide right after the cover (`<section class="slide" data-el-points>`, filled by `shared/js/el-deck-points.js` with the three rules of `elPointsRules()` and a quick check); the student board (`today.html`) shows "🏆 My points", the bonus and the rules, and a "Join the class" link while the teacher presents to their group; the dashboard's Points box (per group, per term) corrects one student's points. `sid` is the teacher's class session (when the group was chosen; a new one for another deck in the same lesson), so each class starts empty and a student who reloads keeps their answers. `firestore.rules`: a student of the group writes and reads only their own answers doc; the teacher reads all and writes points. A graded Learning Path page is blocked until the teacher enables it, so the board's link is the other way in. **Chromebook mode:** once at least one student has joined, the teacher's `el-deck-points.js` puts the root class `el-cb` on and, on every slide with `data-q`, an ask whose 3 · 2 · 1 is for fingers or thumbs (`.go321[data-say="fingers"|"thumbs"]`) shows "💻 Tap your answer on your Chromebook" (cards: "💻 Tap a card, then your answer") instead of its finger code. Students never see the 3 · 2 · 1 buttons. New decks need nothing extra if they copy the reference deck. **Students use touch-screen Chromebooks (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** keep buttons students tap at least about 36 px high, and check new student views at 1366×768 with touch. **Answers stay (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** on the LS2 activity pages (`ls2-engine.js`, and `4eso/…/getting-started.html` with its own code), what a student typed or chose in a checked step, with its feedback and result, is saved (`state.ans`, synced with the rest) and shown again when they come back to the page, until they press "Do it again" next to Check; a restore never counts as a try.
- **The points bonus is in the term mark (Roger, 25 Nov 2026):** `termFinal(base, b)` / `tfCell` in `teacher.html` add `termBonus` (ptGets: 0 with any `elMissing` activity of the term) to the term mark by competence, capped at 10: 📆 Term and year marks (Term n mark + ⭐ Bonus column, the degree cards and the group average use it; the year mark = `yearOf` + Σ n·bonus_n ÷ Σ n over the terms with a mark) and the 🗓 Term n mark column of 👥 Students (`tBest` = ptBestOk of the term). `today.html`: `myBonus(id)` / `withBonus(m, n)` (from `pointsLine`'s rules: best = teams.bestOk) and `bonusNote` under the term mark of the 📄 Term report and 🎓 My progress (⭐ included, or 🔒 n activities missing and what it would be). The term report drafts in Firestore hold no term mark, so nothing to re-apply. **Ended terms stay true:** `ptSync` now rewrites `teams/{group}.bestOk` for every term so far, not only the current one (Term 1's stayed at 0 after the term ended, so the board gave every student with all the work done the whole +0.5), and `elDueWork` for an ended term counts only the tabs taught by its last day (`elDueBefore(info, gid, day after the term end)`). `pointsLine` shows the ended terms' points and bonus as `.mkpast`, outside How it works.
- **My activities box (Roger, 2 Nov 2026; since 19 Nov 2026 `workData()` + the ⏰ card of `marksHome`):** `workData()` in `today.html` computes the activities done so far this term by the time plan (`elDueWork` in `el-points.js`, the same rule as the bonus), the continuous-assessment share of the current LS mark (`elFormativeShare`: every `EL_GRADING` block except exam and final product, 40 % in LS2) and the missing ones with "Do it now". It never says which activities are assessed. The ⏰ late box leaves out the ones already listed there. **Missing work is counted in tabs, not learning paths (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "it says she's missing one but she's missing 5!"):** every number of missing or done work (board banner "Et falten N activitats", 🏆 "All my work done", My activities, the mark-so-far lines, the learning-paths table chips, the dashboard Points box "N tabs missing" + CSV) counts the tabs of the due learning paths (`elTabsLeft` / `elTabsTotal` in `el-catalog.js`: `EL_ACTIONS` minus `skip`, grouped by `elTabGroups`); which paths count (and the bonus rule) stays `elDueWork`/`elMissing`. On the teacher dashboard, LS grade › **📊 What makes up the LS mark** (`planTable(g)` in `teacher.html`, unfolded on each visit) lists every part of `EL_GRADING[ls]` for the group on screen with its share of the LS mark, what feeds it and who marks it; it is built from the grading data, so keep it data-driven. **My LS mark so far** (Roger, 2 Nov 2026, "marks so far per part"): the student's 📈 tab shows each block with its weight and their mark so far (`progressBox` in `today.html`); the marks come from `results/{ls}__assessment.progress = {exam, fp, gv, other, total, at}`, which the dashboard writes (`progSync`, "not counted yet" values, only when they change, group on screen) because only it can mark the secure checkpoints. Students never see which steps count. Since 2 Nov 2026 (Roger): in that student mark a missing activity counts 0 once its day in the time plan has passed (`S.dueZero`/`missZ` in `teacher.html`, used only by `progSync`), and the box opens with the Daily work / Exam / Final product bar and "If you don't work every day, your mark goes down". **Other competences read learning-path work only**; final product levels on 1.1, 1.2, 6.1 (4ESO) and 6.2 (2ESO) count in the final product 25 % (rows and rubric pct in `el-grading.js` and both `final-rubric-data.js`); transversal rows are marked but count nowhere. LS2 exam sections carry the real points (20/20/15/15/30). LS2 daily work 40 % = block `gv` "Grammar, vocabulary and skills" 35 % (checkpoints 10 · warm-ups 2.5 · practice on time 7.5 · **skills tabs on time 15**, as % of the LS) + other competences 5 % (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: shared by the amount of work, checkpoints a bit more).
- **Late = only the tabs taught (problem report, 4ESOA, 19 Nov 2026):** when the group has a Time plan agenda, `elDueWork` counts a learning path only with the tabs that were on a class day before today, ticked done in class or set as homework (`elDueBefore`, `elTaughtTabs` in `el-points.js`; its third item is that Set, null = the whole path); tabs not taught yet are never ⏰ late, never missing for the 🏆 bonus and never in the done/total count. Count them with `elTabsLeft(a, done, r, tabs)` / `elTabsTotal(a, tabs)` (board `tabsMiss`/`lateList`, dashboard `ptLeft`).
- **Learning path score and "done" (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: "I don't understand this score … some students have that they did everything and that the bonus counts, however this is not true"):** the Score of a learning path is 1 point per step done (`elPoints`), counted again from the saved progress every time (`elScore` in `el-catalog.js`), never the saved `results` score, which `firestore.rules` freeze once a student presses Submit. It shows how much is done, not how well, and is not the LS mark. "Done" (green ✅, the bonus "All work", missing lists, the board's ✔ Done) = every non-skipped step in `EL_ACTIONS` done (`elAllDone`); Submit / Mark as done are only notes (📤 / 🟡). Only an activity with no steps listed falls back to the flag. A step the teacher has corrected (`results.replies[step]`, not sent back to redo) counts as done too (`elCorrected` / `elInjectCorrected`, in memory on the dashboard and the board): drafts written in the old ChatGPT flow (before 1 Nov) were corrected on Wednesday but never saved as a final version (Roger, 4 Nov 2026).
- **Step names in the learning-path grid open the tab (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "when I touch these concepts, I want to go there"):** each step header (recall, cards, read, shadow…) in the dashboard grid is a link (`stepLink` in `teacher.html`) to `a.path#<panel>` in a new tab: the panel with the step's own id in `EL_SECTIONS`, `#vocab` for cards and practice, else the page itself (checkpoints, LS1 steps). The criterion chips under it still open the criterion window. A new LP whose step keys are not panel ids needs a case in `stepTab`.
- **Grid columns = the tabs of the learning path (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "practice, cards don't exist in the web"; "should go to the SLP not the LP"):** from LS2 on, the grid has one column per tab with the tab's own name and order (`lpCols` in `teacher.html`, from `EL_SECTIONS`); the Vocabulary tab's two steps (cards + practice) share one column (✔ when both, "1 of 2" otherwise). Names only: steps, tries and the score are counted as before. Tooltips, the student's activity dialog and the mark breakdown use `tabName`. LS1 keeps its step keys. The page opens the tab in the link for the teacher too (`openHashTab` in the teacher branch of `el-cloud.js`, not LS1). Students see the same (Roger: "make sure students see the same"): the board's tab chips, next step and counts, and the LS home pages' "n / m" use `elTabGroups` (el-catalog.js), so Vocabulary is one tab and a path counts its tabs (12), not its steps (13).
- **Question analysis (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: “spot the questions that are better answered and the ones that are more difficult… by levels… understand my students' needs”):** LS2 pages save every question of a panel's **first counted check** in the progress, once per route: `state.items[panel_route] = {level, date, q, ok, a}` (`firstItems` in `ls2-engine.js`; `q` = the question's place in the page's list, or in the route's own list when the page gives one per route; `ok` 1/0; `a` = what was typed, up to 40 characters, or the option chosen, 0 = right). Restores and later tries never change it, and students never see it. The dashboard tab **🔎 Questions** (and a “🔎 Questions” button on each group tab) reads it with the questions from each page's `data/<page>.js` (`shared/js/el-items.js`, loaded once per LS): what each route needs (weakest topics with 3+ answers, questions to review first, what goes well), the hardest and best answered questions, skills and topics × routes × groups, and every question with its route and group cells (hover: who got it wrong) and the most common wrong answers. Areas come from the panel (vocab → Vocabulary, typed → Grammar, text → Reading, audio or video → Listening, sample-exam quiz by its id, otherwise Other skills); recall and checkpoint questions are split into Grammar or Vocabulary from the question (a verb in brackets, a rewrite or a grammar word as the answer). **Every first try already saved counts** (Roger, 26 Oct 2026: “I want the first try answers of everything”): without `state.items`, a section checked only once (its first try and latest check saved within a second) gives its answers per question from `state.ans`; otherwise a typed question with no wrong try was right; and a section with only its first-try score (`state.tries`, `state.first`) counts for its section in “What each route needs” and “Skills and topics” (“n scores”, its points as right / points). **4ESO LS2 Getting started** is a standalone page: its questions live in `data/getting-started.js` (`window.LP` with the same tabs, so `tools/gen-sections.js` is unchanged; a panel may set `area`) and the page saves `state.items` with its own `firstItems`. Not covered: writing, speaking, checklists, hand-ins, LS1 pages and class presentations. A new LS built with `ls2-engine.js` and data files needs nothing extra; a standalone page needs its questions in `data/<page>.js` and `firstItems` on its checks.
- **Marking PDF per round (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** a "📄 Marking PDF" button next to the filters of a group tab (LSs whose `EL_GRADING[ls]` has `marking`) asks for the round and opens a print view that calls `print()`, so Roger saves it as an A4 PDF; it stores nothing and the answers stay in the browser. One section per final product group (`results/{fp}.team`, sorted; students without a group last), in the rubric of the route most members have: a group page only when the round has group rows (round 2: the video sheet, rubric only: *My part of the video* once per member plus the video group rows; round 1 has none), then one page per signed-in student (surname order; students not signed in are named in the toolbar) with their hand-ins for the round (`marking.rounds[r].answers` = `[activity, step]`, every field with its line breaks, self-assessment average, comment; `{rate:true}` also prints the self-check), the individual rows of the round in their own route's rubric with ☐ per level, and comment lines. Rows come from `round`, `group` and `video` in the LS's rubric data (`marking.rubric`, loaded on demand), never from row numbers; the row with `peers: true` shows the teammates' average (their typed names matched to members by first or full name, ignoring case and accents; unmatched ratings are listed on the group page; 3.5–4 Level 4, 2.5–3.4 Level 3, 1.5–2.4 Level 2, below 1.5 Level 1) and the row with `process: true` the process summary (steps in `marking.process`, on time = by the final product's `due`; `afterDue` steps come after submitting). Rubric text is quoted as the route sees it (Insecure in Catalan); the rest is in English. Print CSS: A4, a page break before every group and student, rows never split.
- Preview without Firebase: define `window.EL_DEMO_DATA` (teacher) or `window.EL_DEMO_STUDENT` (student page) (same shape as `load()` returns) before the module script; nothing is saved.
- **Roger's 2026–27 timetable (English):** 4ESO-A Mon 8:30 · Wed 12:00 · Fri 13:00. 4ESO-B Mon 15:00 · Tue 9:30 · Wed 10:30. 2ESO-A Fri 8:30 · Tue 12:00 · Thu 12:00. 2ESO-B Thu 8:30 · Tue 10:30 · Fri 9:30. Plans in `el-plan.js` list dated sessions per group (`groups` + `g`). The same timetable is data in `EL_TIMETABLE` (end of `el-plan.js`, with `off` days: add each term's holidays). **🗓 Timetable on the dashboard (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "I want a section where the teacher can add the schedule"):** Home > 🗓 Timetable edits each group's classes (day + start), how long a class lasts (`len`, default 60) and the days off; saved in `settings/timetable` (teacher only; `elTTStore`: days off as "from/to" strings because Firestore keeps no arrays inside arrays) and each group's part in `live/{group}.tt`. The dashboard reads it with the light load (`ttApply`) and replaces `window.EL_TIMETABLE`; when there is none yet it saves the one in `el-plan.js` (which stays the default); `ttHeal` rewrites a group's `tt` when it differs. So edit the timetable on the dashboard, not only in `el-plan.js`; `ELWeek.classAt()` gives the class going on now (from 5 min before). **Class now on the dashboard (Roger, 2 Nov 2026):** opening `teacher.html` during a class opens that group and its current LS (green note); a class that starts while it shows something else, or a dashboard opened from a link to another group/LS, gets a yellow bar "Go to <group> / Stay here", once per class per browser tab (`sessionStorage.el_t_class`). Classes marked `· self-study` are skipped. Previews: `window.EL_NOW="YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM"`.
- **Publishing `firestore.rules` (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** the GitHub Action `.github/workflows/firestore-rules.yml` publishes them to `english-lab-ietm` whenever a merge to `main` changes the file (or on demand: Actions → Publish Firestore rules → Run workflow). It signs in with the repository secret `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_ENGLISH_LAB_IETM` (a service account with only Firebase Rules Admin, Service Usage Consumer and Cloud Datastore Viewer). So merging a PR that changes the rules is the deploy: do not run `firebase deploy` from a PC; pasting the rules in the Firebase console is only a fallback if the Action fails. After the merge, check the Action run is green.
- **Backups of the students' data (Roger, 20 Nov 2026):** Firestore's own backups (daily, kept 7 days; Sunday, kept 14 weeks) and delete protection are on in `english-lab-ietm`; every Friday the free Action *Database copy to Drive* (`.github/workflows/database-copy.yml`, `tools/backup/firestore-copy.mjs`, read-only secret) copies every document as one gzip JSON into 04_Students (private) › English Lab database copies through the school Apps Script (action `backup`, v35). Copies are personal data: never in the repo or a log. Restore and costs: `docs/general/backups.md`.
- **Roger's to-dos in Google Tasks (Roger, 25 Nov 2026; school account):** school script v39 (`tools/notify/school-mail.gs`, deployment version 36) adds key-only actions `tasks` (`{key, action, list = "English Lab", items: [{title, notes, due: "YYYY-MM-DD"}]}`, a title already in the list, done or not, is skipped), `tasks_list` and `tasks_done` (`ids`); never deletes. Needs the advanced service **Tasks** (Google Tasks API v1) in the project and the Tasks permission granted once by the school account (Google's consent opens only from a person's click, or from the script's authorize link opened as a page). From the cloud: `node tools/notify/tasks.mjs add <file.json> [--send]` · `list` · `done <id>… [--send]` with `SCHOOL_MAIL_URL` + `SCHOOL_MAIL_KEY`. Task lists hold students' names, so their JSON lives in `/mnt/project-files/alumnes/google-tasks/`, never in the repo.
- **English Lab emails (Roger, 1 Nov 2026; free, no Claude tokens):** `.github/workflows/teacher-emails.yml` ("English Lab emails"), code in `tools/notify/` (`lib.mjs` sending + read-only Firestore). Teacher (`notify.mjs`, to his school inbox `roger.beltran@ietramuntana.cat`, never his personal Gmail: Roger, 19 Nov 2026 "stop sending mails with the reports to my personal mail"; `TEACHER_TO` in `lib.mjs` turns the Gmail into the school address even in `ALERT_EMAIL_TO`, and there is no Gmail fallback): every 15 min about 7:00–23:00 Madrid (`--instant`), one email when there are new ⚠️ Problem reports (`problems`) or 💡 Ideas (`suggestions`) since the last check (last-check time in the Actions cache: the Action's service account `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_ENGLISH_LAB_IETM` can only read); Saturday ~8:00 (after the Friday 19:37 marking audit), the class sessions not ticked per group, with the same rules as the dashboard's Weekend check (`pending()` in `teacher.html`; keep both in step), plus the week's open reports and ideas. Students (`students.mjs`, Roger chose "Weekly summary on Sunday"): Sunday ~18:00, one email each with their homework and classes on their own for Mon–Sun (`ELWeek.upcoming` + `ls/{id}.homework`) and pointers (activity · step, no words) to what the teacher answered or corrected that week. Language as on the board (Catalan Starter/Base/no route, English Core/Plus/Pro), Roger's voice, signed "Roger", never mentions AI. School accounts only receive mail from the school domain, so everything is sent from `roger.beltran@ietramuntana.cat`. The school blocks app passwords, so it goes through Roger's Apps Script `tools/notify/school-mail.gs` deployed as a web app (only sends to @ietramuntana.cat and his Gmail); its link is the secret `SCHOOL_MAIL_URL` and it refuses any request without its KEY (secret `SCHOOL_MAIL_KEY`; the repo copy has a placeholder). Students' emails start on 8 Nov 2026. No secret = nothing is sent. No email when there is nothing. The Action log is public: counts only. By hand: Actions → English Lab emails → Run workflow (test to me, one student's email to me, …).
- **Student emails for homework, announcements and results (Roger, 6 Nov 2026):** homework and announcements are emailed by the dashboard at the moment they are set or sent (✉️ tick in both dialogs, on by default; `mailStudents` in `teacher.html`): it posts `{idToken, bcc, subject, body}` to the same school Apps Script (`MAIL_URL`), which checks the teacher's Firebase sign-in with Google (`accounts:lookup`), so no secret is in the page; students in Bcc, ≤45 per email, only @ietramuntana.cat, never testers, roster students who never signed in included. Scheduled announcements carry `mail:true` and are emailed by the first dashboard open after their time (`annDue`, a transaction sets `mailed` so two open dashboards never both send). Every send asks the teacher first (confirm, ticks start unticked; Roger 6 Nov 2026 "before sending a mail, I want the program to ask me"); the daily results and Sunday student crons are PAUSED, run by hand. Results go by `tools/notify/news.mjs` (Run workflow "new results to students"): one email per student with what changed since the last run (exam results with every section, final product mark, corrections `replies`, answers to comments, problem replies), detected by signatures kept in the Actions cache (`.news-state.json`), not by `updated` (the dashboard and students rewrite results docs all the time); no cache = it only takes note and sends nothing. Tools that write marks straight to Firestore need nothing special; they may run the workflow by hand afterwards so students hear the same day.
- **Bottom bar (`bar()` in `el-cloud.js`) has no page links for students (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "we don't need this mini menu on the bottom"):** name, ⚠️ Problem, 💡 Idea, the tester's View as / Level selects and Sign out; no My dashboard / Games (the left menu has both; LS1 pages keep ☰ My dashboard next to their home link). The teacher keeps a Dashboard link.
- **Problem reports (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** students (and the tester) tell the teacher that something on the site does not work with "⚠️ Problema / Problem" in the bottom bar of every page that loads `el-cloud.js` (`reportProblem`, window in `shared/js/el-report.js`). Catalan for Insecure or with no route, English for Safe and Daring (the route on LS pages, the board's language on `today.html`). The student picks a kind (`media` audio or video does not play · `stuck` cannot go on / a button · `marking` correct answer marked wrong · `display` page looks wrong · `other`, which needs a text) and can explain it (up to 1000 characters). The report adds where it happened without asking: page path and hash, page title, the heading of the section on screen, LS, activity, route, group, device (system, browser, window size) and up to 5 errors the page logged (JS errors, rejected promises, audio, video or images that did not load, also media that failed before the script loaded). Stored as `problems/{id}`; `firestore.rules` lets a signed-in student create only their own, with those fields and sizes, and only the teacher read, mark (`fixed`) or delete them. **Screenshot (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** the window has an optional "📷 Screenshot": "📸 Capture this screen" draws the part of the page on screen with html2canvas (vendored in `shared/js/vendor/html2canvas-1.4.1.min.js`, MIT, loaded only on that click; no screen-share prompt, but videos and embedded content come out blank), "🖼️ Choose an image" or Ctrl+V pastes the student's own. It is shrunk to a JPEG data URL of at most 600 000 characters (`SHOT_MAX`, ≤ 1280 px) and saved in the same batch as `problemShots/{same id}` = `{uid, img, created}` (rules: own uid, ≤ 900 000 characters, the report must exist after the batch; only the teacher reads or deletes it), so the dashboard loads it only when the teacher presses "📷 Screenshot" on the report; `problems/{id}.shot` is `page`, `file` or null. No Firebase Storage (it would need the paid plan and its own rules deploy). Kept separate from suggestions. The dashboard shows "⚠️ Problem reports" (all groups, open first) under To do on every group tab and on Time plan, and To do counts the open ones; the page link opens only for a plain relative path. **Fix with Claude and the teacher's bell (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** every open report on the dashboard has "🤖 Fix with Claude": a dialog with a prompt holding the whole report (problem, student's words, student, group, route, time, section, live page link, LS, activity, device, screenshot yes/no, errors, report id; `pbPrompt` in `teacher.html`), editable, with "💻 Copy and open the project in Claude Desktop" and "📋 Copy and open the project in the browser". Both copy the prompt and open the English Lab project (no link can start a thread in a Claude project, so Roger pastes it in the project chat). **Always the project, never a new chat (Roger, 5 Nov 2026):** the Desktop button opens `PB_DESKTOP` (`claude://claude.ai/code/project/chan_…`, plus `?q=` in case the app fills it in); it used to be `claude://code/new?q=…`, which starts a new chat outside the project. The teacher gets a 🔔 in the bottom bar of every page that loads `el-cloud.js` (`shared/js/el-bell.js`, loaded only for the teacher): the number of open reports (all groups), live with `onSnapshot` on `problems`, a list of the latest 8 and "Open them in the dashboard" (`teacher.html#probs`, which reloads the reports and opens the box). Students never load it, and no rules change was needed (the teacher already reads `problems`). **Answer to the student (Roger, 26 Oct 2026):** "✅ Mark as fixed" also writes `problemReplies/{same id}` = `{uid, kind:"fixed", what, text, where, en, created}`; "Reopen" deletes it (taken back if still unread). "Delete" on an open report asks: "💌 Delete and thank the student" (kind `thanks`: Roger checked it and the site is right, so a polite, grateful note that nothing was changed) or "Delete without a message"; deleting a fixed report sends nothing more. The words are in `shared/js/el-reply.js` (`replyText`), in the language of the route the report was sent with (`en`: Catalan for Beginners, Insecure and no route). `el-cloud.js` shows each note once to the student as a card at the top of the next page they open ("OK / D'acord" deletes it). `firestore.rules`: only the teacher writes `problemReplies`; the student reads and deletes their own. **Answer with your own words (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "I want to be able to answer students when they report a problem"):** every report (open or fixed) has "💬 Answer": a dialog with the student's words, a box for the teacher's message (≤ 1000 characters, `MSG_MAX` in `el-reply.js`) and a live preview of the card. "💬 Send" writes `problemReplies/{report id}_m{time}` = the usual fields plus `kind:"message"`, `msg` (a new id each time, so answers never overwrite the fixed/thanks note or each other); on an open report "✅ Send and mark as fixed" writes the `fixed` note itself with `msg` (one card). The card shows the standard sentence in the report's language and the teacher's words as written, in a blue box. Each answer is also kept on the report, `problems/{id}.msgs` = `[{text, at (ISO), rid, fix, back?}]`, and the dashboard lists them under the report with "not read yet" while `problemReplies/{rid}` exists (the dashboard loads the ids of all unread notes) and "read ✓" once the student pressed OK; Reopen before the student read a fixed note with a message marks it `back:true` ("taken back"). No rules change (the teacher already writes `problems` and `problemReplies`).
- **⚠️ Problem on every page (Roger, 19 Nov 2026: "I want to be able to report a problem everywhere"):** a page without `el-cloud.js` (so without the bottom bar) loads `shared/js/el-report-btn.js` as a module before `</body>`: the class game boards (host, Code Breakers, Space rescue, results; `play.html` with `?who=teacher`, students have the ⚠️ in its top bar), model videos, LS1 examples, rubrics and sample exams, flashcards, grammar presentations (`?before=fsBtn`: in their top bar, the bottom right is the big next-step button), Shadowing and the print pages. Same window and save as the bar (`el-report.js`, problems/{id}), for a signed-in student or the teacher; no reads until a report is sent. **A new page without el-cloud.js gets this script too**; `?at=bl|br|tl|tr` moves it if the corner holds a control. 2btx/ (not English Lab) has none.
- **The bottom bar never hides content (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "students can't see the bottom side"):** the floating bar (`el-cloud.js` `bar()`: name, Problem, Idea, Sign out/Exit) covered the last card at the end of a page (the board's "About this LS"). `keepBarSpace()` keeps an empty block as tall as the bar (+24 px) at the end of the page while the bar floats (none when it sits in the page on phones): inside the main column on `.app` pages (board, LS homes, English Lab, Practise) so the sticky side menu is not pushed up, and as `padding-bottom:var(--el-bar-space)` on `main#lp` on learning paths. Sticky side menus are `box-sizing:border-box` (with padding added to 100vh their top slid away), and on short screens (≥981 px wide, ≤820 px high: Chromebook) the menu buttons are smaller so the whole menu fits (`el-app.css`). New floating things at the bottom right must leave the same room.
- **Problem reports fixed automatically (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: "read it straightaway and, if it's a real problem, fix it and answer the student that it is fixed straightaway. Merge, but it must be a technical problem, not a design or pedagogical thing" + "I must be informed via mail, with an important subject"):** the scheduled task "English Lab: problem reports (instant)" (no schedule) follows `docs/general/problem-reports-auto.md` with `tools/problems/auto.mjs` (`open` lists open reports without `auto`; `fixed <id> --msg --note --apply` writes the ✅ fixed card like the dashboard's "✅ Send and mark as fixed"; `leave <id> --note --apply` keeps it open for Roger). Technical bugs: fixed, PR squash-merged without asking, student answered in Roger's voice, Roger emailed with a `⚠️ IMPORTANT · English Lab: N problem report(s) fixed automatically` subject by `notify.mjs --instant` ("English Lab emails" Action, reports whose `auto.at` is newer than its last check; the task starts it at once with workflow_dispatch). Design, pedagogy, marks, routes, unclear reports and LS1 content: left for Roger. It starts at once: after a report is saved, `el-cloud.js` (`wakeFixer`) posts `{idToken, action:"problem"}` to the school Apps Script, which checks the sign-in and fires the task's API trigger (`PROBLEM_TASK_TOKEN`, ≤ once per 3 min, ≤ 30/day; a report inside the 3 minutes starts it again when they are over via the `startProblemsLater` timer). It runs only for reports: Roger, 8 Nov 2026, "I want you to be triggered only when there's a problem". Each report keeps `problems/{id}.auto = {verdict: "fixed"|"roger", status, note, todo, reply, at}`, shown on the dashboard under the report. **Status + suggested reply (Roger, 10 Nov 2026: "I need you to tell me if the problems are fixed and a suggested message to send with a button"):** every report left for Roger gets `leave <id> --status fixed|nobug|you --note --reply [--todo]`: the dashboard shows ✅ Fixed / 👍 Not a bug / ✋ Needs you (+ "👉 First: todo"), and the suggested reply (report's language, Roger's voice, never AI) with one-click 📨 Send (fixed → ✅ fixed card with it; nobug → message card and the report closes as "Closed", `nobug: true`; you → message card, stays open) and ✏️ Edit first (💬 Answer prefilled). It hides once sent (`msgs[].sug`) or once the report is fixed.
- **The teacher's own ⚠️ Problem (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "I need a problem button available for the teacher" + "little tweaks merge, big changes start a thread"):** the teacher's bottom bar (dashboard, class tab, every page signed in) and the ⋯ menu of a learning path on a phone have ⚠️ Problem too (`reportProblem` with `teacher`, window in English with its own words, no 💡 Idea). The report is the usual `problems/{id}` plus `teacher: true` (`firestore.rules`: `signedIn() || isTeacher()`, and `teacher` must equal `isTeacher()`, so only his account sets it), `route` null and `group` = the class mode's group on the class tab (`cmRead`). His window has a dropdown (Roger, same day: "I want a dropdown button where I say merge or don't merge yet"), saved as `merge`: ✅ Merge straight away (default) or ⏸ Don't merge yet. It starts the same instant task (`wakeFixer`; section "The teacher's own reports" in `docs/general/problem-reports-auto.md`): ✅ = made and squash-merged at once, no preview, the ⚠️ IMPORTANT email says what changed (a plainly big change is still not merged); ⏸ = a PR with a preview, or only a plan for a big change, and `notify.mjs --instant` emails him the note with the report's link, where 🤖 Fix with Claude starts the thread. The dashboard tags each one ✅ / ⏸. No student card is ever written for it (`fixed` and the dashboard's ✅ / Delete skip `problemReplies`), the dashboard shows it as 👨‍🏫 You without 💬 Answer or a suggested reply, and the 15-minute "new report" email skips it.
- **Suggestions (Roger, 25 Oct 2026; merged 31 Oct):** students (and the tester) send ideas to improve the website with "💡 Idea" in the bottom bar of every page that loads `el-cloud.js` (`suggest`), next to ⚠️ Problem: a small window in the same language as the Problem window (`repEn`), up to 1000 characters. Stored as `suggestions/{id}` = `{uid, email, name, group, page, text, created}`, kept apart from problem reports; `firestore.rules` lets a signed-in student create only their own and only the teacher read, mark (`read`) or delete them. The dashboard shows "💡 Suggestions from students" (all groups, new first) after the problem reports on every group tab and on Time plan, and To do counts the new ones.
- **A draft ready in every 💬 Answer box (Roger, 20 Nov 2026: "Pq no tens un draft preparat per contestar?"):** a 💡 Idea and a student's answer to a private message start the same instant task as a ⚠️ Problem (`wakeFixer` in `el-cloud.js`, after saving). The task writes a draft in Roger's voice, Catalan + English (`tools/problems/auto.mjs idea|msg`, section "Drafts for ideas and private answers" in `docs/general/problem-reports-auto.md`): ideas in `suggestions/{id}.auto = {en, ca, note, at}`, private answers in `msgDrafts/{announcement id}` (teacher-only collection in `firestore.rules`, because the student can read the announcement; `n` = the thread length it answers). The dashboard opens ↩ Answer with the draft in the box(es) (`sgPre`, `msgDraft`; boxes grow to show it all, caret at the start), "Reply ready" on the idea in the list, and ↩ Answer on a problem report also opens with Beltran's suggested reply while it is unsent (`pbPre`). Never sent by itself: Roger changes it and presses Send.
- **Announcements (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "I want to be able to send messages to my students, like announcements from the teacher"):** "📣 Announcements" on the dashboard (every group tab, before To do, and on Time plan) with "📣 New announcement": to one group, a whole course, every group or some students of the group on screen (only signed-in ones can be ticked), up to 1000 characters (`ANN_MAX`), sent exactly as written (links become clickable), with a live preview of the card in both languages. Stored as `announcements/{id}` = `{text, to, label, groups, uids, created, read:{uid: time}}`; testers are always in `uids`. A student gets the ones whose `groups` holds their group or whose `uids` holds them (two queries in `myAnnouncements`, `el-cloud.js`; also students who sign in later): once as a card at the top of the next page they open (`shared/js/el-announce.js`, only from the last 14 days, `CARD_DAYS`, one card at a time with the other notices), and always in "📣 Announcements from your teacher / Anuncis del professor" at the top of their board (`today.html`, newest first, "New" until read). The card's own words follow the route (Catalan for Starter and Base). "OK" adds `read.{uid}`; the dashboard shows "read by n of m" and who has not read it yet. Delete removes it from every board. `firestore.rules`: only the teacher creates or deletes; a student reads only the ones for their group or for them and can only add their own read time (tested in the Firestore emulator). No email is sent.
- **Private message to one student (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "sending students messages through the app, a notification and an email"):** the student window on the dashboard (a name in any table, `stuPanel`) has "✉️ Send a message" (`msgDialog`/`msgSend`). It is an announcement only for them: `announcements/{id}` with `to:"one"`, `label` = full name, `groups:[]`, `uids:[student, testers]`, so no new rules or queries. The student gets the card titled "✉️ Message from your teacher / Missatge del teu professor" (`annTitle(en, one)`), it stays in their board's announcements with "✉️ Just for you", and the ✉️ email tick is ON by default here (still a confirm before it goes; subject and footer in the route's language). Students not signed in yet get it by email only (nothing stored). The student window lists "✉️ Messages with them" (read ✓ / not read yet) from the announcements already loaded: no extra reads. **Students answer back (Roger, 7 Nov 2026):** ↩ Reply on the card and under the message on their board adds `thread` item `{by:"s", uid, text, at}` with their read time and `sAt` (server time); `firestore.rules` `annReply()` allows exactly one such item, only on `to:"one"` messages sent to them (emulator-tested). Your ↩ Answer adds `{by:"t", …}`, removes their `read.{uid}` (the card comes back with the whole conversation) and sets `tDone`; "✓ Seen, no answer needed" sets `tDone` only. Unanswered replies (`msgWait`) show as "✉️ n new answers" alerts on Home and the group page, the 💬 Messages badge and tags. The 15-minute teacher email (`notify.mjs --instant`) includes new answers (`since("announcements", last, "sAt")`). Group announcements have no replies.
- **💬 Messages: tabs and ✓ Mark all as read (Roger, 19 Nov 2026: "a way to unmark all the messages on my dashboard"; "I want the ideas and the problems to be in different sections"):** `msgsBoxes()` in `teacher.html` shows three tabs, ⚠️ Problems · 💡 Ideas · 📣 Announcements, each with its count, and only the chosen box (`S.msgTab`; none chosen = the first with something new; Home alerts, Read buttons and the 🔔 open their own tab). The counts (menu badge, phone bottom bar, Home and group alerts, To do line, 🔔) count only **new** items: open problem reports without `seen` (`pbNew`), unread ideas (`sgNew`), unanswered replies to private messages (`msgNew`). **✓ Mark all as read** (`msgsSeen`) writes `problems/{id}.seen:true`, `suggestions/{id}.read:true`, `announcements/{id}.tDone` in one batch: one write per item, no new reads, no rules change (teacher-only fields), nothing sent to students. Reports stay open (and listed in the 🔔 panel); a new report counts again. **Inbox (Roger, 19 Nov 2026: "I want to see the reports as a message as I would in an email inbox … now it has a lot of info and it's misleading"):** Problems and Ideas are an email-style list (`ibRow`, Gmail style on Roger's screenshot: sender, group, grey label (Needs you / Fixed / Not a bug, "· reply ready" while Beltran's suggested reply is unsent), subject - start of the text, time; unread rows white and bold, read rows light blue-grey), newest first, fixed/closed reports in a fold below. A click opens that one alone (`S.pbOpen`/`S.sgOpen`, "‹ All reports"), laid out like an email (`ibMail`; Roger: "who sends the message and once it's open a clear message and the icons below mustn't be protagonist"): subject, round avatar + "First Surname" (`ibSender`), group, "to you · date", the message big in a white card, "Sent from 📍 page", then quieter Beltran's check / suggested reply / your answers, and small plain text buttons at the bottom (`.mtools`), and marks it read (`api.pbSeen` / `sgRead`: one write). The 🔔 and `#pb-<id>` open the report itself. Long explanations sit in `ⓘ How it works`.
- **Answered = dealt with (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** `pbAns(x)` = a student report with any `msgs[]` item not `back` (whichever button sent it; before, only `m.sug` from 📨 Send hid the suggested reply, so ✏️ Edit first left ✋ Needs you and the 📨 Send box). `pbDone(x)` = `fixed || pbAns(x)` drives the open list, `pbNew` (every count and badge), the tab choice and `pbSeen` on open; the tag reads 💬 Answered (`.pbst.ans`), `pbPre`/`pbAuto` drop the draft and "👉 First". Ideas: `sgNew` and the row's new dot skip ideas with `msgs`. The Saturday email (`tools/notify/notify.mjs`, `answered()`) leaves out answered reports and ideas and counts them in one line. No new reads or writes. **Ideas archived like reports (21 Nov 2026):** `sgAns(x)` (as `pbAns`) and `sgDone(x)` = `done || sgAns(x)` split `sgBox` into the open list and a collapsed `details.ibdone` "Answered and archived"; 📥 Archive / ↺ Move back to open (`data-do=sgdone`, `api.sgDone`) writes `suggestions/{id}.done` (+ `read:true`), shown only on unanswered ideas; `sgNew` skips `sgDone`; the Saturday email skips `done` ideas too.
- **Answers to students in both languages (Roger, 20 Nov 2026, on an English-only answer to a student who wrote in Catalan: "It should also have the catalan version"):** 💬 Answer on a ⚠️ problem report or a 💡 idea has "I write in: English | Català" (kept on this computer, `el_rpL`, English by default) and a second box Google Translate fills while he types (`annTranslate`, like 📣 Announcements; he can change it, ↻ Translate again, Send waits for both). `problemReplies` get `msgEn` + `msgCa` next to `msg`; `shared/js/el-reply.js` shows the version in the card's language first and the other under "🌐 En català" / "🌐 In English" (old replies with only `msg` as before). `msgs` on the report or suggestion keep `en` + `ca`, so "You answered" shows both. Beltran's answers: `tools/problems/auto.mjs` needs `--msg2` with `--msg` and `--reply2` with `--reply` (the other language); the one-click 📨 Send uses `auto.reply` + `auto.reply2`, and an older suggestion with one language opens ✏️ Edit first instead.
- **📣 Announcement in the top bar (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "where is the announcement?"):** a "📣 Announcement" button next to 🎬 How it works opens the New announcement window from any tab (the box itself sits far down a group's page). The window has "Add a video link" chips (`ANN_VIDEOS` in teacher.html) that add the English and Catalan links of a student video (found by file name in `HOW_VIDEOS`, so its ?v= follows) as full web addresses. Only "How English Lab works" since the student board video was folded into it (PR #259).
- **Several groups and Send later (Roger, 2 Nov 2026: "program announcements and be able to select more than one class"):** in the New announcement window every group chip toggles (course and Every group chips tick or untick their groups), so any set of groups can get one announcement (`to` = `all`, `c:<course>`, `g:<group>` or `groups`; label "2ESO A + 4ESO B"). "⏰ Send later" (datetime-local) stores `at` (ms). `myAnnouncements` in el-cloud.js drops announcements whose `at` is still in the future and dates the others by `at` (`created` := `at`), so the card, its 14 days and the board use the scheduled time. The dashboard lists scheduled ones as "⏰ scheduled for …". No email is sent for announcements (students only see them on the site); the rules are unchanged (a student could read a future one in the raw data, which is acceptable).
- **Two languages, translated for free (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "escriure el text en català i que sortís en anglès als grups que toca i en català als grups que toca"; "with a free translation tool… I don't want this feature to depend on you"):** the New announcement window has "I write in: Català / English / One text for everyone" (kept per computer in `localStorage.el_annL`, Català by default). With Català or English, a second box holds the other version, filled while he types by Google Translate's free web endpoint (`translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx`, no key, CORS open; `annTranslate` in `el-announce.js`, web links kept as written) and editable; once he edits it, it no longer follows ("↻ Translate again" replaces it). Send waits while it translates and while the version is empty; if the service fails he writes it by hand. Stored as `text` (what he wrote) + `lang` + `ca` + `en`; `annText(a, en)` picks the student's version on the card, the board and the previews (Catalan for Starter, Base and no level; English for Core, Plus, Pro), old ones and private messages only have `text`. The email goes as two emails (each asks first): the whole Catalan text to Starter/Base, the whole English text to Core/Plus/Pro, subject and footer in that language. No rule change (only the teacher writes announcements).

## ✏️ Correct it: the teacher's correction screen (Roger, 24 Nov 2026)
- `shared/js/el-fix.js` (`ELFix.open(host, o)`), opened from the correction dialog (`wrOpen` → `fixOpen` in `teacher.html`, button `data-do="fixopen"`, any hand-in that is not a level moment). It reads and writes the correction text itself (`ELMark.fbText` shape, shown by `el-corrections.js` `writing()`): comment lines (✔ line, then `➜ ` lines), `✗ words → right — why` rows (`→ 🔊 …` = a word said wrong: never replaced in the corrected text) and, for a writing, `✅ ` + the text with each underline replaced by `*correction*`. So Beltran's corrections open as underlines (`place`: first time their words appear, not overlapping), and the teacher's version shows to students exactly like Beltran's. A recording edits Roig's *Transcript of the recording* (else `Text`), and gets no ✅ line.
- Save (`fixOpen` onSave) writes what `marks.mjs fb-apply` writes: `replies[step]`, `fbSig[step]` = sig of the current fields (Beltran skips this version, corrects a new one), `fbBy[step]: null`, `fbAt[step]`, `fbGo[step]` (Beltran's pending 17:00 hold kept, else the 🌙 `lateAsk` hold or now), plus `fbWho[step] = "teacher"` (labels "✏️ Your correction") and, for a writing in an LS with 5.1 indicators, `levels[step]["5.1"]` + `marked[step]`.
- The double check (`ELFix.check`) is local and free: no reads, no AI. The ➜ suggestion (`suggestNext`) groups the rows' reasons by their first word ("he/she/it…"). Inputs have `spellcheck` with `lang`, so the browser underlines spelling slips in the corrections. No AI suggestion on the spot yet (design: any AI by API, later).

## Beltran's answers and corrections wait for weekdays 17:00 (Roger, 17 Nov 2026; 24 Nov 2026)

**Weekdays from 17:00 (Roger, 24 Nov 2026: "These answers must be in the afternoon from 17h and the same for all the corrections during
weekdays at 17h"; "all the writing and corrections everyday at 17 except for weekends. Reports are done immediately but we don't send
an answers until I press the button"; it replaces the 08:00-18:00 window below).** The scheduled task "English Lab: mark and correct"
runs every weekday at 15:13 Madrid (was Wednesday); `ELMark.lastRun()` is the last weekday before today and the dashboard's `corrNext()`
the next weekday run. Problem reports: `auto.mjs fixed` saves the ✅ card with `at` = 2100-01-01 and `auto.wait: true`; the report stays
open (`pbWait`, "Fixed · send answer") until Roger's 📨 Send it (`pbNow` sets `at` = now). Comment answers: `replyAt()` now gives at least 2 hours after the comment or report,
Monday to Friday 17:00-21:00 Madrid (a few random minutes, never after 20:55), else the next weekday 17:00-17:25. New `corrAt()`:
now inside that window, else the next weekday at 17:00 sharp. `marks.mjs fb-apply` and `apply --feedback` (and both auto-redo notes)
save `results/{activity}.fbGo[step]` = `corrAt()` with the correction; `--now` = at once. `el-cloud.js held()` (every student page reads
results through it, and the teacher's 👁 student view applies `elHeld`) takes out `answers[step]` whose `at` and `replies`/`fbSig`/`flags[step]`
whose `fbGo` are still to come, so the step's tab, the cards, the notebook, the board and the exam page never show them early, and a
redo opens at the same time. `news.mjs` skips held replies. Dashboard: the correction dialog (`wrOpen`) shows 🕗 Not shown yet with
📨 Show it now (`api.fbNow` sets `fbGo` to now); Roger's own answer (`api.reply`) clears `fbGo`, so it shows at once. Towards Nativeness
(`tn-apply`, Roger's own course) is not held.

### Before: school hours (17 Nov 2026)

"Please don't answer straight away; always answer from 8-18 and after 2 hours since u receive the message o report, don't use their names
or surnames since sometimes it feels like AI." Every automatic answer to a student (a fixed problem report's ✅ card and message,
`tools/problems/auto.mjs fixed`; an answer to a comment on a hand-in, `tools/marking/marks.mjs answer`) is saved at once but dated
`tools/problems/reply-time.mjs replyAt()`: at least 2 hours after the report or comment arrived, inside 08:00-18:00 Madrid time, with a few
random minutes (after 17:55 → the next day from 08:05). Students' pages show it only from then (`problemReplies.at` in el-cloud.js,
`answers[step].at` in el-corrections.js), and the results emails wait for it too. The dashboard says "🕗 Not shown yet … at 10:12 today"
with 📨 Show it now (Roger's "send it anyway"). `--now` skips the wait, only when Roger asks for it. Both tools refuse an answer that
uses the student's name or surnames (also no greeting with the name in the marking sheets). Roger's own sends from the dashboard keep the
🌙 21:00-7:30 hold (lateAsk) and are not delayed otherwise.

**No names, ever (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "I said never use their names or surnames please",** on a 2ESO problem report whose ↩ Answer box
opened with "Hola Alejandra!"). That draft was written on 17 Nov at 17:23Z, three hours before the rule, and PR #777 later started
pre-filling ↩ Answer with old `auto.reply` drafts, so it came back. Now: `namesIn()` in `reply-time.mjs` also refuses any greeting followed
by a capitalised word ("Hola Alejandra!", "Hi Ammy,"), so a classmate's name is caught too, in `auto.mjs idea|msg|fixed|leave` and
`marks.mjs answer`. In `teacher.html`, `noNames(text, nmWords(student, …))` cleans every draft before it reaches a box, a preview or a
one-click send (`pbPre`, `pbRe` in `pbAuto` and `pbsug`, `sgDraft`, the `msgDraft` pre-fill): "Hola Alejandra!" → "Hola!", "Thanks, Aya." →
"Thanks.", "Aya, you…" → "You…"; only capitalised words, so "pau" or "mar" inside a sentence stay. The automatic emails
(`tools/notify/news.mjs`, `tools/notify/students.mjs`) open with "Hola!" / "Hi!" instead of "Hola Pau,".

## Redo one activity (Roger, 2 and 4 Nov 2026)
"I don't want them restart the whole learning path" (2 Nov) and "I only want redo this activity, not the entire page" (4 Nov). In LS2 Learning Paths every checked activity has its own **↺ Redo this activity** / **↺ Torna a fer aquesta activitat** next to its Check (`againBtn` in both `ls2-engine.js`: the questions, the vocabulary practice, the micro-dictation `{panel}DAgain`; 4 ESO `getting-started.html` too). It empties only that activity's boxes for a new try (`forget`: every route's `state.typing`, the in-page tries; the dictation: `state.dictT[panel]`), so the other activities of the section keep their answers and corrections. **Since 6 Nov 2026 it is called ↺ Practise again / ↺ Torna a practicar and keeps the last checked answers saved** (`state.ans`, `state.dict`) until the new try is checked (Roger: "if they don't finish and leave the pages without checking, next time they come back they see the old results"); `fresh[id]` only hides them on the page for the new try. **Except after a class game (Roger, 16 Nov 2026, Víctor lost a new try when the game started):** el-cloud.js `gamePush` notes the page in sessionStorage `el_game_took` before it opens the game; on the first page after the game, an activity where ↺ Practise again was pressed and nothing of the new try is checked yet (`state.again[id]`, set by `forget`, cleared by `keep`) and answers are typed (`state.typing`) opens as that new try with what they had typed, in the laptop look (`retryBack` in both ls2-engine.js). Leaving on their own keeps the rule above. There is **no button that clears a whole section or page** (the 2 Nov "Redo this section" button was removed). Marks never change: `done`, `doneAt` (done on time), `tries`, `first`, `items` (first try only) and hand-ins stay; new checks only update `last`. Secure checkpoints have no redo (one try); locked sections and read-only pages disable it. Never say on the page that the first try counts. A path a student did always opens with all their answers, corrections and feedback (micro-dictation feedback too, `bindDict`). A new checked activity gets its own `againBtn`, and a new answer panel registers through `stays()`.
**Answers always come back (Roger, 6 Nov 2026: "ALL THE ACTIVITIES STUDENTS DID, THEY MUST SEE THEM WITH THEIR RESPONSES UNLESS THEY REDO THAT ACTIVITY"):** every box a student types in or chooses on a learning path is saved as they go and is back, with its corrections, after a reload, on another device and after a level change, until they press that activity's ↺ Redo. Micro-dictation typed but not checked yet: `state.dictT[panel]`; 4 ESO Getting started speaking plan and transcript: `state.speak.gs`. Not kept on purpose: the study guide's practice (`study_*`), the study card being typed (`card*`), recordings, secure checkpoints (their answers are in the cloud and come back with the teacher's correction). **Check:** `node tools/answers-stay/check.mjs [page]` (Playwright, offline from the repo files) answers every activity of every LS2 learning path at every route, reloads, changes the level and back, and lists every answer or correction that did not come back; the GitHub Action *Answers stay* runs it on every pull request that touches `2eso/`, `4eso/` or `shared/`. A new LS adds its folder and routes to `LSS` in the script; a new box that is not an answer on purpose goes in `NOT_KEPT` with the reason. **A step is done when every activity is answered and checked, whatever the score (Roger, 6 Nov 2026, chose "All activities checked" over the old 80 %):** `answered()` in both `ls2-engine.js`, `allIn()` in 4 ESO `getting-started.html`; the score and the first try (the mark) still show and count as before; the texts say "Answer every question to complete the step" / "Respon totes les preguntes per completar el pas". **No Submit on the student board (Roger, 6 Nov 2026):** answers save on their own, so the board has no Submit / "Submit them" button any more (it only froze the score and said work "can no longer be changed"); a learning path's card says ✔ All done or Some steps are still to do. Hand-ins to the teacher (Writing, Speaking, Talk) stay.
**🆕 The latest answer shows (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "Es millor que els alumnes i jo tinguin les últimes versions a la llibreta i a la pàgina, així si ja ho han arreglat ho veuen bé"):** a step opened again shows, question by question, the newest answer given (a new ✓ Check on the page, ↺ Practise again, or ✏️ Correct it in the notebook look), checked again with today's check, in the laptop look AND the notebook look; ✏️ Correct it shows only while the newest answer is wrong. **Students never see a first try** (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "No need to say your first, students must be their last version even on the cover"; "First tries will be a statistic for me"): no first-try line, note or "(first try: n)" on the page, and 📓 My notebook (`el-exam.js` `latestOf` → `firstTry`, which now returns the latest answers: `given`, `ok`, `right` = latest right count, `day` = latest check) stamps the latest score; its cover (`el-notebook.js` `lsSum`) reads `ELExam.latest(activity, step)` (filled by `build` for every step it draws), so the rings, the "done well" tile and the criteria follow the latest answers straight away. The mark never changes: `state.items`, `state.tries` and the notebook's first try `state.nb[id].v` are untouched; they are the dashboard's statistic. In all three `ls2-engine.js` (2 ESO LS2, 4 ESO LS2, Towards Nativeness): `state.ans[id]` is the newest answers, with `t[i]` (when question i was checked; an unchanged answer keeps its time) and `ok[i]` (1 / 0 as the page showed it); `newest()` merges `state.ans`, the first try and `state.nb[id].re` by time (answers saved before this have no `t`: one that differs from the first try counts from `state.last[step].date`); ✏️ Correct it (`nbRedo`) also writes into `state.ans` (`setAns`); `latestBack()` writes what the page shows back into `state.ans` each time the step opens. Until a step is opened again, `el-exam.js` checks a saved answer itself (`checkNow`: a copy of the engine's `norm` / `expand` / `bare` / `fits` / `wholeOk`, phone words only in a personal LS; an own sentence waits): **keep it in step when the engines' check changes**. 📊 Dashboard: `state.last[step]` (the latest score, "first → latest" in `tryCell` and the step popup's First try / Now columns) also follows ✏️ Correct it and today's check (`lastFromPage`, counted questions only, an empty one not right).

**✨ Questions added after a step was done** (problem report, 4ESOB, 24 Nov 2026; Roger: "he should see his latest answers"): a step whose questions changed after the student did it (4 ESO LS2 LP3 Vocabulary got 10 *Listen and complete* questions on 14 Nov) shows the saved answers where their questions still are, and the new ones empty, with **no red "Type an answer."** (`quiet(k, …)` after the restore in `stays` and `nbShow`: only questions the saved record lacks, `k.v[i] == null`; a box left empty in a real ✓ Check keeps its line) and the ✨ "I've added new questions…" note **in both looks** (it was laptop look only). In a frozen notebook a counted question still empty stays open (`nbFix`: added to `S.open`) so it can be answered and ✓ Checked there; `setAns` adds a question missing from `state.ans[id].q`, and `newest()` brings `state.ans` / `nb.re` answers for questions the record lacks, so the answer comes back on reload. All three `ls2-engine.js`. Marks never change.
**Study cards are always open (Roger, 4 Nov 2026: "they must be able to do the flashcards always without pressing redo"):** the LS2 cards (`shared/js/el-wordcards.js` since 20 Nov 2026) never lock: after 🎉 the deck starts again, and the cards step stays done. LS1 pages (`shared/js/flashcard-set.js`) are unchanged.

## No translating, no copying on student pages (Roger, 9 Nov 2026)
- Every student page and game (everything except `teacher.html` and `2btx/`) starts with `<html … class="notranslate" translate="no">` and, right after `<meta charset>`, `<meta name="google" content="notranslate"><script src="…/shared/js/el-nocopy.js"></script>`. A NEW page copies these. Chrome then never offers or does "Translate this page".
- `el-nocopy.js`: page text can't be selected, copied, cut, dragged or right-clicked. Answer boxes work as usual (typing, right-click, copy/cut of their own text) but with NO spelling help (Roger, 13 Nov 2026): every box, also ones built later (MutationObserver + focusin), gets `spellcheck="false" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="off" writingsuggestions="false"`, the Grammarly/LanguageTool off switches and `autocomplete="off"` (unless the box already names one, e.g. sign-in `username`); `<html spellcheck="false">` too; password boxes untouched; teacher.html keeps spellcheck; pasting text is only allowed when it is text they copied from their own boxes on that page; image pastes (report screenshot) and in-page drag-and-drop still work. Blocked pastes never reach `el-typing.js`, so they don't count as pasted characters.

## 🔍 Dictionary on learning path steps (Roger, 12 Nov 2026)
- "és possible que els alumnes tinguin una pantalla emergent en els SLP on puguin buscar paraules i traduccions de l'anglès?" `shared/js/el-dict.js`, loaded by `el-app.js` on every learning path page (`mode === "lp"`); it only shows on LS2-engine learning paths (its folder's `ls2-engine.js` lists the page with a number), so LS1, recall checkpoints, the sample exam, the final product and Reflect have none. A new LS built on the engine gets it by itself.
- 🔍 Dictionary button in the top bar on the right (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: the Learning Path dropdown there is hidden), its window and the first-time bubble open under it; on a phone and in a step's full screen view it floats bottom right, stacked above ✅ To do and the account bar (`place()` measures them). 🔍 because 📖 is Books. Typing a word or double-clicking one on the page opens it. It looks in the LS's word list first (every `items` [English, Catalan, example] of every learning path's `data/*.js`, labelled with the first LP that has it), then online for free: Google Translate's `gtx` endpoint (as the announcements) and dictionaryapi.dev for an English meaning. **Back-up sources (Roger, 22 Nov 2026, "calque" showed "I can't reach the online dictionary"; "Calque should be in the dictionary"):** `gtx` answers 429 "automated queries" to some networks, and every look-up then failed. Now the English meaning is asked for at once (dictionaryapi.dev, then Wiktionary's `page/definition` REST endpoint, definitions stripped of HTML with `DOMParser`); when `gtx` fails it rests for 10 minutes on that tab (`sessionStorage.el_dict_gt_rest`) and MyMemory (`api.mymemory.translated.net`, free, no key) translates instead: a word the English dictionary knows is English (plus a "from Catalan" card when MyMemory also reads it as Catalan: mar, sol, gat), otherwise Catalan → English. A word `gtx` reads as French or Spanish but the English dictionary knows (calque, menu) stays English. `getJSON` tells "not found" (404) from "can't reach": "Not found. Check the spelling." whenever any source answered, "I can't reach the online dictionary" only when none did. 🔊 uses the browser voice. Last 8 lookups in `localStorage.el_dict_recent`; nothing goes to Firestore.
- It must not give answers away: words, phrasal verbs and short expressions only (max 4 words online, up to 6 when the expression is on the LS's list; 60 characters); hidden on Recall steps (no notes); a Catalan word whose English is (or contains) an answer of an activity on this page (`a`/`answer` in the page's data) shows "Try it yourself first!" and is not looked up online. `MODE` at the top of the file: `both` (default), `en-ca` (English → Catalan only), `ls` (LS list only).
- Until a student first opens it, it says "Dictionary" and wiggles every few seconds (none with reduced motion); from then on it is only the round 🔍 (`el_dict_used_<uid>`). Students can drag it anywhere (a press that moves 7 px+); the place is kept per student on that browser (`el_dict_pos_<uid>`, centre as a share of the window) and its window opens under it (top half) or above it (bottom half). Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "pots fer que es mogui? i que es faci petit com la lupa el primer cop que el toques o el facis servir?"
- On a phone the window sits at the top of what is visible and never grows past it (Roger, 21 Nov 2026, iPhone: "the dictionary moves all around the page and sometimes I don't even see what I'm typing"): iPhone Safari doesn't shrink the page when the keyboard opens, it slides it, so the old bottom-fixed window went off the top. `fit()` in `el-dict.js` places it from `visualViewport` (`offsetTop` + `height`, on its resize and scroll), the typing box at its top and the results scrolling inside. While its input has focus on a phone, `body.eldict-kb` hides the bars fixed at the bottom (Next step, ✅ To do, 🔍, the cloud bar, the tab bar) so they don't float above the keyboard; Look up closes the keyboard. Computers unchanged (`onPhone()` = `max-width:640px`).
- First time (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "when they enter the first time, either in class mode or when doing the activities, I want a message telling them that they have a dictionary"): a dark bubble over the 🔍 button ("You have a dictionary!", Catalan for Starter/Base) on the first step where the button shows, with Got it / Try it; it comes back on every page until they press one or open the dictionary. Once per student on that browser (`el_dict_seen_<uid>`, kept after a sign-out).

## Copyright on downloads and printouts
- **Everything students can download or print carries the copyright line (Roger, 25 Oct 2026):** "© <year> Roger Beltran · English Lab · All rights reserved". The wording lives only in `shared/js/el-copyright.js` (`window.EL_COPYRIGHT`); `tools/stamp-copyright.py` reads it from there.
- **Printed pages and "Save as PDF":** `el-copyright.js` prints the line at the foot of every page in Chrome/Edge (`@page` margin box, 12 mm bottom margin), with the page number "1 / 3" bottom right (Roger, 12 Nov 2026), and once at the end in other browsers (`.el-copyright`, hidden on screen). `el-cloud.js` imports it, so every page that loads `el-cloud.js` (and every print view built in it, such as dashboard PDFs) gets it; a page without `el-cloud.js` adds `<script src="…/shared/js/el-copyright.js" defer></script>` in its `<head>`. Do not hide `.el-copyright` in a page's print CSS.
- **LS vocabulary and grammar summaries** (one PDF per route in `<ls>/downloads/`, teacher's Word in the LS's Drive `Summaries`): LS1 of both courses is built by `tools/study-summary/` (`dump.js` reads the pages, `build.py` writes the Word, `build-all.sh` rebuilds everything, converts with LibreOffice and stamps); the LS2 summaries were built the same way from the engine's `data/*.js` (see their LS `AGENTS.md`). A new LS gets its summary from its own data with the same layout. **Pro (native) PDFs** (`ls<n>-vocabulary-grammar-native.pdf`, 4 ESO LS1 and LS2, Roger 30 Oct 2026): `tools/study-summary/build-pro.sh` (LS2 from `data/native/*.js` via `dump-pro.js`; LS1 has no Pro pages, so its C2 extension is hand-written in `pro-ls1-4eso.json`), `pro.py` writes the Word. An LS with a Pro PDF has `pro:true` in `EL_LS`; there the Pro student, the LS page, the board and the teacher's Materials tab show it, elsewhere Pro gets the Daring PDF.
- **PDF files in the repo** (for example the LS summaries in `<ls>/downloads/`): after building or rebuilding one, run `python3 tools/stamp-copyright.py <file.pdf>` (bottom left, on the footer line of the LibreOffice A4 layout; `--x`/`--y` move it). A PDF already stamped is left as it is. Word or PDF files given to students outside the site (Classroom, Drive) get the same line in their footer.

## Study guide in a Learning Path
- **Study guide (Roger, 30–31 Oct 2026):** every LS2 Learning Path 1–6 in both courses has a folded card "📚 Study guide" under "My goal", drawn by `shared/js/el-study.js` (called from both `ls2-engine.js` and, in 4 ESO, the inline `getting-started.html`). Content per page in `data/study/<page>.js` as `LP.study` (format at the top of `el-study.js`): `next` checkpoint, what it `covers`, what comes up in the `exam`, `today`'s recall task, and item lists `check`, `listen` (the LP's own audio or video), `read` (texts per route: insecure, safe), `vocab`, `grammar`; items are `[route, question, answer, why]`, cumulative by route like the Learning Paths, `why` as `[Catalan, English]` for items Starter and Base see. 4 ESO adds `native` lists (C2 tasks) for the Pro route. Tips, plan and labels are shared in `el-study.js` (Catalan for Starter and Base).
- The practice is **new items**, never the checkpoint's or the sample exam's. "Practise for Checkpoint N" works like the secure checkpoint (one question at a time, 45 s, no going back, then the answers next to the key; repeatable, nothing sent or saved) and has the same number of questions per route (13 items: 5 for all, +3 Base, +3 Core, +2 Plus; Pro 10). Exam blocks use the exam format and points (four options with the first right, or gaps; listening and reading 2 points, vocabulary and grammar 1 / gaps 2). Never say that checkpoints are assessed. A new LS: add `data/study/<page>.js` and the two script tags (`el-study.js`, the data file).

## Paper worksheets
- **For students who can't use a computer (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** `shared/print/worksheet.html?ls=<course>/<ls>&lp=<page>&route=<route>[&key=1]` prints any LS2-engine Learning Path from its `data/<lp>.js` (same route rules as `ls2-engine.js`): gaps to write in, a/b/c options in a fixed shuffled order, vocabulary table, lines for writing, fields and self-assessment, a speaking script, and the listening transcripts at the end (audio `script` or the video `.vtt`; YouTube: the teacher plays it). `key=1` prints the answers in red. Screen-only bits (buttons, links, ChatGPT, dictation, final product, Reflect) are left out.
- The list of Learning Paths with a worksheet is `shared/print/lp-list.js`: add each new LS's pages there. The teacher's index is `shared/print/index.html`, linked teacher-only (`data-el-teacher`) from each LS2 landing page. The teacher dashboard's **📚 Materials** tab (Roger, 30 Oct 2026) lists per course the class games (`EL_GAMES`, opens the board), these worksheets per route with the keys (`LP_PRINT`) and the study PDFs (`EL_LS[].study`); new entries there appear by themselves. **Teacher only (Roger, 30 Oct 2026):** both pages open only for the teacher's Google account (`shared/print/teacher-gate.js`); anyone else sees a sign-in box.
- **The paper matches the screen (Roger, 31 Oct 2026: "make sure the printable versions match the current learning paths"):** the worksheet follows every rule of `ls2-engine.js` that changes what a route sees: Starter's three options for each typed answer in `LP.starter` paths (the same options in the same order as the screen: `starterChoices` is a copy of the engine's `choices`, so change both together), ★ on the route's key words (`core`) and the review words in vocabulary panels, "My goal" at the start and "How did it go?" at the end (paths with a recall step), the **📚 Study guide** after the last step (`ELStudy.html` laid out for paper, the checkpoint practice as a list, its answer key on its own page; `ELStudy.items`), and the **Pro** route in 4 ESO (`route=native`: `data/native/<lp>.js` replaces panels by id as `usePanels`, Getting started maps `GS_NATIVE`, a `plan` step prints the question, strategy, notes and model plan). The key header shows only the student route names. A new step type or route rule in the engine needs its paper version in `shared/js/lp-worksheet.js` in the same PR.
- **Vocabulary and grammar summaries** (`shared/print/summary.html?ls=<course>/<ls>&route=<route>`, `shared/js/lp-summary.js`, teacher only like the worksheets): built from the same data files, so they always match the Learning Paths: every vocabulary panel (★ key words, review and receptive words) and every typed panel with `explain` except Mixed (How it works + practice with answers and why), Catalan for Starter and Base, Pro from `data/native`. Starter has its own summary from LS2 on (`starter: true` in `EL_LS`; LS1: Starter uses Base). The PDFs students download (`<ls>/downloads/ls<n>-vocabulary-grammar-<route>.pdf`, the LS page card, the student board and the Materials tab) are printed from this page: `node tools/lp-worksheets/build.mjs --summaries` (also run by a full build); commit them. Rebuild them whenever a Learning Path's vocabulary or grammar changes.
- PDFs: `node tools/lp-worksheets/build.mjs OUTDIR --keys <ls2-checkpoint-keys.json>` writes one PDF per Learning Path and route (Pro too in 4 ESO) plus one KEY per Learning Path (every route), and the summaries. The worksheets are not committed: they go to the LS's `Worksheets (paper)` folder in Drive (older ones to its Archive). Rebuild them after any change to a Learning Path.
- Content that a page keeps in its own HTML instead of `data/` is not printed: keep page content in the data file (4 ESO Getting started's speaking task and model moved there for this).

## Correct together (reading corrections with the whole class)
- **Name on screen: 🙌 Work it out together (CA "Ho resolem junts")** (Roger, 19 Nov 2026: "we shouldn't say correct but something like work together"; he picked "🙌 Work it out together"). Every new button, title or student-facing line for this uses that name (the class mode bar says "🙌 Work it out" to fit), never "Correct". The code and this section keep the old name (`shared/correct/`, `el-correct`, `EL_CORRECT…`).
- **Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "how I do the reading corrections for everybody in class, covering the levels"; "don't change any reading that's already done in class or set as homework".** `shared/correct/index.html?ls=<course>/<ls>&lp=<page>&tab=read|log[&g=<group>]` puts a reading on the projector question by question: the answer in words (the options are shuffled on every Chromebook, so never letters), the levels that have the question with their own question number, and the sentence that answers it highlighted in each level's text. Teacher only (teacher-gate.js).
- Two kinds of reading: a list with `r:` tags (the Learning Paths: one text that grows with the level) gives one slide per question, "Everyone" first, then the Base+, Core+ and Plus extras; a list per route (sample exams) gives slide n = question n of every level side by side. A reading log (step with fields) gets a table of model notes per level. Pro (4 ESO, `data/native/`) comes last, only when the group has a Pro student.
- It reads `data/<lp>.js` and never changes it. What the data does not say lives in `shared/correct/notes.js` (`EL_CORRECT`): `ev` overrides for the highlighted sentence (otherwise `correct-core.js` picks the sentence that shares most words with the answer and the question) and `log` model notes. **A new LS with a reading log: write its model notes there; a new reading: check the highlighted sentences** (e.g. a Node dump of `ELCorrect.slidesFor`) and add `ev` entries where the pick is wrong.
- **📖 Read together, then ⏱ Your turn (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "read together instead of everyone having a different text, and then leave time for each one to do their level"):** the Reading tab starts with one level's version of the text on the projector (Base by default, level buttons switch it, kept per course in the browser), a sentence at a time (Space, →, a clicker or a click), while every Chromebook shows that same text and follows the paragraph (`live/{group}.rt` = {u, k: block, n, chat, l: level, h: the shared text's html, at}, written only when the paragraph or level changes, null when the screen closes; `el-cloud.js rtFollow` swaps the reading box's content for `h` on screen only, and puts the page's own back afterwards: nothing saved changes). From ⏱ Your turn on, rt = {u, k: -1, x: 1, l, h}: each student's own level's text is back and `rtExtras` marks green (CSS highlight, no DOM change) the words it has that the shared text did not, so Core and Plus read their extra details before answering (Roger: "if they read different texts…"); a student on the shared level, or below it, sees no marks. On the question slides rt also carries c = {p: the step, qs: [{q, a, alt, h}] the question (one per level on a sample exam), s: the answer shown, w} (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "students see the feedback on their Chromebook with the answers according to their level and see what they did"): `el-cloud.js rtCorrect` outlines the student's own question with that text and shows a floating card (Roger: "floating and they can move it"; dragged by its top bar, place kept for the visit in sessionStorage el_rc_pos): the question, then, once the answer is shown, what they answered (✓ / ✗), their level's right answer and where their text says it (c.w: each level's snippet with the sentence marked, or h per question on a sample exam; the student's route, else the longest marked sentence found in the text on their screen). The card changes with the projector's question and goes when the screen closes or moves on to the reading log; a question their level does not have says "listen". Nothing is saved. Then a countdown (5 / 10 / 15 min, +1) while each student answers their own level's questions on the Chromebook; 🔄 re-reads how many have checked. Then the questions.
- With a group it reads each student's progress for the LS once (about one read per student) for "Right in the class" (latest checked answers, `state.ans`).
- Opened over the class tab (`shared/correct/open.js`, an iframe; the students stay on their tab, nothing is sent): **✅ Correct** on the class mode bar on a Reading / Reading log tab, and **✅ Correct together** under those tabs in 📋 Today.
- **📘 Grammar together (Roger, 12 Nov 2026: "I need the same we did with the reading but with the grammar section. Start with the grammar of the LP3, LS2"):** `tab=grammar`, only on the pages listed in `EL_CORRECT_GRAMMAR` (el-catalog.js; every LS2 learning path with a Grammar tab, both courses, since 13 Nov 2026; a new LS: add its pages there). The same three parts: 📘 How it works together (the step's `explain` boxes, Everyone first, then Base, Core+, Plus, each with its level; **All levels** by default or one level's, kept per course in the browser; a box at a time; every Chromebook's "How it works" card shows the same boxes, Catalan where the box has it for Base and Starter, and follows the yellow one: `live/{group}.rt` = {u, p: "grammar", k, n, l, h, hc, at}), ⏱ Your turn (rt = {u, p, k: -1, x: 1, hs: the text of the boxes seen together}: the student's own boxes are back and those not seen together turn green; a countdown; 🔄 who has checked the grammar), then one slide per sentence: the gaps filled with the answer (`correct-core.js fill`), the levels that have it with their number, the item's rule (`en`/`ca`), "Right in the class" with the most common wrong answer. Pro's own grammar (data/native) comes last when the group has a Pro student. `el-cloud.js rtGram` draws the students' side; nothing saved changes. **📝 Notes (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "during the grammar together I want students to have a note place … It should appear in their notes with the day, the grammar summary and their notes"):** while the boxes are explained together, each student gets 📝 Take your notes under them (`el-cloud.js gtBox`; saved after 2.5 s without typing, at ⏱ Your turn and when the page closes; nothing until they write) in `students/{uid}/notes/gt-<course>-<ls>-<page>` = {ls, course, deck, title, lp: "LP<n> <title>", parts:{gt:{topic, day, sum: the boxes as they saw them, text, at}}, updated} (the class games' notes collection, same rules). 🎯 What's in the exam on the LS page shows it in that learning path's card: the day, 📘 How it works and their notes (`el-notes.js gtCard`, read only; only b/strong/em/i/br/del/s/u tags kept from the saved boxes).
- **📊 Who did it and how (Roger, 13 Nov 2026: "I want to see who's done the activities during the grammar … their % of good answers and who did it, I need to see the statistic right away but also somewhere else to assess my performance"):** on ⏱ Your turn (Grammar and Reading) every student of the group as a chip: ✅ % right on their FIRST check (`state.tries[step]`, what counts; "before" when it was checked on an earlier day), ✏ working (answers typed, not checked: `state.typing`), — not started, plus the class % (each student counts the same).
