Re-read every 30 s only while that slide is on screen (one read per student; the group's list is read once), and once more when the sentences start. 🙈 Hide names for the projector (remembered). From ⏱ Your turn on, each read is saved (only when it changed) in `classResults/{group}_{ls}_{lp}_{tab}_{day}` (teacher-only rule): rows per student + `qs` per sentence from the first check (`state.items`). The dashboard shows them in **📊 Class results** (left menu; `crPage` in teacher.html, latest 80 lessons, read only when opened): per group average + bars of recent lessons, each lesson opens to the students and the sentences hardest first.

## Verification and repository hygiene
- Check affected routes, route switching, interactive feedback and progress. For matching changes, verify duplicate interchangeability, alternatives, round completion and route coverage.
- Check changed JavaScript syntax and run `git diff --check`. Use isolated browser automation when available; do not require macOS App Management or broader system permissions. Report programmatic-only verification honestly when browser automation is unavailable.
- Preserve existing user changes. Commit on the thread's branch and open a PR for every change; merge only on Roger's "merge" (REQUIREMENTS §1 and §11).

## One level per student (Roger, 1 Nov 2026)
A student has ONE level (route) per course, the same in every LS: `elRouteOf` (el-catalog.js) returns the route the teacher set most recently in any LS of the course (`levels/{ls}.changed`, else `.updated`; without times: this LS, then nearest earlier, then later). Setting a route in any LS view (dashboard Route cell, routes box, accepted request) changes it everywhere, for the dashboard and the students. Student-chosen routes (`by:"student"`) count only while the teacher has set none.

## Dashboard reads (Roger, 1 Nov 2026: the free Firestore plan's 50,000 reads/day ran out)
teacher.html keeps the students' work (students, results, levels, progress, checkpoints, roster, points, teams) in the browser (IndexedDB `el-teacher`, `RAW` + `AT` fetch times) and opens from that copy, reading only the light docs (ls, teacherPlan, settings, problems, suggestions, announcements, problemReplies, cpKeys). "🔄 Update <group>" (`load(gid)`) fetches one group, "Update all" (`load("all")`) everything, as does the first open on a browser with no copy. **Opens at once from the copy (Roger, 21 Nov 2026, iPhone: "It took ages to open"):** the light part is kept in the copy too (`el_t_light_v1`, `saveLight` after every fresh light read), so `start()` draws the page from `fromCopy()` (`D.copy`, `tkOk`/`ttOk` off) without waiting, then `freshen()` reads the light part and redraws through `liveDraw` (never while typing or with `#dlg` open). While `D.copy` is on nothing is written from the copy: `progSync`, `annDue`, `cpSync`, `cpHashAll`/`cpHashSync`, `ttHeal`, `optHeal`, ticks (tkOk) all wait; the load bar says "⏳ Checking new messages and settings…". A device with no copy still fetches everything first (~1,600 docs, ~17 MB, mostly `results`), with a note that only the first time is slow; the page asks `navigator.storage.persist()` so Safari keeps the copy. `ptSync` only writes team points for groups fetched in this visit (`FRESH`). After a save, `act()` → `reread()` reads back only the documents the dashboard wrote (the Firestore module is wrapped so every setDoc/updateDoc/deleteDoc/batch write notes its path in TOUCHED); a full `load()` only when that can't be exact (progress/checkpoints, unknown paths, >60 docs). One-click `patch()` writes are already on screen and read nothing back. Never add a full reload after each click again. **Live during class (Roger, 5 Nov 2026: "always when I have a class, refresh the dashboard of that class"):** from 5 min before a class (`EL_TIMETABLE`) until 10 min after it ends, while that group is on screen and the tab is visible, `liveTick` in `teacher.html` asks every 7 minutes (Roger's choice) only for that group's documents that changed (`students/{uid}/progress` and `results` `updated >` the last one seen, `checkpoints` `sent >`). Single-field queries on a student's subcollection: automatic indexes, nothing to deploy. About three reads per student per round plus the changed documents (roughly 800–1,500 per class; one 🔄 Update is ~900). It redraws only when something changed and never while `#dlg` is open or a box is being edited. The student list, levels and team points still need 🔄 Update. Keep any new live feature to changed-docs queries like this. Cloud scripts reading Firestore must check for 429 errors instead of treating them as empty data.

## Pages open on their real version (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "When I open a page, it loads old version for seconds and then it opens the last version")
Who is signed in is known only after the Firebase SDK has loaded and el-cloud.js has read the student's documents (1–3 s on a Chromebook). Pages used to draw a default meanwhile (the old course page, the Sign in card, the student menu for the teacher, Beginners/Insecure/Safe), then the real one. Now:
- el-cloud.js imports the Firebase SDK with `import()` after its early part, never with static imports (they held back the whole module, so `el-app.js` and `el-route-names.js` waited for the SDK). If the SDK cannot load (offline), the module stops quietly; the page works on its own.
- `el_who` (localStorage, `remember` in el-cloud.js): uid, role (teacher / tester / student), group, `ca`, saved at every sign-in, removed on sign-out and for refused accounts; the teacher's student view keeps the teacher's own, and the viewed student's language goes in `el_as.ca` (sessionStorage; el-cloud.js `remember`, the board, `el-ls-home.js`), so the first drawing in the student view is in the student's language, not English then Catalan (7 Nov 2026). `el_ls_<id>`: the last LS state read (status, groups, open). First drawing uses them: `el-app.js` (menu, All Learning Situations), `el-ls-home.js` (`early`), `lab/index.html` (`el_lab_last`), `today.html` and `teacher.html` (Loading text in the right language). The page draws again when the sign-in is known; with the same account it is the same picture. Nothing is opened or saved from them: drawing only.
- `el-app.css`, `el-catalog.js` and `el-app.js` are in the HTML of the course pages, English Lab, Practise and the LS2 vocabulary games; `el-route-names.js` is the first thing in the body of every LS2 page and relabels from the start. A new student page: do the same, and never draw a guessed version that changes after sign-in (no chip, no list rather than a wrong one).
- An async drawing that waits (allLs) looks its box up again after the wait and draws again if a newer drawing came meanwhile (two lists showed on the course page).
- Second round (Roger, 7 Nov 2026, video: "all pages still pop up in between"): the learning paths still loaded `el-app.js` late (injected by the engine, async) and it loaded `el-app.css` itself, and `el-app.js` waited for DOMContentLoaded (= el-cloud.js and its imports), so a page showed its old big header, then the old tabs layout, then the menu without styles, then the real look. Now every LS2 learning path, checkpoint, sample exam, Reflect and final product has `el-app.css` in the head and `el-catalog.js` + `el-app.js` as plain scripts right before el-cloud.js; `el-app.js` draws at once when it is a plain script (no DOMContentLoaded wait). The engines no longer inject it.
- Scrolling to a place (LS home `#about`, `#final`, `#notes`): `ls-home.css` has `html{scroll-behavior:smooth}`, so jump with `scrollTo({top, behavior:"instant"})`, never a plain `scrollTo(0,0)` (it showed the place at the old scroll and slid up). Back on the LS home it returns to where the student was on it (7 Nov 2026).
- The gate: `<html class="elgate">` hides the body until the page's script has drawn its real look and set `html.elready` (`el-app.css`, `ls-home.css`; shown anyway after 1.5 s). Set by `el-app.js` (learning paths, course pages, Practise, vocabulary games), `el-ls-home.js` (LS homes) and English Lab itself (`elhold`: el-app.js leaves it to the page). The browser keeps the previous page on screen meanwhile, so it reads as one step. A new student page: add the class and set `elready` after its first drawing.
- Before the sign-in is known: the bottom-right chip stays empty (no "Not signed in · Sign in"), a student sees only their own level button (`el-app.js` ownLevel; applyLevel shows the right one), `ELCloud.onUser` calls a late listener only once group, level and gate are known (`userDone`; it got a half-known state and the menu went English → Catalan), `index.html` sends a student (el_who) to the board at once, English Lab shows no Sign in card to who was signed in last. `el_who.ca` keeps the language saved before when a page does not read the level (the board wrote null, and the next page drew English first). The course pages load `el-plan.js` + `el-week.js` as plain scripts (the LS the group is doing now is first from the start). The teacher's "Teacher mode · LS" bar shows only for a hidden or closed LS (it pushed every open LS page down a second after it opened).
- The teacher's sections card on a learning path (el-cloud.js teacherSections) is drawn at once by `el-app.js` (tsecEarly) from the copy of its last drawing on that page (`el_tsec_<page path>`, localStorage); el-cloud.js swaps in the real one in the same place. First visit on a browser: it still comes after the reads.
- Still changing after the reads, on purpose: blocks for a learning path not open yet or a checkpoint not done, the board and the dashboard (Loading… until their data is read).

## Maintenance mode (Roger, 7 Nov 2026)
The dashboard's 🛠 Maintenance (side menu; a red bar on every dashboard page while it is on) writes `site/maintenance` = {on, note, since} (`firestore.rules`: anyone reads, the teacher writes). While it is on, `el-cloud.js` (`mtCheck`, `mtShow`) covers every student page with the message of `shared/js/el-maintenance.js` (Catalan for Starter, Base and no level, English otherwise, the teacher's optional note) and the worksheets of the student's own course and level to download; the page saves nothing and opens again by itself when it goes off (a listener on that one document). The teacher, the 👁 student view and the test account's "see everything" get in; the test account in a group's view sees the message with a way back. Reads: one per page, none for 2 minutes after a check that found it off (`el_mt`); `el_mt` also shows the message at once on a student's next page. **Which PDFs (Roger, 7 Nov 2026: "make possible to select the pdf that they can download"):** the dialog has a grid per course, items x levels (Roger, 8 Nov 2026: each level its own choice; `site/maintenance.pdfs` = {course: {route: [item ids]}}, the older {course: [item ids]} = the same list for every level; nothing saved = `DEFAULT`, LP1 + LP2 for every level; a click on an item's name or a level ticks the whole row or column): every LS2 learning path's worksheet and the sample exam (recall checkpoints are left out: their questions stay secure), the vocabulary and grammar summary and the reading story (Pro reads Plus). Each student gets only the items ticked for their own level, in their level's version, opened in a new tab. The worksheets (`PDFS` in el-maintenance.js, never keys: the repo is public) are committed in `<ls>/downloads/worksheets/<lp>-<route>.pdf`: `node tools/lp-worksheets/build.mjs --maintenance` (also run by a full build). Rebuild them when a learning path changes; a new LS: add it to `PDFS` and run it again.

## Student page reads (Roger, 1 Nov 2026, same limit)
🟢 Online / 🕘 Visit history (shared/js/el-visits.js, 13 Nov 2026): every student page writes its own `visits/{day}_{uid}` (no reads): at most one write a minute when pages open, then one every 4 minutes on screen. Keep it that rare. The dashboard listens to today's docs only while 🟢 Online now is open.
Every page loads el-cloud.js, so its reads count once per page per student. el-cloud.js reads the student's own documents once per page and shares them (`once`: `myDoc`, `myLevels`, `lsInfo`; `ELCloud.lsInfo` stays a fresh read for the dashboard). The results list is kept in the browser for 30 minutes (`el_rc_<uid>`, `myResults`); the student's own result saves go into it (`rcSaved`), and the board reads it fresh (`ELCloud.results(true)`). Cards that are usually empty (problem replies, announcements) skip the check for 15 minutes after an empty one (`quiet`). `students/{uid}` (name, last active) is written at most every 5 minutes. The teacher's 🔔 listens live only on the dashboard; elsewhere it uses the copy `el_bell` (10 minutes). A learning path page went from ~67 reads to ~7. New features: use these helpers, never a new `getDocs` of a whole collection per page. `lsRead` (el-cloud.js) copies only the `ls/{id}` fields it names: a new field that `elLpOpen` or another student-side check reads (e.g. `cpShown`, `cpClosed`) must be added there too, or student pages never see it (problem report, 4ESOB, 11 Nov 2026).

## Towards Nativeness: Roger's own C1 → C2 course (Roger, 17 Nov 2026)
Roger is the student here ("I'll be the teacher but the student here"): a hidden LS of 4 ESO, `4eso/towards-nativeness/`, done with the tester account (TESTERS in `el-firebase.js`), with its own notebook. `EL_LS` marks it `personal: true`: `elLsNum`/`elLsName` skip it, and `el-catalog.js` removes personal LSs and their activities from `EL_LS`/`EL_CATALOG` unless `el_who` is teacher or tester, so students never see it and "the latest LS of the course" (`.pop()` in el-cloud.js and lab/index.html) never picks it. Activities `4eso/tn-<page>`, `graded: false`. Keep the LS hidden in the dashboard (never open it to a class).
- **Content comes only from Roger's own English** (his corrections lists and the "Your English: feedback and workbook" doc) and his vocabulary sheet. Only grammar and idiom count: typing slips, capitals and punctuation never do. In / on / at is his weak spot (17 Nov 2026): it has its own 📍 step.
- **Engine:** its own copy of the LS2 engine (`ls2-engine.js`), daring route only (shown as C1), plus: `talk` with `pick: n` (🎲 n random questions from the `partner` pool, kept in `sp.qs`), `talk` with `redo: 'talk'` (🔁 Speak again: the same questions after Roig's correction), `movie` (🎬 short gapped lines of real film scenes, YouTube embed with start/end; short quotes only, never whole scenes; each line has its own ✓ Check or Enter, plus Check all, 22 Nov 2026) `idiom` (✨ Make it idiomatic: Roger rewrites his own sentence, typed or 🎙 said; an exact match with a natural version is right, otherwise he sees it and rates his own version) and `fluency` (⏱ Nation's 4/3/2: one Speaking question told three times with less time each round, recorded on the device only, never uploaded). Mobile first: Roger does most of it on his phone.
- **🔍 Spot the mistake is written (Roger, 21 Nov 2026):** panel type `rewrite` in `ls2-engine.js` (`R.rewrite` → `R.typed` with `whole` items): items `{ s, a: [accepted sentences], ex, note? }`. `wholeOk` compares the whole sentence two ways (`bare`: contractions written out; `flat`: apostrophes dropped, so "dont" passes), allows one slip (`lev1`) in words of 5+ letters unless the typed word is in the original sentence, and never accepts the original. The corrected part alone also passes: a run of the right sentence's words that holds every word that differs from the original (prefix/suffix diff), or, when a word moved, two or more words of the changed part in an order the original never has ("What exactly" for "What did you exactly do?", 22 Nov 2026). On opening the step, `R.rewrite` re-checks the stored first try (`state.items[spotw_<route>]`) and gives credit to answers the current check accepts (never takes any away), updating `state.tries` score. The four LPs use id `spotw`, My latest mistakes `spotw_<batch>` (built from `s.replace(bad, fix)`), so the old choose-the-part tries (`spot`, `spot_<batch>`) stay apart.
- **The answer box shows the whole sentence (Roger, 22 Nov 2026, iPhone: "I can't see my sentence"):** in `ls2-engine.js` every typed answer of `R.typed` (Catalan → English, Grammar, Spot the mistake, Fix it, the mixes) and of 🎬 Movie dictation is a `<textarea rows="1" class="typed el-grow">` (pick boxes stay `<input>`): `growCss()` wires each one (`growWire`: it grows a line at a time, Enter never adds a line and still runs ✓ Check, a pasted line break becomes a space, setting `.value` from code grows it, a `ResizeObserver` grows a box drawn in a hidden tab). At ≤640 px the box takes the whole line and ✓ Check goes under it; computers unchanged. Code that looks for answer boxes uses `.typed`, not `input.typed`. In exact-answer checks (`fits`) `phone()` makes cellphone, cell phone, mobile phone, smartphone and "my mobile" = phone; nothing else is relaxed ("yet" still counts). Students' LS2 engines are separate copies and unchanged.
- **It grows:** each new corrections list becomes a batch FIRST in `TN_MISTAKES` (`data/my-latest-mistakes.js`, newest first, never rename an id): items (spot it, fix it, Catalan → English), `idiom` and `spoken` (✨ panels). The page shows the 3 newest batches; older ones go to 🔁 Review. New preposition slips go at the top of `TN_PREPS`. 🗓 This week's mix draws 12 items from every batch + TN_PREPS per ISO week (seeded; id `week_<year>w<nn>`). The mistakes Roig finds in Roger's 🎙 Speaking answers go into the next batch too (Roger, 17 Nov 2026). **⚡ Instant correction (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "speaking task should be corrected instantly and then I would need to answer a new task out of my mistakes"):** Towards Nativeness uploads every recording with `coach: "c2"`; the school script (v31, `coachC2`) answers at once with `fixes` [{said, natural, why}] and `next` {questions, use}; the Talk tab shows them under the hand-in and 🔁 Speak again asks `next.questions` (kept in `state.speak[talk_route]`). Without them (no key, script not redeployed) it falls back to the same questions and the Wednesday correction. **Writing is corrected by Claude, not Roig** (Roger, 17 Nov 2026: "I only want Gemini, Roig, to correct my speakings"): the hand-in (and the short new task) marks `state.coach[id].sent` / `nextSent` and asks the school script (action `tn_mark`, only Roger's accounts, v32) to start the scheduled task "English Lab: mark and correct"; the task's stored prompt can't be changed from a thread, so `node tools/marking/marks.mjs ready` (and `tn-ready`) prints Roger's pieces first as STEP 0 and says the MODE (only Towards Nativeness unless it is the Wednesday run or ✏️ Correct everything waiting now); in a TN-only run `ready` lists nothing for students and doesn't save run.started, and `fb-ready` prints nothing, `tn-apply <file> --apply` saves `results/{activity}.tnCoach[id]` (`[id_2]` for the new task) = {fix, corrected, well, next, at}, which the page polls (45 s, ↻ Check now). **Only what is really off or sounds non-native** is corrected, never a nicer version of what a native could say (Roger, 17 Nov 2026), in the speaking prompt (coachC2) too. Students (2 ESO and 4 ESO) keep the scheduled Wednesday corrections. **Roger's teacher account works in it as a learner** (Roger, 17 Nov 2026, "My teacher account too"): in a personal LS el-cloud.js `tnMe` skips teacher mode and treats the teacher like a tester (pulls and saves only the `tn…` keys to students/{teacherUid}/progress, firestore.rules lets the teacher write their own progress), el-notes.js opens the teacher's own 📓 notebook, and marks.mjs `tnItems` already reads the teacher's account; the school account still works too.
- **Audio (Kokoro, af_heart):** listenings `tools/towards-nativeness/listenings.js` + `voice.py`; shadowing `shadow-lines.js` + `shadow-eleven.mjs` (ElevenLabs voice Chris, Roger 22 Nov 2026; PAID, run only on his OK; each sentence cached in `tools/towards-nativeness/eleven-cache/` so a rerun never pays twice; files `audio/shadow-<key>-chris.mp3`) or the older free `shadow.py` (Kokoro), which records one sentence at a time and writes `data/shadow.js` (`TN_SHADOW`, with cues and marks, so 🔁 Listen and repeat needs no alignment). Marks = **stressed** syllables, ‿ linking, {{assimilated words|IPA of only the changed sounds}} (shown dotted + /tʃ/, 22 Nov 2026) and ↗ ↘, hand-written per sentence in `tools/towards-nativeness/shadow-marks.json` (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "more complete with stress words, linking"); a sentence missing there gets arrows only and shadow.py lists it. After adding a batch or changing ✨ sentences, add their marks to shadow-marks.json and run shadow.py again. Micro-dictation bits (Roger, 22 Nov 2026: "It doesn't work", the browser voice made no sound on his iPhone): `tools/towards-nativeness/dictation.js` (lists every `chunks` bit and rewrites `data/tts.js`) + `dictation.py` record `audio/tts/<key>.mp3` and `-slow.mp3`, so ▶ Listen and 🐢 Slower play a file. **A new LP with `chunks`: run both before it opens.** Every engine cancels the browser voice only when it is speaking (on iOS a `speak()` straight after `cancel()` can stay silent).

- **🌙 Night mode (Roger, 21 Nov 2026: "Can towards nativeness have dark mode at night?"):** `4eso/towards-nativeness/tn-night.js`, loaded first in the `<head>` of every course page (so nothing is painted light first), sets `html.tn-dark` from 21:00 to 7:00 on the device clock and whenever the device is in dark mode; the 🌙 / ☀️ button (in the phone's top bar `header.elplh` before ⓘ, beside the title on the course home, a round button at the top right of a learning path on a computer; a MutationObserver puts it back whenever el-app.js draws or redraws the bar, however late) switches it by hand (Auto → Dark or Light → Auto, localStorage `tn_theme`). It darkens by inverting the page (`filter: invert(.9) hue-rotate(180deg)` on `html`, which keeps fixed and sticky bars working) and inverts pictures, videos, iframes, canvases and inline `url(` backgrounds back, so every text keeps its daytime contrast and no stylesheet needs dark rules. Re-checked every minute and on return to the tab. Only Towards Nativeness loads it; student pages never do.
- **🌱 Garden (17 Nov 2026):** `4eso/towards-nativeness/tn-garden.js` turns the course into the students' garden (shared/js/el-garden.js): a bed per learning path (LP1–LP4, ✦ My latest mistakes), a plant per tab (bloom = state.done[tab], bud = anything saved under the tab's id, sprout = nothing). Shown on the course home (localStorage progress) and on the dashboard's ✦ page (the tester account's progress); both keep the flowers already seen in `el_garden_tn`, so a new flower chimes once on that device, wherever it is seen first (teacher.html loads `el-sound.js` for it). It loads the course's data files itself (TNGarden.load) and restores window.LP afterwards.
- **Teacher dashboard (✦ Towards Nativeness, 17 Nov 2026):** teacher.html takes the personal LS out of EL_LS / EL_CATALOG right after el-catalog.js (into TN_LS / TN_CAT), so it is not one more 4 ESO LS there; it has its own side-menu entry (and "Mine" in the phone More sheet) opening tnPage: progress per learning path, first-try %, every slip Roig corrected at once (state.speak[..].fix, state.coach[..].fix / r2.fix) sorted by kind (tnKind: prepositions, articles, word order, tenses, word choice), slips said more than once, ✨ sentences marked "I'll learn this one", 4/3/2 rounds, and the LS status with a one-tap Hidden. It reads only Roger's accounts (people().tester) and the course's data files, loaded once.
